TL;DR: 本文探讨了在图形设计中迁移文本样式的词对齐问题，提出三种基于NMT和LLM的新方法，并与传统注意力头方法进行比较，发现注意力头基线方法表现优于单独使用NMT或LLM的方法，与混合NMT+LLM方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球化营销材料设计需要准确翻译文本内容并保留原始文本样式（如字体、颜色、布局等），这依赖于源语言与目标语言之间的高精度词对齐技术。传统方法依赖Giza++和NMT注意力概率，但在商业翻译工具上的应用效果有待探索。

Method: 提出三种基于商业NMT和LLM翻译技术的方法：(1) 带自定义输入输出标签的NMT；(2) 带自定义输入输出标签的LLM；(3) NMT翻译+LLM结合unigram映射的混合方法。使用注意力头方法作为基线进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，注意力头强基线方法在词对齐准确性上优于单独的LLM或NMT方法，并与混合NMT+LLM方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 对于图形设计中的文本样式迁移任务，传统的注意力头方法仍然有效且准确；LLM和NMT虽为新技术，但在词对齐任务上尚未展现出显著优势；混合方法可能是一种有潜力的折中方案。

Abstract: Globalization of graphic designs such as those used in marketing materials and magazines is increasingly important for communication to broad audiences. To accomplish this, the textual content in the graphic designs needs to be accurately translated and have the text styling preserved in order to fit visually into the design. Preserving text styling requires high accuracy word alignment between the original and the translated text. The problem of word alignment between source and translated text is long known. The industry standards for extracting word alignments are defined by Giza++ and attention probabilities from neural machine translation (NMT) models. In this paper, we explore three new methods to tackle the word alignment problem for transferring text styles from the source to the translated text. The proposed methods are developed on top of commercially available NMT and LLM translation technologies. They include: NMT with custom input and output tags for text styling; LLM with custom input and output tags; a hybrid with NMT for translation followed by an LLM with use of unigram mappings. To analyze the performance of these solutions, their alignment results are compared with the results of an attention head approach to gauge their usability in graphic design applications. Interestingly, the attention head strong baseline proves more accurate than the LLM or NMT approach and on par with the hybrid NMT+LLM approach.

</details>


### [34] [SG-UniBuc-NLP at SemEval-2026 Task 6: Multi-Head RoBERTa with Chunking for Long-Context Evasion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26375)
*Gabriel Stefan,Sergiu Nisioi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个用于SemEval-2026 CLARITY任务的系统，对政治采访回应进行3分类清晰度判断和9分类规避策略识别。针对长文本序列超过Transformer 512 token限制的问题，采用重叠滑动窗口分块策略和Max-Pooling聚合方法。在两个子任务上均获得第11名，Macro-F1分别为0.80和0.51。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决政治采访回应中问题规避行为的自动识别问题，帮助公众更好地理解和评估政治人物的回应质量。

Method: 1) 使用重叠滑动窗口分块处理长文本；2) 对分块表征进行逐元素Max-Pooling聚合；3) 共享RoBERTa-large编码器+双任务专用头；4) 多任务联合训练；5) 7折分层交叉验证集成推理。

Result: Subtask 1（3分类）Macro-F1=0.80，Subtask 2（9分类）Macro-F1=0.51，双双获得第11名。

Conclusion: 共享编码器+多任务学习框架有效，滑动窗口策略解决了长文本限制问题，但细粒度规避策略识别（9分类）仍是挑战。

Abstract: We describe our system for SemEval-2026 Task 6 (CLARITY: Unmasking Political Question Evasions), which classifies English political interview responses by coarse-grained clarity (3-way) and fine-grained evasion strategy (9-way). Since responses frequently exceed the 512-token limit of standard Transformer encoders, we apply an overlapping sliding-window chunking strategy with element-wise Max-Pooling aggregation over chunk representations. A shared RoBERTa-large encoder supplies two task-specific heads trained jointly via a multi-task objective, with inference-time ensembling over 7-fold stratified cross-validation. Our system achieves a Macro-F1 of 0.80 on Subtask 1 and 0.51 on Subtask 2, ranking 11th in both subtasks.

</details>


### [35] [When Hidden States Drift: Can KV Caches Rescue Long-Range Speculative Decoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26412)
*Tianyu Liu,Yuhao Shen,Xinyi Hu,Baolin Zhang,Hengxin Zhang,Jun Dai,Jun Zhang,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,MingCheng Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了推测解码中的长距离精度衰减问题，从上下文信息保持角度提出KV-Reuse假设，即允许draft模型复用target模型的KV cache可为长程推测提供更丰富的信号。实验表明KV-Reuse能提升长距离接受率，但受限于浅层drafter查询估计不准确和KV投影梯度信号稀疏两大结构瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码虽能加速LLM推理，但现有基于隐藏状态的draft模型存在严重的长距离衰减问题——推测步数增加时draft精度下降。尽管test-time training (TTT)被提出作为补救措施，但作者观察到TTT训练的drafter仍无法解决长距离衰减问题，这促使研究者重新审视问题的根本原因。

Method: 作者从上下文信息保持角度分析，发现target hidden state本质上是"有偏的上下文压缩"——它根据当前位置的attention query聚合历史token信息，生成针对即时下一个token预测优化的紧凑表示，可能抑制对后续推测步骤重要的信息。而target模型的KV cache则保留完整的token级KV表示。因此提出KV-Reuse假设，允许draft模型复用target KV cache。研究者设计了KVShot诊断框架，对比三种复用范式：hidden-only、KV-only和hybrid。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上的广泛评估表明，KV-Reuse确实改善了长距离接受率，但端到端加速效果仍然有限。分析揭示两个关键结构瓶颈：(1) 浅层drafter难以准确估计target查询；(2) draft侧KV投影接收到的梯度信号稀疏。这些发现表明实现KV感知解码的潜力需要超越TTT，转向block-wise训练范式。

Conclusion: KVShot提供了基础性诊断测试平台和清晰的研究路线图。KV-Reuse虽能改善长程推测精度，但当前训练pipeline下端到端加速效果有限。核心瓶颈在于浅层架构与稀疏梯度的结构性限制，未来需探索block-wise训练范式以充分发挥KV-aware解码的潜力。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference, but SOTA hidden-state-based drafters suffer from long-range decay: draft accuracy degrades as the speculative step increases. Existing work attributes this decay to train-inference mismatch and proposes test-time training (TTT) as a remedy, yet we observe that long-range decay persists even in TTT-trained drafters. We revisit long-range decay from the perspective of context information preservation. In hidden-state reuse, we argue the target hidden state acts as a biased context compression: it aggregates historical token information according to the attention query at the current position, yielding a compact representation optimized for immediate next-token prediction. This compression can suppress information less relevant to the current query but important for later speculative steps. In contrast, the target model's KV cache serves as an explicit context, retaining the complete set of token-wise KV representations. We therefore posit the KV-Reuse Hypothesis: allowing the draft model to reuse the target KV cache can provide richer signals for long-horizon drafting. To test this hypothesis, we introduce KVShot, a diagnostic framework that compares three reuse paradigms: hidden-only, KV-only, and hybrid. Extensive evaluations on Qwen3-8B show that KV-Reuse improves long-range acceptance, although end-to-end speedups remain marginal under current training pipelines. Our analysis identifies two key structural bottlenecks: shallow drafters struggle to estimate target queries accurately, and draft-side KV projections receive sparse gradient signals. These findings suggest that realizing the full potential of KV-aware decoding requires moving beyond TTT toward block-wise training paradigms. By exposing these bottlenecks, KVShot provides a foundational diagnostic testbed and a clear roadmap for designing next-generation inference architectures.

</details>


### [36] [EmoTransCap: Dataset and Pipeline for Emotion Transition-Aware Speech Captioning in Discourses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26417)
*Shuhao Xu,Yifan Hu,Jingjing Wu,Zhihao Du,Zheng Lian,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出情感转换感知语音描述(EmoTransCap)新范式，解决现有语音情感识别仅关注静态单情感的问题。通过自动化流程构建大规模情感转换数据集，引入多任务情感转换识别模型(MTETR)，结合大语言模型生成两种标注版本，实现话语层面的动态情感理解和可控制的情感语音合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感描述系统局限于静态、单一情感表征，忽视话语层面的动态情感转换；缺乏能够捕捉情感过渡的大规模数据集；无法支持具有情感智能的对话智能体。

Method: 1) 设计自动化数据集构建流程；2) 提出多任务情感转换识别模型(MTETR)进行联合情感转换检测和说话人分离；3) 利用LLM生成描述性和指令性两类标注；4) 开发可控的转换感知情感语音合成系统。

Result: 构建首个专注于话语层面情感转换的大规模数据集；实现捕捉情感转换的语音描述；支持时序动态和细粒度情感理解；增强拟人化情感表达能力。

Conclusion: EmoTransCap范式和相应数据集为情感感知和情感表达研究提供了宝贵资源，有望推动情感智能对话系统的发展。

Abstract: Emotion perception and adaptive expression are fundamental capabilities in human-agent interaction. While recent advances in speech emotion captioning (SEC) have improved fine-grained emotional modeling, existing systems remain limited to static, single-emotion characterization within isolated sentences, neglecting dynamic emotional transitions at the discourse level. To address this gap, we propose Emotion Transition-Aware Speech Captioning (EmoTransCap), a paradigm that integrates temporal emotion dynamics with discourse-level speech description. To construct a dataset rich in emotion transitions while enabling scalable expansion, we design an automated pipeline for dataset creation. This is the first large-scale dataset explicitly designed to capture discourse-level emotion transitions. To generate semantically rich descriptions, we incorporate acoustic attributes and temporal cues from discourse-level speech. Our Multi-Task Emotion Transition Recognition (MTETR) model performs joint emotion transition detection and diarization. Leveraging the semantic analysis capabilities of LLMs, we produce two annotation versions: descriptive and instruction-oriented. These data and annotations offer a valuable resource for advancing emotion perception and emotional expressiveness. The dataset enables speech captions that capture emotional transitions, facilitating temporal-dynamic and fine-grained emotion understanding. We also introduce a controllable, transition-aware emotional speech synthesis system at the discourse level, enhancing anthropomorphic emotional expressiveness and supporting emotionally intelligent conversational agents.

</details>


### [37] [Naamah: A Large Scale Synthetic Sanskrit NER Corpus via DBpedia Seeding and LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26456)
*Akhil Rajeev P,Annarao Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Naamah，一个包含102,942个句子的高质量银标准梵语命名实体识别数据集，利用DBpedia实体提取和24B参数混合推理模型生成语法自然且多样化合成训练数据，并基于XLM RoBERTa和IndicBERTv2进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语古典文学数字化受限于标注资源匮乏，现有基于通用大语言模型的数据增强方法存在错误率高、缺乏古典语法推理深度的问题。

Method: 1) 从DBpedia提取实体；2) 使用24B参数混合推理模型生成语法自然且合成多样的训练数据；3) 构建Naamah数据集（102,942句子）；4) 使用XLM RoBERTa和IndicBERTv2进行基准测试。

Result: 研究提供了高质量银标准数据集和模型基准测试结果，验证了混合推理模型在梵语NER数据增强中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功构建了大规模梵语NER数据集，为古典语言数字化提供了重要资源支撑。

Abstract: The digitisation of classical Sanskrit literature is impeded by a scarcity of annotated resources, particularly for Named Entity Recognition. While recent methodologies utilise generic Large Language Models (LLMs) for data augmentation, these approaches remain prone to error and often lack the reasoning depth required for classical grammar. In this work, we introduce Naamah, a high quality silver standard Sanskrit NER dataset comprising 102,942 sentences. We propose a methodology that combines entity extraction from DBpedia with the generative capabilities of a 24B parameter hybrid reasoning model to create grammatically natural and synthetically diverse training data. We utilize this dataset to benchmark two transformer architectures: the massive multilingual XLM RoBERTa and the parameter efficient IndicBERTv2.

</details>


### [38] [Theory-Grounded Evaluation Exposes the Authorship Gap in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26460)
*Yash Ganpat Sawant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明将作者身份验证理论引入LLM风格个性化评估可带来本质性改变：理论支撑的LUAR模型提供了校准基线（人类天花板0.756，跨作者基线0.626），暴露了当前方法均低于基线（0.484-0.508）的"作者身份差距"；而无理论支撑的指标间相关性接近零（|r|<0.07），结论完全取决于指标选择，展示了理论-基准循环的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM风格个性化评估缺乏作者身份科学（authorship science）的理论支撑。现有基准无法提供有意义的绝对参考点，导致评估结论严重依赖指标选择，不同指标可能得出相互矛盾的结论。

Method: 实验评估了四种推理时个性化方法，涉及50位作者和1000次生成。采用三种评估传统：LUAR（训练的作者身份验证模型）、具有解耦特质匹配的LLM评判器、以及经典功能词 stylometrics。三种指标进行成对相关性分析以检验结论的一致性。

Result: 基于理论的标准LUAR提供了人类上限（0.756）和跨作者下限（0.626）的校准基准；所有评估方法的得分均低于下限（0.484-0.508），揭示了传统指标无法捕捉的"作者身份差距"。三种评估指标的成对相关性接近零（|r|<0.07），LLM评判器宣城有明确优胜者，而LUAR显示各方法无显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究表明，不同学科理论支撑的评估指标会产生截然不同的结论：LLM评判器宣城有明确的优胜者，而基于作者验证理论的LUAR则显示各方法之间无显著差异。这揭示了"理论-基准循环"的价值——作者身份理论能够暴露临时基准无法发现的评估缺陷。现有风格个性化方法与真实人类作者之间存在明显差距，但无理论支撑的指标无法揭示这一差距。

Abstract: Stylistic personalization - making LLMs write in a specific individual's style, rather than merely adapting to task preferences - lacks evaluation grounded in authorship science. We show that grounding evaluation in authorship verification theory transforms what benchmarks can measure. Drawing on three measurement traditions - LUAR, a trained authorship verification model; an LLM-as-judge with decoupled trait matching; and classical function-word stylometrics - we evaluate four inference-time personalization methods across 50 authors and 1,000 generations. The theory-grounded metric, LUAR, provides what ad hoc alternatives cannot: calibrated baselines, with a human ceiling of 0.756 and a cross-author floor of 0.626, that give scores absolute meaning. All methods score below this floor, from 0.484 to 0.508, exposing an authorship gap invisible to uncalibrated metrics. The three metrics produce near-zero pairwise correlations, with absolute r less than 0.07, confirming that without theoretical grounding, metric choice determines conclusions: an LLM judge declares a clear winner while LUAR finds no meaningful differentiation. These findings demonstrate the theory-benchmark cycle in action: authorship theory exposes evaluation failures that ad hoc benchmarks miss.

</details>


### [39] [StarDrinks: An English and Korean Test Set for SLU Evaluation in a Drink Ordering Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26500)
*Marcely Zanon Boito,Caroline Brun,Inyoung Kim,Denys Proux,Salah Ait-Mokhtar,Nikolaos Lagos,Jean-Luc Meunier,Ioan Calapodescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StarDrinks addresses the gap between controlled and real-world SLU evaluation by providing a bilingual (English/Korean) dataset for drink ordering that captures authentic speech phenomena, enabling comprehensive robustness testing of speech assistants.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM/speech assistant evaluation relies on controlled scenarios that inadequately represent real-world complexity. Drink ordering presents realistic challenges: diverse named entities, spontaneous speech phenomena (hesitations, self-corrections), and linguistically varied terminology.

Method: StarDrinks dataset construction: collecting speech utterances with transcriptions, annotating slots (drink types, sizes, customizations, brand-specific terminology), supporting three evaluation task formats.

Result: StarDrinks offers a linguistically rich, real-world testbed for evaluating model robustness and generalization across speech-to-slots, transcription-to-slots, and ASR tasks.

Conclusion: StarDrinks provides a multilingual (English/Korean) benchmark supporting three evaluation tracks (ASR, NLU, SLU), enabling assessment of model robustness in realistic drink ordering scenarios with authentic speech phenomena.

Abstract: LLMs and speech assistants are increasingly used for task-oriented interactions, yet their evaluation often relies on controlled scenarios that fail to capture the variability and complexity of real user requests. Drink ordering, for example, involves diverse named entities, drink types, sizes, customizations, and brand-specific terminology, as well as spontaneous speech phenomena such as hesitations and self-corrections. To address this gap, we introduce StarDrinks, a test set in English and Korean containing speech utterances features, transcriptions, and annotated slots. Our dataset supports speech-to-slots SLU, transcription-to-slots NLU, and speech-to-transcription ASR evaluation, providing a realistic benchmark for model robustness and generalization in a linguistically rich, real-world task.

</details>


### [40] [Tree-of-Text: A Tree-based Prompting Framework for Table-to-Text Generation in the Sports Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26501)
*Shang-Hsuan Chiang,Tsan-Tsung Yang,An-Zi Yen,Wen-Chih Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Tree-of-Text是一种用于从结构化表格生成体育赛事报告的树状提示框架，通过内容规划、操作执行和内容生成三阶段流程解决大语言模型的表格理解和幻觉问题，在多个数据集上表现优异且效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的表格到文本方法需要大量标注数据集，而基于大语言模型(LLM)的提示方法由于表格理解能力弱，容易产生幻觉问题，导致生成质量不稳定。

Method: 提出Tree-of-Text树状提示框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 内容规划阶段从输入表格中选择相关操作和参数；(2) 操作执行阶段将大表格分解为可管理的子表；(3) 内容生成阶段将短文本输出合并重写为连贯报告。

Result: 在ShuttleSet+上优于现有方法；在RotoWire-FG的RG和CO指标上领先；在MLB的CS和CO上表现优异；且时间和成本仅为Chain-of-Table的约40%。

Conclusion: Tree-of-Text方法有效且高效，为体育领域基于提示的表格到文本生成提供了有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Generating sports game reports from structured tables is a complex table-to-text task that demands both precise data interpretation and fluent narrative generation. Traditional model-based approaches require large, annotated datasets, while prompt-based methods using large language models (LLMs) often struggle with hallucination due to weak table comprehension. To overcome these challenges, we propose Tree-of-Text, a tree-structured prompting framework that guides LLMs through a three-stage generation process: (1) Content Planning, where relevant operations and arguments are selected from the input tables; (2) Operation Execution, which breaks down large tables into manageable sub-tables; and (3) Content Generation, where short textual outputs are merged and rewritten into a cohesive report. Experiments show that our method outperforms existing methods on ShuttleSet+, leads in RG and CO metrics on RotoWire-FG, and excels in CS and CO on MLB with roughly 40% of the time and cost of Chain-of-Table. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of Tree-of-Text and suggest a promising direction for prompt-based table-to-text generation in the sports domain.

</details>


### [41] [Text-Utilization for Encoder-dominated Speech Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26514)
*Albert Zeyer,Tim Posielek,Ralf Schlüter,Hermann Ney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何利用纯文本数据改进语音识别，比较了多种整合技术，实验表明大编码器配小解码器的架构更为高效，且简单配置往往优于复杂方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何高效利用纯文本数据来改进语音识别，重点关注支持更快识别的编码器主导模型。

Method: 比较了将文本数据整合到语音识别模型的技术，包括模态匹配和动态下采样，以在编码器内达到文本级表示。

Result: 更大的编码器配合更小的解码器可达到或超越使用更大解码器的架构性能。

Conclusion: 简单配置（如随机持续时间模型）往往比复杂替代方案更有效，显著简化了训练流程。所有代码和配方均已公开可用。

Abstract: This paper investigates efficient methods for utilizing text-only data to improve speech recognition, focusing on encoder-dominated models that facilitate faster recognition. We provide a comprehensive comparison of techniques to integrate text-only data, including modality matching and dynamic downsampling to reach text-level representations within the encoder. Our experiments on the LibriSpeech corpus show that a larger encoder with a smaller decoder can equal or surpass the performance of architectures with larger decoders. We demonstrate that simple configurations, such as random duration models, are often more effective than complex alternatives, significantly simplifying the training pipeline. All code and recipes are made publicly available.

</details>


### [42] [TLPO: Token-Level Policy Optimization for Mitigating Language Confusion in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26553)
*Jinho Choo,JunSeung Lee,Jimyeong Kim,Yeeho Song,S. K. Hong,Yeong-Dae Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TLPO，一种在token级别缓解大语言模型语言混淆问题的微调框架，通过定位错误位置、探索替代token并使用定制目标更新策略，实现精细化干预，在保持模型通用能力的同时有效提升多语言一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级微调方法（如DPO、ORPO、GRPO）在整体响应层面操作，容易导致模型通用能力的意外退化，因此需要更细粒度的替代方案。

Method: TLPO框架包含三个核心步骤：(1)识别错误易发位置；(2)探索替代候选token；(3)使用定制目标进行策略更新，在token级别抑制错误输出。

Result: 在多种多语言LLM和多种语言上的实验表明，TLPO在提升语言一致性方面显著优于基线方法，同时保持了下游任务准确率。

Conclusion: TLPO通过选择性局部干预，有效解决了语言混淆问题，且不损害模型通用能力，为多语言LLM的语言一致性优化提供了精细化新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong multilingual capabilities, yet often fail to consistently generate responses in the intended language, exhibiting a phenomenon known as language confusion. Prior mitigation approaches based on sequence-level fine-tuning, such as DPO, ORPO, and GRPO, operate at the level of entire responses and can lead to unintended degradation of general model capabilities, motivating the need for more fine-grained alternatives. To address this, we introduce Token-Level Policy Optimization (TLPO), a fine-tuning framework designed to mitigate language confusion through localized, token-level updates. TLPO identifies error-prone positions, explores alternative candidate tokens, and updates the policy using a tailored objective to suppress error-inducing outputs at a granular level. This selective intervention enables effective mitigation of language confusion without compromising the model's general abilities. Experiments on multiple multilingual LLMs across diverse languages demonstrate that TLPO significantly outperforms baselines in improving language consistency while preserving downstream task accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [Multimodal LLMs are not all you need for Pediatric Speech Language Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26568)
*Darren Fürst,Sebastian Steindl,Ulrich Schäfer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对言语语音障碍(SSD)的层级分类方法，通过级联分类策略和语音表征模型微调，在SLPHelm UltraSuitePlus基准上优于现有LLM方法，旨在辅助临床诊断以应对言语语言病理学家人手不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约5%的儿童受言语语音障碍(SSD)影响，但言语语言病理学家面临严重的人员短缺和工作量过大的问题，需要自动化工具辅助临床诊断

Method: 提出级联分类方法（从二分类到类型分类再到症状分类）；对语音表征模型(SRM)进行微调；采用针对性的数据增强技术来缓解偏差

Result: SRM在所有评估任务中均以较大优势超越基于LLM的最先进方法

Conclusion: 发布模型和代码以促进未来研究；SRM方法在大规模基准测试中证明了其优于LLM方法的临床实用性

Abstract: Speech Sound Disorders (SSD) affect roughly five percent of children, yet speech-language pathologists face severe staffing shortages and unmanageable caseloads. We test a hierarchical approach to SSD classification on the granular multi-task SLPHelmUltraSuitePlus benchmark. We propose a cascading approach from binary classification to type, and symptom classification. By fine-tuning Speech Representation Models (SRM), and using targeted data augmentation we mitigate biases found by previous works, and improve upon all clinical tasks in the benchmark. We also treat Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) with our data augmentation approach. Our results demonstrate that SRM consistently outperform the LLM-based state-of-the-art across all evaluated tasks by a large margin. We publish our models and code to foster future research.

</details>


### [44] [Translating Under Pressure: Domain-Aware LLMs for Crisis Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26597)
*Antonio Castaldo,Maria Carmen Staiano,Johanna Monti,Sheila Castilho,Francesca Chiusaroli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对危机场景中多语言通信数据稀缺的问题，提出了一种领域自适应管道，通过从通用语料库中检索和过滤数据来扩展小规模参考语料库，并利用偏好优化将翻译输出偏向CEFR A2级简化英语，以提升可读性同时保持充足的语义准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机通信中的及时可靠的多语言沟通至关重要，但高质量的危机领域平行语料库极度稀缺，限制了有效翻译解决方案的开发。当无法实现完整的多语言覆盖时，需要一种实用的替代方案。

Method: 1) 开发领域自适应管道，从通用语料库中检索和过滤数据以扩展小规模参考语料库；2) 使用扩展后的数据集微调小型语言模型进行危机领域翻译；3) 应用偏好优化将输出偏向CEFR A2级别英语。

Result: 自动评估和人工评估表明，该方法在保持良好语义充分性的同时，显著提升了可读性。

Conclusion: 简化英语结合领域适应可作为紧急通信的实用通用语，尤其在无法实现完整多语言覆盖的场景下具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Timely and reliable multilingual communication is critical during natural and human-induced disasters, but developing effective solutions for crisis communication is limited by the scarcity of curated parallel data. We propose a domain-adaptive pipeline that expands a small reference corpus, by retrieving and filtering data from general corpora. We use the resulting dataset to fine-tune a small language model for crisis-domain translation and then apply preference optimization to bias outputs toward CEFR A2-level English. Automatic and human evaluation shows that this approach improves readability, while maintaining strong adequacy. Our results indicate that simplified English, combined with domain adaptation, can function as a practical lingua franca for emergency communication when full multilingual coverage is not feasible.

</details>


### [45] [Zero-Shot to Full-Resource: Cross-lingual Transfer Strategies for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26619)
*Jakob Fehle,Nils Constantin Hellwig,Udo Kruschwitz,Christian Wolff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对跨语言方面级情感分析（ABSA）进行了系统性多语言评估，涵盖7种语言和4个子任务，对比了不同Transformer架构在零资源、数据有限和完整资源三种设置下的表现。研究发现微调LLM表现最佳，少样本设置下小模型也具竞争力，并贡献了两个新的德语数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方面级情感分析（ABSA）虽然取得了显著进展，但主要集中于英语，缺乏多语言评估研究。跨语言迁移、代码切换和机器翻译等策略在不同架构中的效果尚不明确，需要系统性的多语言对比研究。

Method: 研究涵盖7种语言（英、德、法、荷、俄、西、捷）和4个ABSA子任务（ACD、ACSA、TASD、ASQP），在三种资源设置下系统比较不同Transformer架构，采用跨语言迁移、代码切换和机器翻译三种策略。

Result: 微调LLM在整体表现上最优，尤其在复杂的生成任务中；少样本设置下模型性能接近微调模型，小型编码器模型也具竞争力；跨语言多语言训练为微调LLM提供最强迁移效果，代码切换则更有利于小型编码器或序列到序列模型。

Conclusion: 不同架构需要不同的多语言策略：大型微调模型受益于跨语言多语言训练，而小型模型更适合代码切换。研究还贡献了两个新的德语数据集（GERestaurant适配版和首个德语ASQP数据集GERest），推动多语言ABSA研究发展。

Abstract: Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) extracts fine-grained opinions toward specific aspects within text but remains largely English-focused despite major advances in transformer-based and instruction-tuned models. This work presents a multilingual evaluation of state-of-the-art ABSA approaches across seven languages (English, German, French, Dutch, Russian, Spanish, and Czech) and four subtasks (ACD, ACSA, TASD, ASQP). We systematically compare different transformer architectures under zero-resource, data-only, and full-resource settings, using cross-lingual transfer, code-switching and machine translation. Fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve the highest overall scores, particularly in complex generative tasks, while few-shot counterparts approach this performance in simpler setups, where smaller encoder models also remain competitive. Cross-lingual training on multiple non-target languages yields the strongest transfer for fine-tuned LLMs, while smaller encoder or seq-to-seq models benefit most from code-switching, highlighting architecture-specific strategies for multilingual ABSA. We further contribute two new German datasets, an adapted GERestaurant and the first German ASQP dataset (GERest), to encourage multilingual ABSA research beyond English.

</details>


### [46] [OCR-Memory: Optical Context Retrieval for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26622)
*Jinze Li,Yang Zhang,Xin Yang,Jiayi Qu,Jinfeng Xu,Shuo Yang,Junhua Ding,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OCR-Memory框架，通过将Agent历史轨迹编码为带视觉标识符的图像，利用视觉模态的高信息密度突破文本上下文窗口限制，实现长程交互任务中的高效经验复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent记忆系统受限于文本上下文预算：存储完整轨迹token成本过高，而摘要化和纯文本检索会导致信息丢失和证据碎片化，无法有效支撑长时域交互任务中的经验复用。

Method: OCR-Memory将历史轨迹渲染为带唯一视觉标识符的图像；检索采用"定位-转录"范式，通过视觉锚点选择相关区域并提取对应原文，避免自由生成以减少幻觉。

Result: 在严格上下文限制的长时域Agent基准测试中，该方法表现出一致性提升，验证了光学编码在保持忠实证据恢复的同时有效扩展了记忆容量。

Conclusion: 视觉模态可作为文本模态的有效补充甚至替代，用于解决LLM Agent的记忆瓶颈问题，为构建更高效的长程交互系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents increasingly operate in long-horizon, interactive settings where success depends on reusing experience accumulated over extended histories. However, existing agent memory systems are fundamentally constrained by text-context budgets: storing or revisiting raw trajectories is prohibitively token-expensive, while summarization and text-only retrieval trade token savings for information loss and fragmented evidence. To address this limitation, we propose Optical Context Retrieval Memory (OCR-Memory), a memory framework that leverages the visual modality as a high-density representation of agent experience, enabling retention of arbitrarily long histories with minimal prompt overhead at retrieval time. Specifically, OCR-Memory renders historical trajectories into images annotated with unique visual identifiers. OCR-Memory retrieves stored experience via a \emph{locate-and-transcribe} paradigm that selects relevant regions through visual anchors and retrieves the corresponding verbatim text, avoiding free-form generation and reducing hallucination. Experiments on long-horizon agent benchmarks show consistent gains under strict context limits, demonstrating that optical encoding increases effective memory capacity while preserving faithful evidence recovery.

</details>


### [47] [SAGE: A Strategy-Aware Graph-Enhanced Generation Framework For Online Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26630)
*Eliya Naomi Aharon,Meytal Grimland,Avi Segal,Loona Ben Dayan,Inbar Shenfeld,Yossi Levi Belz,Kobi Gal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAGE是一个将结构化临床知识与生成式AI相结合的新型框架，通过异构图统一对话动态与心理学基础层，并使用Next Strategy Classifier识别最佳干预策略，Graph-Aware Attention机制将图结构信号投影到软提示中。在策略预测和响应质量方面优于基线模型，旨在作为高风险危机咨询的决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型缺乏心理健康咨询所需的复杂临床推理能力，无法同时整合心理学框架、实时 distress 信号和策略性干预规划，而这些都是确保安全和治疗效果的关键要素。

Method: SAGE框架包含三个核心组件：1）构建统一对话动态与心理学基础层的异构图；2）Next Strategy Classifier用于识别最优治疗干预策略；3）Graph-Aware Attention机制将图结构信号投影到软提示中，引导LLM生成具有临床深度的响应。

Result: 通过自动化指标和专家人工评估验证，SAGE在策略预测和推荐响应质量方面均优于基线模型。

Conclusion: SAGE作为前沿决策支持工具，旨在增强高风险危机咨询中人类专家的能力，提供可操作的干预建议。

Abstract: Effective mental health counseling is a complex, theory-driven process requiring the simultaneous integration of psychological frameworks, real-time distress signals, and strategic intervention planning. This level of clinical reasoning is critical for safety and therapeutic effectiveness but is often missing in general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs). We introduce SAGE (Strategy-Aware Graph-Enhanced), a novel framework designed to bridge the gap between structured clinical knowledge and generative AI. SAGE constructs a heterogeneous graph that unifies conversational dynamics with a psychologically grounded layer, explicitly anchoring interactions in a theory-driven lexicon. Our architecture first employs a Next Strategy Classifier to identify the optimal therapeutic intervention. Subsequently, a Graph-Aware Attention mechanism projects graph-derived structural signals into soft prompts, conditioning the LLM to generate responses that maintain clinical depth. Validated through both automated metrics and expert human evaluation, SAGE outperforms baselines in strategy prediction and recommended response quality. By providing actionable intervention recommendations, SAGE serves as a cutting-edge decision-support tool designed to augment human expertise in high-stakes crisis counseling.

</details>


### [48] [Differentially-Private Text Rewriting reshapes Linguistic Style](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26656)
*Stefan Arnold*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现差分隐私文本重写会系统性破坏文本的语体特征，在保留语义的同时消除交互标记、语境指涉和复杂句式结构，导致文本风格趋于同质化的"中立无风格"模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私文本处理已从词级替换发展到句级重写，但在保护隐私的同时，其对文本语体特征的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 对差分隐私重写进行多维度风格分析，比较自回归模型与双向替代模型在不同隐私预算下的表现。

Result: 1. 隐私代价远超词汇层面变化；2. 重写导致文本交际特征的"功能性突变"；3. 交互标记、语境指涉、复杂从属结构严重流失；4. 两种架构均趋同于"非参与性、非说服性"语体。

Conclusion: 差分隐私文本处理虽保留语义内容，但会系统性地抹除人类话语中的细微语体标记，导致文本风格同质化。这表明当前隐私保护机制存在"风格盲区"。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) for text matured from disjointed word-level substitutions to contiguous sentence-level rewriting by leveraging the generative capacity of language models. While this form of text privatization is best suited for balancing formal privacy guarantees with grammatical coherence, its impact on the register identity of text remains largely unexplored. By conducting a multidimensional stylistic profiling of differentially-private rewriting, we demonstrate that the cost of privacy extends far beyond lexical variation. Specifically, we find that rewriting under privacy constraints induces a systematic functional mutation of the text's communicative signature. This shift is characterized by the severe attrition of interactive markers, contextual references, and complex subordination. By comparing autoregressive paraphrasing against bidirectional substitution across a spectrum of privacy budgets, we observe that both architectures force convergence toward a non-involved and non-persuasive register. This register-blind sanitization effectively preserves semantic content but structurally homogenizes the nuanced stylistic markers that define human-authored discourse.

</details>


### [49] [Swap distance minimization shapes the order of subject, object and verb in languages of the world](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26726)
*Jairo Rios-El-Yazidi,Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了传统语言类型学中以SOV/SVO为主导词序的理论框架，提出交换距离最小化原则可普遍解释各类语言中的词序变化现象，包括非常规词序和缺乏主导词序的语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型主要针对SOV和SVO这两种最常见的词序模式构建，但部分语言不符合这一预期或缺乏主导词序，需要解释这些例外现象。

Method: 对多种语言家族和宏观区域进行跨语言比较分析。

Result: 交换距离最小化原则能够解释语言家族和宏观区域间词序变化的规律。

Conclusion: 语言中词序变化受交换距离最小化原则影响，即使在非SOV/SVO主导或缺乏主导词序的语言中亦然。

Abstract: Languages of the world vary concerning the order of subject, object and verb. The most frequent dominant orders are SOV and SVO, and researchers have tailored models to this fact. However, there are still languages whose dominant order does not conform to these expectations or even lack a dominant order. Here we show that across linguistic families and macroareas, word order variation within languages is shaped by the principle of swap distance minimization even when the dominant order is not SOV/SVO and even when a dominant order is lacking.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate and consistent Emergency Severity Index (ESI) assignment remains a persistent challenge in emergency departments, where highly variable free-text triage documentation contributes to mistriage and workflow inefficiencies. This study evaluates whether open-source small language models (SLMs) can serve as reliable, privacy-preserving decision-support tools for clinical triage. We systematically compared multiple SLMs across diverse prompting pipelines and found that clinical vignettes, concise summaries of triage narratives, yielded the most accurate predictions. The SLM, Qwen2.5-7B, demonstrated the strongest balance of accuracy, stability, and computational efficiency. Through large-scale domain adaptation using expert-curated and silver-standard pediatric triage data, fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B models substantially reduced discordance and clinically significant errors, outperforming all baseline SLMs and advanced proprietary large language models (LLMs, e.g., GPT-4o). These findings highlight the feasibility of institution-specific SLMs for reliable, privacy-preserving ESI decision support and underscore the importance of targeted fine-tuning over more complex inference strategies.

### [51] [Decoupling Knowledge and Task Subspaces for Composable Parametric Retrieval Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26768)
*Weihang Su,Hanwen Zhang,Qingyao Ai,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出正交子空间分解（OSD）方法来解决参数化检索增强生成（PRAG）中文档适配器训练时的任务行为与文档知识纠缠问题。通过先训练任务LoRA捕获可复用任务行为，再在正交子空间中训练文档LoRA编码文档特定知识，从而提高多文档PRAG场景下的组合鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PRAG实现中，文档适配器使用任务监督目标训练，导致文档特定事实与可复用任务行为相互纠缠。当多个适配器在推理时合并，这种重叠的任务行为会累积，可能使合并后的适配器不稳定且偏离目标文档知识。需要一种方法将这两类知识解耦。

Method: OSD方法采用两阶段训练：首先训练Task LoRA捕获可复用的任务解决行为；然后训练文档LoRA时，将其更新限制在与Task LoRA正交的子空间中，使文档LoRA仅编码文档特定知识。这种设置提供了可控的方式来检验正交化任务与文档LoRA更新对多文档PRAG中适配器组合的影响。

Result: 跨多个知识密集型任务和模型规模的实验表明，正交化策略能够改善参数化RAG的组合鲁棒性，尤其在多个文档适配器合并时效果显著。

Conclusion: 正交子空间分解是提升参数化RAG适配器组合质量的有效方法。通过将任务行为与文档知识在参数空间中解耦，可以使多文档适配器合并更加稳定可靠，为构建更高效的多文档RAG系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Parametric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (PRAG) encodes external documents into lightweight parameter modules that can be retrieved and merged at inference time, offering a promising alternative to in-context retrieval augmentation. Despite its potential, many PRAG implementations train document adapters with task-supervised objectives, which may cause each adapter to encode both document-specific facts and reusable task-solving behavior. This entanglement may make adapter composition less reliable: when multiple adapters are merged at inference time, their overlapping task behaviors can accumulate together with document-specific updates, potentially making the merged adapter less stable and less focused on the intended document knowledge. To examine this issue, we explore Orthogonal Subspace Decomposition (OSD), an adapter-training setup that separates reusable task behavior from document-specific knowledge adapters. Concretely, we first train a Task LoRA to capture reusable task behavior, and then train document LoRAs to encode document-specific knowledge in a orthogonal subspace. This setup provides a controlled way to examine how orthogonalizing task and document LoRA updates affects adapter composition in multi-document PRAG. Experiments across multiple knowledge-intensive tasks and model scales suggest that this orthogonalization strategy can improve compositional robustness in parametric RAG, especially when multiple document adapters are merged.

</details>


### [52] [HalluCiteChecker: A Lightweight Toolkit for Hallucinated Citation Detection and Verification in the Era of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26835)
*Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluCiteChecker是一个用于检测和验证科学论文中幻觉引用的工具包。该工具轻量级、可离线运行、使用CPU高效执行，旨在解决AI辅助学术写作带来的虚假引用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手技术在学术写作中的应用导致幻觉引用（不存在对应文献的虚假引用）大量涌现，这不仅损害了论文可信度，还给审稿人和作者增加了额外的验证负担。

Method: 将幻觉引用检测问题形式化为NLP任务，开发轻量级工具包，支持秒级验证，完全离线运行，仅需CPU资源。

Result: 工具包可快速、准确地检测幻觉引用，具备实用性。

Conclusion: HalluCiteChecker有望减轻审稿人工作负担，支持出版前的系统性检查，提升学术出版质量。代码以Apache 2.0许可证开源，可通过GitHub和PyPI获取。

Abstract: We introduce HalluCiteChecker, a toolkit for detecting and verifying hallucinated citations in scientific papers. While AI assistant technologies have transformed the academic writing process, including citation recommendation, they have also led to the emergence of hallucinated citations that do not correspond to any existing work. Such citations not only undermine the credibility of scientific papers but also impose an additional burden on reviewers and authors, who must manually verify their validity during the review process. In this study, we formalize hallucinated citation detection as an NLP task and provide a corresponding toolkit as a practical foundation for addressing this problem. Our package is lightweight and can perform verification in seconds on a standard laptop. It can also be executed entirely offline and runs efficiently using only CPUs. We hope that HalluCiteChecker will help reduce reviewer workload and support organizers by enabling systematic pre-review and publication checks. Our code is released under the Apache 2.0 license on GitHub and is distributed as an installable package via PyPI. A demonstration video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [53] [What Kind of Language is Easy to Language-Model Under Curriculum Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26844)
*Nadine El-Naggar,Tatsuki Kuribayashi,Ted Briscoe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究课程学习(CL)作为语言模型(LM)的发育动机学习场景，如何影响LMs的归纳偏置。研究发现CL对LMs的表观归纳偏置有显著影响，为理解语言类型学倾向的预测条件提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言类型学中存在从罕见到常见的特征配置频谱（如OVS vs SOV词序），核心问题是语言模型的学习偏置是否足以复现这些模式。现研究多聚焦于模型架构等"什么"(what)维度，而本文探索"如何"(how)学习这一被忽视的维度。

Method: 在El-Naggar等人(2025a,b)现有LM类型学研究基础上，引入课程学习(CL)变体作为发育动机学习场景——即从简单句开始而非随机顺序输入，通过对比实验考察CL对LM归纳偏置的影响。

Result: 课程学习(CL)对语言模型的表观归纳偏置产生了实质性影响，说明学习场景本身是塑造语言模型语言表征的重要因素。

Conclusion: 学习场景是影响LM归纳偏置的关键维度，为语言类型学预测研究开辟了新方向，未来可进一步探索更多学习场景变体与类型学特征分布的交互效应。

Abstract: Many of the thousands of attested languages share common configurations of features, creating a spectrum from typologically very rare (e.g., object-verb-subject word order) or impossible languages to very common combinations of features (e.g., subject-object-verb word order). One central question is under what conditions such typological tendencies can be predicted, and specifically whether the learning bias of language models (LMs) is sufficient to reproduce such patterns. In this study, we add one dimensionality to such analysis -- the learning scenario for LMs -- to explore its interaction with the inductive bias of LMs. Specifically, as a first study, we examine the effect of curriculum learning (CL), as a developmentally motivated learning scenario, i.e., starting with simpler sentences rather than randomly-ordered input. We expand existing LM-based exploration (El-Naggar et al., 2025a,b) with a simple CL variant and find that CL substantially impacts the apparent inductive bias of LMs.

</details>


### [54] [MoRFI: Monotonic Sparse Autoencoder Feature Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26866)
*Dimitris Dimakopoulos,Shay B. Cohen,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验发现SFT中新知识引入会导致幻觉，并利用SAEs和MoRFI方法识别出残差流中因果相关于幻觉的潜在方向，证明单一潜在干预即可恢复被破坏的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调(SFT)在新知识上训练时可能加剧幻觉问题，但其内在机制尚不清楚，需要深入研究幻觉产生的因果根源。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器(SAEs)分析各检查点残差流的激活，通过MoRFI方法筛选对目标属性呈单调响应的SAE特征，并进行单一潜在干预实验验证因果相关性。

Result: 逐步引入新知识会增加幻觉，且延长训练会加剧这一效应；暴露于未知事实会破坏模型沿残差流中特定方向检索已存储知识的能力。

Conclusion: MoRFI方法能够跨模型可靠地发现导致幻觉的因果相关潜在方向，单一潜在干预即可恢复知识，这为理解和缓解LLM幻觉问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire most of their factual knowledge during the pre-training stage, through next token prediction. Subsequent stages of post-training often introduce new facts outwith the parametric knowledge, giving rise to hallucinations. While it has been demonstrated that supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on new knowledge may exacerbate the problem, the underlying mechanisms are still poorly understood. We conduct a controlled fine-tuning experiment, focusing on closed-book QA, and find latent directions that causally contribute to hallucinations. Specifically, we fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B, Gemma 2 9B and Mistral 7B v03 on seven distinct single QA datasets, controlling for the percentage of new knowledge and number of training epochs. By measuring performance on the test set, we validate that incrementally introducing new knowledge increases hallucinations, with the effect being more pronounced with prolonged training. We leverage pre-trained sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze residual stream activations across various checkpoints for each model and propose Monotonic Relationship Feature Identification (MoRFI) for capturing causally relevant latents. MoRFI filters SAE features that respond monotonically to controlled fine-tuning data mixtures of a target property. Our findings show that exposure to unknown facts disrupts the model's ability to retrieve stored knowledge along a set of directions in the residual stream. Our pipeline reliably discovers them across distinct models, recovering knowledge through single-latent interventions.

</details>


### [55] [HealthNLP_Retrievers at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Cascaded LLM Pipeline for Grounded Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26880)
*Md Biplob Hosen,Md Alomgeer Hussein,Md Akmol Masud,Omar Faruque,Tera L Reynolds,Lujie Karen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HealthNLP_Retrievers团队为ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务开发的患者健康记录问答系统。该系统采用基于Gemini 2.5 Pro大语言模型的多阶段级联管道，通过四个集成模块处理患者问题并从临床笔记中检索相关证据，最终在多个评估指标上取得竞争性排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者门户虽然提供了电子健康记录的直接访问权限，但患者往往难以理解其中复杂的临床信息，导致信息获取不等于信息利用。

Method: 采用多阶段级联管道架构，集成四个模块：(1)少样本查询重构单元，用于简化冗长患者查询；(2)基于启发式的证据评分器，优先考虑召回率对临床句子排序；(3)扎根式响应生成器，仅基于已识别证据生成专业级答案；(4)高精度多对多对齐框架，将生成答案与支持性临床句子关联。

Result: 系统在各分项track中表现：问题解释排名第1，答案生成排名第5，证据识别排名第7，答案-证据对齐排名第9。

Conclusion: 在大语言模型中集成结构化多阶段管道可提升答案的扎根性、精确性和患者健康信息交流的专业质量。

Abstract: Patient portals now give individuals direct access to their electronic health records (EHRs), yet access alone does not ensure patients understand or act on the complex clinical information contained in these records. The ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task addresses this challenge by focusing on grounded question answering over EHRs, and this paper presents the system developed by the HealthNLP_Retrievers team for this task. The proposed approach uses a multi-stage cascaded pipeline powered by the Gemini 2.5 Pro large language model to interpret patient-authored questions and retrieve relevant evidence from lengthy clinical notes. Our architecture comprises four integrated modules: (1) a few-shot query reformulation unit which summarizes verbose patient queries; (2) a heuristic-based evidence scorer which ranks clinical sentences to prioritize recall; (3) a grounded response generator which synthesizes professional-caliber answers restricted strictly to identified evidence; and (4) a high-precision many-to-many alignment framework which links generated answers to supporting clinical sentences. This cascaded approach achieved competitive results. Across the individual tracks, the system ranked 1st in question interpretation, 5th in answer generation, 7th in evidence identification, and 9th in answer-evidence alignment. These results show that integrating large language models within a structured multi-stage pipeline improves grounding, precision, and the professional quality of patient-oriented health communication. To support reproducibility, our source code is publicly available in our GitHub repository

</details>


### [56] [Select to Think: Unlocking SLM Potential with Local Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26940)
*Wenxuan Ye,Yangyang Zhang,Xueli An,Georg Carle,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SELECT TO THINK (S2T)方法，通过"局部充分性"洞察——LLM偏好的token总在SLM的top-K预测中——将LLM的角色从开放生成转变为从SLM候选中选择，并将选择逻辑蒸馏到SLM中，实现无推理时LLM依赖的自主重排序。实验表明1.5B SLM的top-8可覆盖95%的32B LLM选择，贪婪解码平均提升24.1%，达到8路径自洽性的效果但仅需单轨迹效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）计算效率高，但推理能力弱于大语言模型（LLM）。现有解决方案存在缺陷：调用LLM进行推理导致高延迟和高成本；标准蒸馏受限于SLM容量，难以准确模仿LLM的复杂生成分布。

Method: 1. 发现"局部充分性"现象：即使LLM的首选token不是SLM的首选，该token仍稳定地存在于SLM的top-K预测中；2. 提出S2T框架，将LLM角色从开放生成转变为从SLM候选中选择，简化监督信号为离散候选排序；3. 进一步提出S2T-LOCAL，将选择逻辑蒸馏到SLM中，实现无推理时LLM依赖的自主重排序。

Result: 1.5B SLM的top-8候选以95%的命中率覆盖32B LLM的选择；S2T-LOCAL使贪婪解码在多个基准上平均提升24.1%，且能以单轨迹效率达到8路径自洽性的效果。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM监督从开放生成简化为候选选择，并成功将选择逻辑蒸馏到SLM中，实现了推理能力与效率的统一，为资源受限场景下部署高性能SLM提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) offer computational efficiency for scalable deployment, yet they often fall short of the reasoning power exhibited by their larger counterparts (LLMs). To mitigate this gap, current approaches invoke an LLM to generate tokens at points of reasoning divergence, but these external calls introduce substantial latency and costs. Alternatively, standard distillation is often hindered by the capacity limitation, as SLMs struggle to accurately mimic the LLM's complex generative distribution. We address this dilemma by identifying local sufficiency: at divergence points, the LLM's preferred token consistently resides within the SLM's top-K next-token predictions, even when failing to emerge as the SLM top-1 choice. We therefore propose SELECT TO THINK (S2T), which reframes the LLM's role from open-ended generation to selection among the SLM's proposals, simplifying the supervision signal to discrete candidate rankings. Leveraging this, we introduce S2T-LOCAL, which distills the selection logic into the SLM, empowering it to perform autonomous re-ranking without inference-time LLM dependency. Empirically, we demonstrate that a 1.5B SLM's top-8 candidates capture the 32B LLM's choice with 95% hit rate. Translating this potential into performance, S2T-LOCAL improves greedy decoding by 24.1% on average across benchmarks, effectively matching the efficacy of 8-path self-consistency while operating with single-trajectory efficiency.

</details>


### [57] [Turning the TIDE: Cross-Architecture Distillation for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26951)
*Gongbo Zhang,Wen Wang,Ye Tian,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE框架，首个实现跨架构dLLM蒸馏的系统方案，通过TIDAL、CompDemo、Reverse CALM三个模块解决异构模型间的知识迁移难题，成功将8B/16B教师模型蒸馏为0.6B学生模型，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLMs）虽具备并行解码和双向上下文优势，但最新模型需要数十亿参数才能达到竞争性性能。现有蒸馏方法仅能在单一架构内减少推理步骤，无法处理跨架构知识迁移问题（师生模型在架构、注意力机制、分词器上均存在差异）。

Method: 提出TIDE框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）TIDAL——联合调节训练进度和扩散时间步的蒸馏强度，考虑教师模型的噪声依赖可靠性；（2）CompDemo——通过互补掩码分割丰富教师上下文，改善重掩码下的预测；（3）Reverse CALM——跨分词器目标函数，反转分块级似然匹配，实现有界梯度和双端噪声过滤。使用两个异构流水线将8B dense和16B MoE教师模型蒸馏至0.6B学生模型。

Result: 在8个基准测试中平均超越基线1.53分；代码生成任务提升显著，HumanEval得分达48.78（基线仅32.3）。

Conclusion: TIDE是首个跨架构dLLM蒸馏框架，通过三个模块化组件（TIDAL、CompDemo、Reverse CALM）实现异构师生模型间的知识迁移，验证了从大规模教师模型（8B/16B）向轻量级学生模型（0.6B）蒸馏的可行性，为高效dLLM部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer parallel decoding and bidirectional context, but state-of-the-art dLLMs require billions of parameters for competitive performance. While existing distillation methods for dLLMs reduce inference steps within a single architecture, none address cross-architecture knowledge transfer, in which the teacher and student differ in architecture, attention mechanism, and tokenizer. We present TIDE, the first framework for cross-architecture dLLM distillation, comprising three modular components: (1) TIDAL, which jointly modulates distillation strength across training progress and diffusion timestep to account for the teacher's noise-dependent reliability; (2) CompDemo, which enriches the teacher's context via complementary mask splitting to improve predictions under heavy masking; and (3) Reverse CALM, a cross-tokenizer objective that inverts chunk-level likelihood matching, yielding bounded gradients and dual-end noise filtering. Distilling 8B dense and 16B MoE teachers into a 0.6B student via two heterogeneous pipelines outperforms the baseline by an average of 1.53 points across eight benchmarks, yielding notable gains in code generation, where HumanEval scores reach 48.78 compared to 32.3 for the AR baseline.

</details>

### [58] [Converting an Integer to a Decimal String in Under Two Nanoseconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26019)
*Jaël Champagne Gareau,Daniel Lemire*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: This paper proposes a SIMD-based algorithm for fast binary-to-decimal conversion using multiply-add instructions, eliminating lookup tables while achieving 1.4-2x speedup over state-of-the-art methods through a dual-variant design that adapts to input characteristics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Binary integer to decimal string conversion is a fundamental computing operation, but existing fast methods rely on recursive division and lookup tables, which may have limitations in performance and memory efficiency.

Method: 1) SIMD-based algorithm leveraging integer multiply-add instructions on AMD/Intel processors; 2) Parallel computation of multiple quotients and remainders; 3) Dual-variant design with dynamic selection - branch-heavy variant for homogeneous digit-length distributions, branch-light variant for heterogeneous datasets.

Result: The algorithm runs 1.4-2x faster than the closest competitor and 2-4x faster than std::to_chars across all tested integer sizes and workloads.

Conclusion: The proposed SIMD-based algorithm with dual-variant design achieves significant performance improvements over existing methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of leveraging modern hardware instructions for integer-to-string conversion without relying on lookup tables.

Abstract: Converting binary integers to variable-length decimal strings is a fundamental operation in computing. Conventional fast approaches rely on recursive division and small lookup tables. We propose a SIMD-based algorithm that leverages integer multiply-add instructions available on recent AMD and Intel processors. Our method eliminates lookup tables entirely and computes multiple quotients and remainders in parallel. Additionally, we introduce a dual-variant design with dynamic selection that adapts to input characteristics: a branch-heavy variant optimized for homogeneous digit-length distributions and a branch-light variant for heterogeneous datasets. Our single-core algorithm consistently outperforms all competing methods across the full range of integer sizes, running 1.4-2x faster than the closest competitor and 2-4x faster than the C++ standard library function std::to_chars across tested workloads.

</details>


### [59] [Incremental Strongly Connected Components with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26062)
*Ronald Deng,Samuel McCauley,Aidin Niaparast,Helia Niaparast,Bennett Ptak,Shirel Quintanilla,Shikha Singh,Nathan Vosburg*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文将预测增强算法框架应用于增量强连通分量问题。通过利用边序列的预测信息预计算部分解，实现了接近最优的更新性能，并具有预测误差下平滑降级的理论保证。实验表明理论分析能有效预测实际运行时间改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习预测与算法设计结合，设计超越最坏情况边界的快速数据结构，用于处理边随时间插入的有向图维护问题。

Method: 使用错误预测的边序列进行预计算部分解，通过预测信息加速插入操作。算法性能随预测误差增大而平滑退化。

Result: 在预测良好时达到接近最优的界限，性能随预测误差增大而平滑退化，实验验证理论能预测实际运行时间提升。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将学习预测框架应用于增量强连通分量问题，提出了利用预测序列预计算部分解的策略，实现了接近最优的渐近性能，并提供了预测误差下的平滑退化保证。实验验证了理论预测与实际运行时间的对应关系。

Abstract: Algorithms with predictions is a growing area that aims to leverage machine-learned predictions to design faster beyond-worst-case algorithms. In this paper, we use this framework to design a learned data structure for the incremental strongly connected components (SCC) problem. In this problem, the $n$ vertices of a graph are known a priori and the $m$ directed edges arrive over time. The goal is to efficiently maintain the strongly connected components of the graph after each insert. Our algorithm receives a possibly erroneous prediction of the edge sequence and uses it to precompute partial solutions to support fast inserts. We show that our algorithm achieves nearly optimal bounds with good predictions and its performance smoothly degrades with the prediction error. We also implement our data structure and perform experiments on real datasets. Our empirical results show that the theory is predictive of practical runtime improvements.

</details>


### [60] [Flashback: A Reversible Bilateral Run-Peeling Decomposition of Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26190)
*Thomas Konstantinovsky,Gur Yaari*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Flashback提出一种可逆字符串分解方法，通过从字符串两端交替剥离最大字符游程并配对记录，实现O(n)时间/空间复杂度的编解码。其核心定理（游程配对定理）证明该方法等价于首尾游程配对，产生1+⌊r/2⌋个精确token数。该方法在任意允许的双侧游程剥离方案中达到下界最优，并推导出核函数二元性、回文对称性、有限状态刻画和单点编辑局部性等重要结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses the problem of reversible string decomposition that can efficiently represent strings through run-length information while maintaining O(n) complexity guarantees for both encoding and decoding.

Method: Flashback repeatedly peels maximal leading and trailing character runs from a sentinel-wrapped input, recording each pair as one bilateral token. Both decomposition and reconstruction run in O(n) time and space. The central mechanism is based on a run-pairing theorem: Flashback pairs the first run of the string with the last, the second with the second-to-last, and so on.

Result: The method yields an exact token count of 1+⌊r/2⌋ for a string with r maximal runs. Key structural properties include: (1) the irreducible peeling kernel uses at most two symbols; (2) palindromes correspond to strings with symmetric run-length encoding containing an odd number of runs; (3) the decomposition's image admits explicit finite-state characterization; (4) modifying one run length changes exactly one content token.

Conclusion: Flashback matches a lower bound for any admissible bilateral run-peeling scheme, making it optimal. The method provides a complete characterization of the decomposition's properties including kernel structure, palindrome recognition, finite-state characterization, and local edit behavior.

Abstract: We introduce Flashback, a reversible string decomposition that repeatedly peels the maximal leading and trailing character runs from a sentinel-wrapped input, recording each pair as one bilateral token. Decomposition and reconstruction both run in O(n) time and space. Our central result is a run-pairing theorem: Flashback is equivalent to pairing the first run of the string with the last, the second with the second-to-last, and so on. This gives an exact token count of 1+[r/2] for a string with r maximal runs, and matches a lower bound that holds for any admissible bilateral run-peeling scheme. From the run-pairing theorem the main structural properties follow as corollaries: the irreducible peeling kernel uses at most two symbols; palindromes are precisely the strings whose run-length encoding is symmetric with an odd number of runs; the image of the decomposition admits an explicit finite-state characterisation; and changing one run length rewrites exactly one content token.

</details>


### [61] [Asymptotically Robust Learning-Augmented Algorithms for Preemptive FIFO Buffer Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26349)
*Wen-Han Hsieh,Ya-Chun Liang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于抢先式FIFO缓冲区管理问题的学习增强在线算法，同时实现了1-一致性、η-平滑性和渐近√3-鲁棒性。核心贡献是提出了基于输出的预测误差度量，并证明了算法可泛化至任意β-竞争算法的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线算法面临无法利用未来信息导致的竞争比下界限制，而学习增强算法试图通过预测来改进性能。现有的预测误差度量（如输入基度量）会因容量约束对未传输数据包产生人为误差惩罚，不适合缓冲区管理问题。因此需要设计更合理的预测质量评估方法。

Method: 1) 提出基于输出的预测误差度量，仅评估最终传输数据包序列的预测质量；2) 设计包含动态预测监控和缓冲区清空策略的自适应算法；3) 当预测恶化时切换至最坏情况备用机制。算法通过监控预测质量并执行缓冲区清空策略保证鲁棒性。

Result: 算法同时达到：1) 完美预测下竞争比为1（最优）；2) 预测误差增大时性能平滑退化；3) 任意不准确预测下渐近竞争比为√3（匹配2009年Englert-Westermann的下界）。此外，清空操作产生的竞争损失被约束为附加容量常数，且随问题规模渐近消失。

Conclusion: 本文提出的输出基预测误差度量更准确地反映了预测对缓冲区管理的实际价值。算法框架具有通用性：将备用模块替换为任意β-竞争在线算法可立即获得渐近β-鲁棒性。该工作为学习增强缓冲区管理提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We present a learning-augmented online algorithm for the preemptive FIFO buffer management problem, where packets arrive online to a finite-capacity buffer, must be transmitted in FIFO order, and the algorithm may preemptively discard buffered packets to accommodate future arrivals. Our algorithm simultaneously achieves 1-consistency, η-smoothness, and asymptotic \sqrt{3}-robustness, where ηdenotes the prediction error. Specifically, it attains an optimal competitive ratio of 1 under perfect predictions, degrades smoothly as the prediction error increases, and maintains an asymptotic competitive ratio of \sqrt{3} under arbitrarily inaccurate predictions, matching the best-known worst-case guarantee for the classical online problem, established by Englert and Westermann in 2009 [Algorithmica 53(4): 523-548].
  A key technical contribution of our work is the introduction of an \emph{output-based prediction error metric}. Because capacity constraints dictate that only a strictly bounded subset of arriving packets is ultimately transmitted, our metric assesses prediction quality over the resulting optimal schedules rather than the raw input sequences, avoiding artificial error penalties. To guarantee robustness, our algorithm dynamically monitors predictions and executes a \emph{buffer-clearing strategy} upon transitioning to a worst-case fallback mechanism. We prove that the competitive loss incurred by this clearing operation is bounded by an additive capacity constant that vanishes asymptotically. Finally, we show that our algorithm provides a generalized framework for learning-augmented buffer management: substituting the fallback module with any β-competitive online algorithm immediately yields asymptotic β-robustness.

</details>


### [62] [Path-Reporting Distance Oracles for Vertex-Labeled Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26451)
*Ofer Neiman,Alon Spector*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究顶点标签距离预言机（vertex-label distance oracle），针对带有标签的加权无向图，支持查询(v, λ)返回从顶点v到最近标签为λ的顶点的距离近似值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶点标签距离预言机存在两个主要缺陷：(1) 拉伸因子(stretch)约为TZ01经典结果的两倍；(2) 不支持路径报告(path-reporting)功能。本文旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: 基于Thorup-Zwick和Chechik的工作，提出两个新技术方案：(1) 利用哈希和随机化技术实现路径报告功能；(2) 通过增加查询时间来改善拉伸因子至最优值2k-1。

Result: 取得两项成果：① 提出路径报告型顶点标签距离预言机，stretch为(4k-5)·(1+ε)，查询时间O(k)，空间为O(n^{1+o(1)}·ℓ^{1/k})；② 提出最优拉伸因子的预言机，stretch为2k-1，查询时间O(ℓ^{1/k}·log n)，空间为O(k·n·ℓ^{1/k})。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了路径报告型顶点标签距离预言机，并达到了理论最优的拉伸因子2k-1，填补了该领域的两个重要空白。

Abstract: Let $G=(V,E)$ be a weighted undirected graph, with $n$ vertices. A distance oracle is a data structure that can quickly answer distance queries, with some stretch factor. A seminal work of \cite{TZ01}, given an integer $k\ge 1$, provides such an oracle with stretch $2k-1$, query time $O(k)$, and size $O(k\cdot n^{1+1/k})$. Furthermore, this oracle can also report a path in $G$ corresponding to the returned distance.
  In this paper we focus on vertex-labeled graphs, in which each vertex is given a label from a set $L$ of size $\ell$. A {\em vertex-label distance oracle} answers queries of the form $(v,λ)$, where $v\in V$ and $λ\in L$, by reporting (an approximation to) the distance from $v$ to the closest vertex of label $λ$. Following \cite{HLWY11}, it was shown in \cite{C12} that for any integer $k> 1$, there exists a vertex-label distance oracle with stretch $4k-5$, query time $O(k)$, and size $O(k\cdot n\cdot \ell^{1/k})$.
  This state-of-the-art result suffers from two main drawbacks: The stretch is roughly a factor of 2 larger than in \cite{TZ01}, and it is not path-reporting. We address these concerns in this work, and provide the following results:
  First, we devise a {\em path-reporting} vertex-label distance oracle, at the cost of a slight increase in stretch and size. For any constant $0<ε<1$, our oracle has stretch $(4k-5)\cdot(1+ε)$, query time $O(k)$, and size $O(n^{1+o(1)}\cdot \ell^{1/k})$.
  Second, we show how to improve the stretch to the optimal $2k-1$, at the cost of mildly increasing the query time. Specifically, we devise a vertex-label distance oracle with stretch $2k-1$, query time $O(\ell^{1/k}\cdot\log n)$, and size $O(k\cdot n\cdot \ell^{1/k})$. \end{itemize}

</details>


### [63] [Small Independent Sets versus Small Separator in Geometric Intersection Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26533)
*Malory Marin,Rémi Watrigant*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: This paper identifies problems that do not fit the classical square-root phenomenon but still admit subexponential algorithms on intersection graphs of similarly sized fat objects in ℝ^d. By introducing the weak square-root phenomenon (2^{O~(n^{1-1/(d+1)})}) and a new structural parameter (α-modulator number), the authors provide algorithmic and lower bound frameworks, demonstrating their approach on 2-Subcoloring and Two Sets Cut-Uncut as concrete examples.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While many NP-hard problems exhibit the square-root phenomenon (solvable in 2^{O(√n)} on geometric graphs), certain problems do not fit this category yet still admit subexponential algorithms. This paper investigates and characterizes such problems, extending previous work of de Berg et al. (2018) which achieved 2^{O(n^{1-1/d})} for fat objects in ℝ^d.

Method: The paper introduces the weak square-root phenomenon (2^{O~(n^{1-1/(d+1)})}) with matching ETH lower bounds. Key contributions include: (1) A new win-win structural theorem guaranteeing sublinear separators whose removal leaves components with sublinear independence number; (2) A new graph parameter (α-modulator number) generalizing both independence number and vertex cover number; (3) An algorithmic framework and corresponding lower bound framework for this problem class.

Result: 2-Subcoloring and Two Sets Cut-Uncut are shown to exhibit the weak square-root phenomenon, admitting algorithms of 2^{O~(n^{1-1/(d+1)})} with matching lower bounds under ETH.

Conclusion: This work extends our understanding of parameterized complexity on geometric intersection graphs by identifying a new class of problems (weak square-root phenomenon) and providing both algorithmic tools and matching lower bounds. The α-modulator number serves as a valuable parameter unifying and generalizing existing graph measures.

Abstract: While most classical NP-hard graph problems cannot be solved in time $2^{o(n)}$ on general graphs under the Exponential Time Hypothesis (ETH), many exhibit the square-root phenomenon and admit optimal algorithms running in time $2^{O(\sqrt{n})}$ on certain geometric intersection graphs, such as planar graphs or unit disk graphs. In 2018, de Berg et al. developed a general algorithmic framework for such problems on intersection graphs of similarly sized fat objects in $\mathbb{R}^d$, achieving running times of the form $2^{O(n^{1-1/d})}$, along with matching lower bounds under ETH. In this paper, we identify problems that do not exhibit the square-root phenomenon, yet still admit subexponential algorithms on intersection graphs of similarly sized fat objects in $\mathbb{R}^d$, for every fixed dimension $d \geqslant 2$. We introduce the notion of a weak square-root phenomenon: problems that can be solved in time $2^{\tilde{O}(n^{1-1/(d+1)})}$, and for which matching lower bounds hold under ETH. We develop both an algorithmic framework and a corresponding lower bound framework. As concrete examples, we show that the problems 2-Subcoloring and Two Sets Cut-Uncut exhibit this behavior. Our algorithms rely on a new win-win structural theorem, which can be informally stated as follows: every such graph admits a sublinear separator whose removal leaves connected components with sublinear independence number. To facilitate the design of these algorithms, we introduce a new graph parameter, the $α$-modulator number, which generalizes both the independence number and the vertex cover number.

</details>


### [64] [On (In)approximability of MaxMin Independent Set Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26714)
*Hung P. Hoang,Naoto Ohsaka,Rin Saito,Yuma Tamura*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究MaxMin独立集重配置问题的可近似性，给出一般图的多项式时间n/log n近似算法，以及对结构化图类的FPT-近似方案，同时扩展了不可近似性结果的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅知道一般图上存在PSPACE难解性下界(n^Ω(1))和NP难解性下界(n^(1-ε))，但缺乏中间的近似算法结果。该研究旨在系统探索该问题的可近似性边界。

Method: 针对不同图类采用分层策略：一般图使用直接近似算法；对于结构化图类（退化图、树宽受限、H-minor-free）设计FPT-approximation scheme；不可近似性扩展通过归约技术将一般图结果转移到受限图类。

Result: （1）一般图：给出多项式时间(n/log n)-因子近似算法；（2）退化图：多项式时间近似算法；（3）有界树宽图/H-minor-free图：FPT-近似方案（参数为近似因子）；（4）有界度图、带宽带图(n^(1/2+Θ(1)))、二部图：扩展不可近似性结果。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了多项近似算法和不可近似性结果：(1) 对一般图给出多项式时间 n/log n 近似算法，填补了PSPACE难解性上界与NP难解性下界之间的空白；(2) 对退化图、有界树宽图和H-minor-free图给出FPT-近似方案；(3) 将不可近似性结果扩展到有界度图、带宽带图和二部图。

Abstract: In the Independent Set Reconfiguration problem under the Token Addition/Removal rule, given a graph $G$ and two independent sets $I$ and $J$ of $G$, we want to transform $I$ into $J$ by adding and removing vertices, such that all the sets throughout the process are independent sets. Its approximate version called MaxMin Independent Set Reconfiguration aims to maximise the minimum size of the independent sets in the process above. We study the (in)approximability of this problem for general graphs as well as restricted graph classes. Firstly, on general graphs, we obtain a polynomial-time $(n / \log n)$-factor approximation algorithm, complementing the $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-hardness of $n^{Ω(1)}$-factor approximation due to Hirahara and Ohsaka [STOC 2024, ICALP 2024] and the $\mathsf{NP}$-hardness of $n^{1-\varepsilon}$-factor approximation due to Ito, Demaine, Harvey, Papadimitriou, Sideri, Uehara, and Uno [TCS 2011]. Secondly, we present a polynomial-time approximation algorithm for degenerate graphs as well as $\mathsf{FPT}$-approximation schemes for bounded-treewidth graphs and $H$-minor-free graphs. Lastly, we extend the above inapproximability results to bounded-degree graphs, graphs of bandwidth $n^{\frac{1}{2}+Θ(1)}$, and bipartite graphs.

</details>


### [65] [Weighted Emulators with Local Heaviest Edges Stretch for Undirected Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26831)
*Liam Roditty,Ariel Sapir*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个广义 emulator 构造家族，在 $\tilde{O}(n^{1+1/k})$ 边数下实现特定的加法/乘法 stretch，推广了 Elkin 等人关于加权图的已有结果，并在一特定距离范围内改进了 Thorup-Zwick 的经典 additive emulator。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的距离 oracle 和 emulator 研究中，如何在边数与近似精度之间取得最优 trade-off 是核心问题。现有加权图上的构造较为有限，本文旨在提供更一般的统一框架。

Method: 构造性地给出一个参数化的 emulator 家族，通过对 $k$ 的奇偶性分析，分别得到 $(k-1, k\cdot W_1 + (k-4)\cdot W_2)$（$k$ 偶）和 $(k-2, (k+1)\cdot W_1 + (k-3)\cdot W_2)$（$k$ 奇）两种 stretch 形式。

Result: 在 $\tilde{O}(n^{1+1/k})$ 边数下，引入了一个统一的 emulator 构造框架，统一了加权图的已有结果，并在 $\delta \leq O(3^{k^2})$ 的距离范围内改进了 Thorup-Zwick 的 $+\tilde{O}(\delta^{1-1/k})$-emulator 的 stretch 表现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个更通用的加权图 emulator 构造范式，并在特定距离区间内取得 stretch 改进，是 Thorup-Zwick 经典框架的重要推广。

Abstract: We introduce a generalized family of $\left( 2\cdot \left\lfloor \frac{k}{2} \right\rfloor-1, 2\cdot \left\lceil \frac{k}{2} \right\rceil \cdot W_{1} +\max\left\{0,2\cdot\left(\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil-2\right)\right\}\cdot W_{2} \right)$-emulators with $\tilde O \left(n^{1+\frac{1}{k}}\right)$ edges, for any $k\in\mathbb{N}$, where $W_{i}$ is the $i$th heaviest edge on a shortest path between two vertices. Our construction generalizes the $+2W_{1}$-spanner of size $\tilde O\left(n^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)$ and the $+4W_{1}$-emulator of size $\tilde O \left(n^{\frac{4}{3}}\right)$, both by Elkin, Gitlitz and Neiman [DISC'21 and DICO'23].
  When $k$ is even, these are $\left(k-1,k\cdot W_{1} + \left(k-4\right)\cdot W_{2}\right)$-emulators and when $k$ is odd, these are $\left(k-2,\left(k+1\right)\cdot W_{1} + \left(k-3\right) \cdot W_{2}\right)$-emulators. Our framework not only expands known constructions for weighted graphs but also yields an improved stretch over state of the art emulators and spanners for unweighted graphs within a specific distance regime. In particular, for all vertex pairs separated by a distance of $δ\leq O\left(3^{k^{2}}\right)$, our construction improves upon the seminal additive $+\tilde O\left(δ^{1-\frac{1}{k}}\right)$-emulator of size $\tilde O\left(n^{1+\frac{1}{2^{k+1}-1}}\right)$ by Thorup and Zwick [SODA'06].

</details>


### [66] [Solving Positive Linear Programs with Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26838)
*Alina Ene,Huy Le Nguyen,Ta Duy Nguyen,Adrian Vladu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: The paper develops differentially private approximation algorithms for positive linear programs (packing, covering, and mixed formulations) in a constraint-private setting where neighboring instances differ by one constraint. By using a dense multiplicative weights update method analyzed through a regularized dual viewpoint, they achieve improved instance-dependent guarantees and dimension-only dependent bounds, with controlled constraint violation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In the high-sensitivity, constraint-private regime (Hsu-Roth-Roughgarden-Ullman, ICALP 2014), standard differential privacy approaches fail because neighboring instances can differ by an arbitrary single constraint, making it impossible to approximately satisfy every constraint. There was a need for improved instance-dependent guarantees and dimension-independent bounds.

Method: The authors employ a dense multiplicative weights update method developed from a regularized dual viewpoint, carefully exploiting structural properties specific to positive linear programs. This technique differs from prior approaches by focusing on the dual structure rather than primal methods.

Result: The proposed algorithms return approximately optimal solutions while violating only a controlled, bounded number of constraints. The work improves upon prior instance-dependent guarantees and introduces new data-independent bounds that depend solely on problem dimension.

Conclusion: The dense multiplicative weights update method, when analyzed from a regularized dual viewpoint and exploiting the specific structure of positive LPs, proves effective for achieving differential privacy in this constrained-violation setting.

Abstract: We study differentially private approximation algorithms for positive linear programs (LPs with nonnegative coefficients and variables), focusing on the fundamental families of packing, covering, and mixed packing-covering formulations. We focus on the high-sensitivity, constraint-private regime of Hsu-Roth-Roughgarden-Ullman (ICALP 2014), where neighboring instances may differ by an arbitrary single constraint, so one cannot hope to approximately satisfy every constraint under privacy. We give private solvers that return approximate solutions while violating only a controlled number of constraints. Our algorithms improve the prior instance-dependent guarantees, and also yield new data-independent bounds that depend only on the dimension. Our techniques involve a dense multiplicative weights update method developed from a regularized dual viewpoint, which we analyze in a way that exploits structure specific to positive LPs.

</details>

### [67] [Preserving Disagreement: Architectural Heterogeneity and Coherence Validation in Multi-Agent Policy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26561)
*Ariel Sela*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究多智能体 deliberation 系统中的"人工共识"问题，提出 AI Council 框架，通过架构异质性（不同小模型分配给不同价值视角）显著减少选择集中度，但发现一致性验证存在保真度-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 多智能体 deliberation 系统存在"人工共识"问题：评估智能体无论分配何种价值视角，最终都收敛到同一选项，无法真实模拟政策辩论中的价值多元性。

Method: 提出三阶段 deliberation 框架 AI Council，在两个政策场景（儿童福利、住房）进行 120 次 deliberation 测试两种干预措施：架构异质性（为每个价值视角分配不同 7-9B 参数模型）和一致性验证（使用前沿模型评估推理是否基于指定价值观）。

Result: 架构异质性显著降低首选集中度（儿童福利：70.9%→46.1%，住房：46.0%→22.9%，p<0.001）。一致性验证呈现保真度-多样性权衡：在有主导选项场景中进一步降低集中度（46.1%→40.8%），但在选项竞争场景中反而增加集中度（22.9%→26.6%）。

Conclusion: 架构异质性可有效缓解无客观正确答案场景下的人工共识；一致性验证的质量加权机制存在固有权衡；8B 模型对反论点呈二元响应而非梯度响应；"可信张力率"可作为小模型 deliberation 能力的诊断指标。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation systems using large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for policy simulation, yet they suffer from artificial consensus: evaluator agents converge on the same option regardless of their assigned value perspectives. We present the AI Council, a three-phase deliberation framework, and conduct 120 deliberations across two policy scenarios to test two interventions. First, architectural heterogeneity (assigning a different 7-9B parameter model to each value perspective) significantly reduces first-choice concentration compared to a homogeneous baseline (child welfare: 70.9% to 46.1%, p < 0.001, r = 0.58; housing: 46.0% to 22.9%, p < 0.001, r = 0.50). This contrasts with accuracy-oriented multi-agent debate, where heterogeneity does not reduce convergence, suggesting model diversity operates differently when no objectively correct answer exists. Second, coherence validation (using a frontier model to assess whether each evaluator's reasoning is grounded in its assigned values) reveals a fidelity-diversity tradeoff: on a scenario with a dominant option, it further reduces concentration (46.1% to 40.8%, p = 0.004), but on a scenario with genuinely competitive options, it increases concentration (22.9% to 26.6%, p = 0.96) by amplifying high-coherence evaluators who cluster on one option. This tradeoff may be a general property of multi-agent systems employing quality weighting. We report negative results from three failed Delphi designs, demonstrate that 8B models exhibit binary rather than graded responses to counter-arguments, and propose the trustworthy tension rate as a diagnostic measure of small-model deliberation capabilities.

</details>

### [68] [A Multimodal and Explainable Machine Learning Approach to Diagnosing Multi-Class Ejection Fraction from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25942)
*Catherine Ning,Yu Ma,Cindy Beini Wang,Sean McMahon,Joseph Radojevic,Steven Zweibel,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种结合12导联ECG特征与EHR数据的多模态XGBoost模型，可将LVEF分为四个临床等级，在时序验证中表现稳定（AUROC 0.82-0.95），有望成为资源有限地区LVEF筛查和分诊的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 左室射血分数（LVEF）评估依赖超声心动图，这限制了其在基层医疗和资源受限地区的可及性，需要一种更实用的筛查工具。

Method: 开发了一个多模态机器学习框架，结合工程化的12导联ECG时间序列特征与结构化EHR变量，使用XGBoost模型对LVEF进行四分类；采用SHAP归因分析实现模型可解释性。数据来自Hartford HealthCare的回顾性数据，训练集包含36,784对ECG-超声心动图配对（来自30,952名门诊患者），时序验证集包含19,966份ECG。

Result: 多模态模型的一对多AUROC达到：重度降低0.95、中度降低0.92、轻度降低0.82、正常0.91，优于仅ECG和仅EHR的基线模型，并在时序验证中保持性能。

Conclusion: 该研究支持将基于ECG的多模态LVEF分层作为实用的筛查和分诊工具，可在资源有限的情况下优先进行确认性影像检查。

Abstract: Left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) assessment depends on echocardiography, limiting access in primary care and resource-constrained settings. We developed a multimodal machine-learning framework that combines engineered 12-lead ECG timeseries features with structured EHR variables to classify LVEF into four clinically used strata: normal (>50%), mildly reduced (40-50%), moderately reduced (30-40%), and severely reduced (<30%). To support model explainability, we identified the most influential ECG and EHR features via SHAP attributions. Using retrospective data from Hartford HealthCare, we trained XGBoost models on 36,784 ECG-echocardiogram pairs from 30,952 outpatients and evaluated temporal generalizability on 19,966 ECGs from a subsequent period. The multimodal model achieved one-vs-rest AUROCs of 0.95 (severe), 0.92 (moderate), 0.82 (mild), and 0.91 (normal), outperforming ECG-only and EHR-only baselines, and maintained performance under temporal validation. This work supports ECG-based, multimodal LVEF stratification as a practical screening and triage aid to prioritize confirmatory imaging where resources are limited.

</details>


### [69] [A Survey of Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning with Graph Neural Network-Based Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25972)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Laetitia Matignon,Maxime Morge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper surveys GNN-based communication methods in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), identifying a gap in existing frameworks for classification. The authors propose a generalized communication framework to clarify the underlying concepts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: There is a lack of explicit structure and framework to distinguish and classify MARL approaches with communication based on GNNs, despite growing research in this area.

Method: Survey-based classification; proposes a generalized GNN-based communication process framework to distinguish and categorize MARL approaches with communication.

Result: A generalized GNN-based communication process framework is proposed to make the underlying concepts behind the methods more obvious and accessible.

Conclusion: The paper provides a generalized framework for GNN-based communication in MARL, making underlying concepts more accessible for researchers.

Abstract: In multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), the integration of a communication mechanism, allowing agents to better learn to coordinate their actions and converge on their objectives by sharing information. Based on an interaction graph, a subclass of methods employs graph neural networks (GNNs) to learn the communication, enabling agents to improve their internal representations by enriching them with information exchanged. With growing research, we note a lack of explicit structure and framework to distinguish and classify MARL approaches with communication based on GNNs. Thus, this paper surveys recent works in this field. We propose a generalized GNN-based communication process with the goal of making the underlying concepts behind the methods more obvious and accessible.

</details>

*Jiaming Yang,Chenwei Tang,Liangli Zhen,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable
