TL;DR: 本文提出AFU-IC框架，通过异步机制和服务器端不变性校准解决联邦遗忘中的同步延迟和数据残留问题，在医学影像场景下实现高效、合规、可靠的数据遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法依赖同步协调，需要整个联邦系统等待滞后设备完成擦除，造成显著延迟；同时被擦除数据的影响只是暂时抑制而非真正消除，在后续训练中可能重新浮现。

Method: 提出AFU-IC框架，通过解耦擦除过程与全局训练流程实现异步联邦遗忘，并在服务器端引入不变性校准机制防止模型重新学习被擦除的数据。

Result: 在三个医学基准数据集上的实验表明，AFU-IC在遗忘效果和模型保真度上与金标准重训练方法相当，同时显著降低了墙钟时间延迟。

Conclusion: AFU-IC为跨孤岛医学环境中的联邦学习提供了高效、合规、可靠的数据遗忘解决方案，有效平衡了数据删除效果与系统效率之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FU) is an emerging paradigm in Federated Learning (FL) that enables participating clients to fully remove their contributions from a trained global model, driven by data protection regulations that mandate the right to be forgotten. However, existing FU methods mostly rely on synchronous coordination. This requirement forces the entire federation to halt and wait for stragglers to complete erasure, creating significant delays due to device heterogeneity. Furthermore, these methods often face the problem that the influence of erased data is merely suppressed temporarily and resurfaces during subsequent training, rather than being genuinely removed. To overcome these limitations, this paper proposes Asynchronous Federated Unlearning with Invariance Calibration (AFU-IC), a novel framework for medical imaging that decouples the erasure process from the global training workflow. This enables the target client to perform unlearning asynchronously without interrupting global training. Meanwhile, a server-side invariance calibration mechanism prevents the model from relearning the erased data. Extensive experiments on three medical benchmarks demonstrate that AFU-IC achieves unlearning efficacy and model fidelity comparable to gold-standard retraining while significantly reducing wall-clock latency compared to synchronous baselines. AFU-IC ensures efficient, compliant and reliable FL in cross-silo medical environments.

</details>


### [108] [Semi-supervised learning with max-margin graph cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26818)
*Branislav Kveton,Michal Valko,Ali Rahimi,Ling Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel algorithm for semisupervised learning. This algorithm learns graph cuts that maximize the margin with respect to the labels induced by the harmonic function solution. We motivate the approach, compare it to existing work, and prove a bound on its generalization error. The quality of our solutions is evaluated on a synthetic problem and three UCI ML repository datasets. In most cases, we outperform manifold regularization of support vector machines, which is a state-of-the-art approach to semi-supervised max-margin learning.

</details>


### [109] [Unifying Sparse Attention with Hierarchical Memory for Scalable Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26837)
*Zihan Zhao,Baotong Lu,Shengjie Lin,Yizou Chen,Jing Liu,Yanqi Zhang,Ziming Miao,Ming-Chang Yang,Haiying Shen,Qi Chen,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIN是一个稀疏注意力感知的推理框架，通过统一分区抽象、局部感知缓存管理和两级元数据布局，共同设计执行流水线与分层KV存储，解决动态稀疏注意力在系统层面的收益转化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态稀疏注意力可减少解码步骤中访问的KV状态，但算法层面的优化难以转化为系统级收益；同时分层KV存储引入新瓶颈——跨GPU-CPU边界的细粒度、不规则KV检索会抵消稀疏性带来的收益。

Method: 提出SPIN框架，通过三大技术实现协同设计：(1)统一分区抽象，将不同稀疏粒度映射到共享的页式KV基板上；(2)局部感知KV缓存管理器，动态调整请求级HBM预算并采用GPU友好的桶式LRU策略以减少PCIe往返；(3)两级层次化元数据布局，按活跃工作集而非最坏情况地址空间进行优化。

Result: 基于vLLM实现，集成三种代表性稀疏注意力算法后，SPIN实现1.66-5.66倍端到端吞吐量提升、7-9倍TTFT降低，TPOT较原稀疏注意力实现降低58%。

Conclusion: SPIN通过与vLLM集成，在三种稀疏注意力算法上验证了设计的有效性，实现了1.66-5.66倍吞吐量提升、7-9倍TTFT降低及高达58%的TPOT改善。

Abstract: Long-context LLM serving is bottlenecked by the cost of attending over ever-growing KV caches. Dynamic sparse attention promises relief by accessing only a small, query-dependent subset of the KV state per decoding step and extending the KV storage to CPU memory. In practice, however, these algorithmic savings rarely translate into end-to-end system-level gains because sparse methods typically operate at different granularities and thus rely on ad hoc, per-algorithm implementations. At the same time, hierarchical KV storage introduces a new systems bottleneck: retrieving fine-grained, irregular KV subsets across the GPU-CPU boundary can easily erase the benefits of sparsity.
  We present SPIN, a sparse-attention-aware inference framework that co-designs the execution pipeline with hierarchical KV storage through three techniques: (1) a unified partition abstraction that maps different sparsity granularities onto a shared page-based KV substrate; (2) a locality-aware KV cache manager that dynamically sizes per-request HBM budgets and uses a GPU-friendly bucketed LRU policy to cut PCIe round-trips; and (3) a two-level hierarchical metadata layout sized to the active working set rather than the worst-case address space. Built on vLLM with three representative sparse attention algorithms, SPIN delivers 1.66-5.66x higher end-to-end throughput and 7-9x lower TTFT than vLLM, and reduces TPOT by up to 58% over the original sparse-attention implementations.

</details>


### [110] [Language Diffusion Models are Associative Memories Capable of Retrieving Unseen Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26841)
*Bao Pham,Mohammed J. Zaki,Luca Ambrogioni,Dmitry Krotov,Matteo Negri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了均匀离散扩散模型（UDDMs）可作为具有涌现创造力的联想记忆模型，并发现训练集规模驱动的记忆化-泛化相变。条件熵被识别为检测该相变的实用探针，为评估部署模型的真实生成模式提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言扩散模型何时记忆训练数据而非真正生成新内容？这一"记忆化-泛化"边界问题缺乏定量评估手段，亟需建立可操作的判断标准。

Method: 研究通过评估训练和测试样本的token恢复能力来识别UDDMs中的 basins of attraction，并利用条件似然最大化而非传统能量函数来形成吸引域。核心实验是观测条件熵随训练集规模变化的行为。

Result: 实验发现UUDMs存在尖锐的记忆化-泛化转变：随着训练数据集规模增大，训练样本周围的吸引域收缩，测试样本周围的吸引域扩张，最终两者收敛至相同水平。该转变可通过预测token序列的条件熵检测：记忆化时条件熵趋于零，泛化时大多数token的条件熵保持有限。

Conclusion: UDDMs可以通过条件熵作为探针来区分记忆化与泛化模式：当预测序列的条件熵趋于零时表明模型处于记忆化状态，而当大多数token的条件熵保持有限时则表明处于泛化状态。该方法为部署模型的记忆化评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: When do language diffusion models memorize their training data, and how to quantitatively assess their true generative regime? We address these questions by showing that Uniform-based Discrete Diffusion Models (UDDMs) fundamentally behave as Associative Memories (AMs) $\textit{with emergent creative capabilities}$. The core idea of an AM is to reliably recover stored data points as $\textit{memories}$ by establishing distinct basins of attraction around them. Historically, models like Hopfield networks use an explicit energy function to guarantee these stable attractors. We broaden this perspective by leveraging the observation that energy is not strictly necessary, as basins of attraction can also be formed via conditional likelihood maximization. By evaluating token recovery of $\textit{training}$ and $\textit{test}$ examples, we identify in UDDMs a sharp memorization-to-generalization transition governed by the size of the training dataset: as it increases, basins around training examples shrink and basins around unseen test examples expand, until both later converge to the same level. Crucially, we can detect this transition using only the conditional entropy of predicted token sequences: memorization is characterized by vanishing conditional entropy, while in the generalization regime the conditional entropy of most tokens remains finite. Thus, conditional entropy offers a practical probe for the memorization-to-generalization transition in deployed models.

</details>


### [111] [KAYRA: A Microservice Architecture for AI-Assisted Karyotyping with Cloud and On-Premise Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26869)
*Attila Pintér,Javier Rico,Attila Répai,Jalal Al-Afandi,Adrienn Éva Borsy,András Kozma,Hajnalka Andrikovics,György Cserey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KAYRA是一种临床级核型分析端到端系统，采用容器化微服务架构，整合EfficientNet-B5+U-Net、Mask R-CNN和ResNet-18三个深度学习模型，通过级联ROI筛选策略实现染色体分割、检测和分类。在459条染色体上的临床评估显示分割准确率98.91%、分类准确率89.1%、旋转准确率89.76%，显著优于传统商用系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核型分析是遗传疾病诊断的关键，但现有商业系统存在性能局限（尤其是分割环节），且缺乏灵活的云端/本地部署选项以满足不同临床环境的数据合规要求。

Method: 采用微服务架构整合多模型ML pipeline：EfficientNet-B5+U-Net进行语义分割、Mask R-CNN进行实例检测、ResNet-18进行分类，通过级联ROI筛选策略逐步聚焦染色体区域，支持云端和本地双重部署模式。

Result: 在10个中期分裂相的459条染色体上评估，分割准确率98.91%（vs 78.21%/40.52%）、分类准确率89.1%（vs 86.9%/54.5%）、旋转准确率89.76%（vs 94.55%/78.43%）。分割和分类指标相比旧系统具有统计学显著性（p<0.0001），分割相比新AI系统也有显著提升（p<0.0001），分类差异不显著（p=0.34）。

Conclusion: 多模型细胞遗传学AI系统可通过微服务架构实现灵活的云端/本地部署，在临床评估中达到强性能表现，达到TRL 6成熟度，并整合了临床所需的人工专家审核工作流程。

Abstract: We present KAYRA, an end-to-end karyotyping system that operates inside the operational constraints of a clinical cytogenetic laboratory. KAYRA is architected as a containerized microservice pipeline whose ML stack combines an EfficientNet-B5 + U-Net semantic segmenter, a Mask R-CNN (ResNet-50 + FPN) instance detector, and a ResNet-18 classifier, orchestrated through a cascaded ROI-narrowing strategy that focuses each downstream model on the chromosome-bearing region. The same container images are deployed both as a cloud service and as an on-premise installation, supporting clinical environments where patient-data egress is not permitted as well as those where it is. A pilot clinical evaluation against two commercial reference karyotyping systems on 459 chromosomes from 10 metaphase spreads shows segmentation accuracy of 98.91 % (vs. 78.21 % / 40.52 %), classification accuracy of 89.1 % (vs. 86.9 % / 54.5 %), and rotation accuracy of 89.76 % (vs. 94.55 % / 78.43 %). KAYRA improves over the older density-thresholding reference on all three axes (p < 0.0001 for segmentation and classification by Fisher's exact test on chromosome-level counts), and on segmentation also against the modern AI- supported reference (p < 0.0001); on classification the difference vs. the modern AI reference is not statistically significant at the present test-set size (p = 0.34). The system reaches TRL 6 maturity and integrates the human-in-the-loop expert-review workflow that diagnostic cytogenetic practice requires. The thesis of this paper is that a multi-model cytogenetic AI service can be packaged as a microservice architecture supporting flexible deployment - cloud-hosted or on-premise - while delivering strong empirical performance on a pilot clinical evaluation.

</details>


### [112] [Multiple Additive Neural Networks for Structured and Unstructured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26888)
*Janis Mohr,Jörg Frochte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MANN extends Gradient Boosting by using shallow neural networks as base learners instead of decision trees, incorporating CNNs and Capsule Neural Networks to handle both structured and unstructured data. It integrates anti-overfitting heuristics for robustness and outperforms XGBoost in accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional Gradient Boosting relies on decision trees as base learners, limiting its adaptability to unstructured data and requiring careful hyperparameter tuning. There is a need for a more flexible framework that can handle both structured and unstructured data while maintaining robustness.

Method: MANN replaces decision trees with shallow neural networks (CNNs and Capsule Neural Networks) as base learners in the Gradient Boosting framework, integrates advanced anti-overfitting heuristics, and applies capsule networks as feature extractors for structured data classification.

Result: MANN achieves superior accuracy over Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGB) across well-known datasets, with reduced sensitivity to hyperparameters like learning rate and iterations.

Conclusion: MANN demonstrates superior precision and generalizability compared to XGBoost, serving as a versatile tool for diverse data types and complex learning environments.

Abstract: This paper extends and explains the Multiple Additive Neural Networks (MANN) methodology, an enhancement to the traditional Gradient Boosting framework, utilizing nearly shallow neural networks instead of decision trees as base learners. This innovative approach leverages neural network architectures, notably Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Capsule Neural Networks, to extend its application to both structured data and unstructured data such as images and audio. For structured data the advantages of capsule neural networks as feature extractors are used and combined with MANN as a classifier. MANN's unique architecture promotes continuous learning and integrates advanced heuristics to combat overfitting, ensuring robustness and reducing sensitivity to hyperparameter settings like learning rate and iterations. Our empirical studies reveal that MANN surpasses traditional methods such as Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGB) in accuracy across well-known datasets. This research demonstrates MANN's superior precision and generalizability, making it a versatile tool for diverse data types and complex learning environments.

</details>


### [113] [Causal Learning with Neural Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26919)
*Evangelia Kopadi,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIRECT机制，证明神经 assembly（神经元集合）能够学习变量间的因果方向。该机制仅依赖局部可塑性，无需反向传播，通过自适应增益调度使源和目标 assembly 共同激活，从而内化定向关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经 assembly 在分类、解析和规划任务中表现出计算通用性，但尚未证明其能够学习因果方向性。这限制了其作为可解释因果模型的潜力。

Method: 引入DIRECT机制：结合投射、局部可塑性控制和稀疏winner选择三种神经assembly固有操作；在自适应增益调度下使源和目标assembly共同激活。通过双重验证策略：(i)突触强度不对称性，测量正向与反向链路的权重差距；(ii)功能传播重叠度，量化定向信号流的可靠性。

Result: 在监督学习且结构已知的设置下，该框架在多个领域实现完美的结构恢复。神经assembly能够内化因果方向性，且因果主张可在机制层面被审计。

Conclusion: 神经assembly可作为生物可信动力学与形式因果模型之间的可审计桥梁，提供"设计即解释"的框架，使因果主张可追溯至特定神经winner和突触不对称性。

Abstract: Can Neural Assemblies -- groups of neurons that fire together and strengthen through co-activation -- learn the direction of causal influence between variables? While established as a computationally general substrate for classification, parsing, and planning, neural assemblies have not yet been shown to internalize causal directionality. We demonstrate that the inherent operations of neural assemblies -- projection, local plasticity control, and sparse winner selection -- are sufficient for directional learning. We introduce DIRECT (DIRectional Edge Coupling/Training), a mechanism that co-activates source and target assemblies under an adaptive gain schedule to internalize directed relations. Unlike backpropagation-based methods, DIRECT relies solely on local plasticity, making the resulting causal claims auditable at the mechanism level. Our findings are verified through a dual-readout validation strategy: (i) synaptic-strength asymmetry, measuring the emergent weight gap between forward and reverse links, and (ii) functional propagation overlap, quantifying the reliability of directional signal flow. Across multiple domains, the framework achieves perfect structural recovery under a supervised, known-structure setting. These results establish neural assemblies as an auditable bridge between biologically plausible dynamics and formal causal models, offering an "explainable by design" framework where causal claims are traceable to specific neural winners and synaptic asymmetries.

</details>


### [114] [A Note on How to Remove the $\ln\ln T$ Term from the Squint Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26926)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This technical note shows that the shifted KT potentials from Orabona and Pál [2016] are equivalent to changing the prior in the Krichevsky-Trofimov algorithm, and the same idea can be used to remove the ln ln T factor from the Squint algorithm's data-independent bound.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The ln ln T factor in parameter-free learning bounds (both expert bounds and data-independent bounds for Squint) is considered undesirable, and removing this factor improves the theoretical guarantees.

Method: The equivalence between shifted KT potentials and prior modification in the Krichevsky-Trofimov algorithm is established through theoretical analysis. This same approach is then applied to the Squint algorithm to remove its ln ln T factor.

Result: Successfully demonstrated that shifted KT potentials are equivalent to changing the prior in the KT algorithm, and extended this equivalence to remove the ln ln T factor in the Squint algorithm.

Conclusion: The modified prior approach effectively eliminates the ln ln T factor in both the KT-based method and the Squint algorithm, providing a unified framework for parameter-free learning.

Abstract: In Orabona and Pál [2016], we introduced the shifted KT potentials, to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the parameter-free learning with expert bound. In this short technical note, I show that this is equivalent to changing the prior in the Krichevsky--Trofimov algorithm. Then, I show how to use the same idea to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the data-independent bound for the Squint algorithm.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Hierarchical Multi-Persona Induction from User Behavioral Logs: Learning Evidence-Grounded and Truthful Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26120)
*Nayoung Choi,Haeyu Jeong,Changbong Kim,Hongjun Lim,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种层级化框架，通过将用户行为聚合为意图记忆，并聚类标记这些记忆来诱导多个有证据支撑的用户画像。使用群体直接偏好优化(DPO)训练，平衡聚类内聚力、画像-证据对齐度和可信度三个目标。实验表明该方法生成更连贯、有据可查、可信赖的用户画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户画像生成方法多关注下游效用，缺乏对画像本身质量的评估；且行为日志通常噪声大、意图混杂，需要系统性方法从中提取高质量、结构化的用户表征。

Method: 1. 层级化框架：用户行为→意图记忆→聚类→画像标签；2. 将画像诱导建模为优化问题，目标函数包含聚类内聚力、画像-证据对齐度、真实可信度；3. 采用群体扩展的DPO(Direct Preference Optimization)训练画像模型。

Result: 在大规模服务日志及两个公开数据集上，生成的用户画像在连贯性、证据支撑、可信度方面均有提升，同时改善了未来交互预测效果。

Conclusion: 通过将画像诱导形式化为优化问题并结合人类偏好学习，可有效从噪声行为日志中提取高质量、多维度、有据可查的用户画像，兼具解释性与预测性能。

Abstract: Behavioral logs provide rich signals for user modeling, but are noisy and interleaved across diverse intents. Recent work uses LLMs to generate interpretable natural-language personas from user logs, yet evaluation often emphasizes downstream utility, providing limited assurance of persona quality itself. We propose a hierarchical framework that aggregates user actions into intent memories and induces multiple evidence-grounded personas by clustering and labeling these memories. We formulate persona induction as an optimization problem over persona quality-captured by cluster cohesion, persona-evidence alignment, and persona truthfulness-and train the persona model using a groupwise extension of Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Experiments on a large-scale service log and two public datasets show that our method induces more coherent, evidence-grounded, and trustworthy personas, while also improving future interaction prediction.

</details>


### [116] [AGEL-Comp: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Compositional Generalization in Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26522)
*Mahnoor Shahid,Hannes Rothe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGEL-Comp是一种神经符号AI智能体架构，通过因果程序图(CPG)、归纳逻辑编程(ILP)引擎和混合推理核心三个创新组件，解决大语言模型智能体在组合泛化方面的系统性失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型(LLM)智能体在组合泛化(compositional generalization)方面存在系统性失败，限制了其在交互环境中的鲁棒性。这一缺陷阻碍了智能体在新场景中灵活应用已有知识和技能的能力。

Method: 该框架提出三大核心创新：(1)动态因果程序图(CPG)作为世界模型，将程序性和因果性知识表示为有向超图；(2)归纳逻辑编程(ILP)引擎，从体验反馈中综合新的Horn子句，通过交互实现符号知识的接地；(3)混合推理核心，其中LLM提出候选子目标，由神经定理证明器(NTP)验证逻辑一致性。三者协同实现演绎-归纳学习循环，使智能体能够演绎规划并归纳性地扩展符号世界模型，同时神经适应阶段保持推理引擎与新知识对齐。

Result: 在Retro Quest仿真环境中进行评估，实验结果明确表明AGEL模型在组合泛化任务上的性能优于纯LLM模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建具有显式、可解释和组合结构化世界理解的智能体提供了原则性路径，标志着神经符号方法在提升智能体组合泛化能力方面的重要进展。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents exhibit systemic failures in compositional generalization, limiting their robustness in interactive environments. This work introduces AGEL-Comp, a neuro-symbolic AI agent architecture designed to address this challenge by grounding actions of the agent. AGEL-Comp integrates three core innovations: (1) a dynamic Causal Program Graph (CPG) as a world model, representing procedural and causal knowledge as a directed hypergraph; (2) an Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) engine that synthesizes new Horn clauses from experiential feedback, grounding symbolic knowledge through interaction; and (3) a hybrid reasoning core where an LLM proposes a set of candidate sub-goals that are verified for logical consistency by a Neural Theorem Prover (NTP). Together, these components operationalize a deduction--abduction learning cycle: enabling the agent to deduce plans and abductively expand its symbolic world model, while a neural adaptation phase keeps its reasoning engine aligned with new knowledge. We propose an evaluation protocol within the \texttt{Retro Quest} simulation environment to probe for compositional generalization scenarios to evaluate our AGEL agent. Our findings clearly indicate the better performance of our AGEL model over pure LLM-based models. Our framework presents a principled path toward agents that build an explicit, interpretable, and compositionally structured understanding of their world.

</details>


### [117] [OMEGA: Optimizing Machine Learning by Evaluating Generated Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26211)
*Jeremy Nixon,Annika Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OMEGA框架，一个端到端的自动化AI研究系统，通过结合元提示工程与代码生成技术自动设计机器学习分类算法。该系统在20个基准数据集上验证了生成算法的有效性，并已开源为Python包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在实现AI研究的自动化，通过自动化流程减少人工设计机器学习算法的工作量。

Method: OMEGA框架结合了结构化元提示工程（structured meta-prompt engineering）与可执行代码生成技术，实现从想法生成到代码执行的全流程自动化。

Result: OMEGA框架成功生成了多个创新的ML分类算法，在20个基准数据集（infinity-bench）上的性能超越了scikit-learn基线模型。

Conclusion: OMEGA框架已实现落地应用，可通过pip install omega-models获取。该研究在自动化机器学习算法设计领域提供了端到端的完整解决方案。

Abstract: In order to automate AI research we introduce a full, end-to-end framework, OMEGA: Optimizing Machine learning by Evaluating Generated Algorithms, that starts at idea generation and ends with executable code. Our system combines structured meta-prompt engineering with executable code generation to create new ML classifiers. The OMEGA framework has been utilized to generate several novel algorithms that outperform scikit-learn baselines across a robust selection of 20 benchmark datasets (infinity-bench). You can access models discussed in this paper and more in the python package: pip install omega-models.

</details>


### [118] [Bian Que: An Agentic Framework with Flexible Skill Arrangement for Online System Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26805)
*Bochao Liu,Zhipeng Qian,Yang Zhao,Xinyuan Jiang,Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Junpeng Zhuang,Ben Chen,Shuo Yang,Hongen Wan,Yao Wu,Chenyi Lei,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Bian Que是一个用于大规模在线引擎系统运维的智能体框架，通过统一运维范式、灵活技能编排和自演化机制，解决了LLM智能体在运维场景下的编排瓶颈问题，在快手平台实现显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线引擎系统的运维面临release监控、告警响应、根源分析等高人力成本挑战。LLM智能体能胜任此类任务，但瓶颈在于编排——需为每个运维事件选择相关数据（指标、日志、变更事件）和适用知识（手册规则、实践经验）。无差别投喂所有信号会导致稀释和幻觉，而人工维护事件到（数据、知识）的映射在每日数十次发布场景下不可扩展。

Method: 提出统一运维范式（含三种模式）、灵活技能编排机制（Skill可自动生成和迭代更新）、统一自演化机制（case-memory-to-knowledge蒸馏与定向Skill优化双路径并行）。

Result: 部署于快手电商搜索引擎后，alert volume降低75%，根因分析准确率80%，MTTR降低50%+，离线评估通过率99.0%。

Conclusion: Bian Que框架在快手电商搜索引擎上成功部署，alert volume降低75%，根因分析准确率达80%，MTTR降低50%以上，离线评估通过率99.0%，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Operating and maintaining (O&M) large-scale online engine systems (search, recommendation, advertising) demands substantial human effort for release monitoring, alert response, and root cause analysis. While LLM-based agents are a natural fit for these tasks, the deployment bottleneck is not reasoning capability but orchestration: selecting, for each operational event, the relevant data (metrics, logs, change events) and the applicable operational knowledge (handbook rules and practitioner experience). Feeding all signals indiscriminately causes dilution and hallucination, while manually curating the event-to-(data, knowledge) mapping is intractable under dozens of daily releases. We present Bian Que, an agentic framework with three contributions: (i) a \emph{unified operational paradigm} abstracting day-to-day O&M into three canonical patterns: release interception, proactive inspection, and alert root cause analysis; (ii) \emph{Flexible Skill Arrangement}, where each Skill specifies which data and knowledge to retrieve for a given business-module context and can be automatically generated and updated by LLMs or iteratively refined through natural-language instructions from on-call engineers; (iii) a \emph{unified self-evolving mechanism} in which one correction signal drives two parallel pathways, case-memory-to-knowledge distillation and targeted Skill refinement. Deployed on the e-commerce search engine of KuaiShou, the major short-video platform in China, Bian Que reduces alert volume by 75%, achieves 80% root-cause analysis accuracy, and cuts mean time to resolution by over 50%. Our framework achieves 99.0% pass rate on offline evaluations. Our code is available at https://github.com/benchen4395/BianQue_Assistant.

</details>


### [119] [Persuadability and LLMs as Legal Decision Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26233)
*Oisin Suttle,David Lillis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）作为法律决策辅助工具或初审决策者时，如何响应法律论证，核心问题是：辩护律师的质量是否会影响LLM接受特定法律观点的倾向。研究通过实验测试了前沿开源和闭源LLM在这一情境下的表现，并探讨了相关驱动因素。结果对于LLM在司法和行政场景中的可行性具有重要参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被提议作为法律决策辅助工具甚至初审决策者，必须理解它们如何回答法律问题，尤其是影响其做出困难决策的因素。法律决策的一个核心特征是需要回应各方提出的论证，因此需要评估LLM是否具备与论证交互、被说服或保持独立判断的能力。

Method: 实验研究方法，通过设计法律论证场景测试多个前沿LLM（开源和闭源模型），考察辩护律师的质量（论证质量/说服力）如何影响模型接受特定法律观点的概率。

Result: 实验结果表明，辩护律师的质量确实会影响LLM对法律观点的接受程度，模型表现出一定程度的被说服性，但同时也揭示了影响这一现象的具体因素。

Conclusion: 研究结果对于LLM在司法和行政环境中的实际应用具有重要影响，提示需要审慎评估LLM作为法律决策者的可行性，尤其是在确保决策的独立性和客观性方面。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are proposed as legal decision assistants, and even first-instance decision-makers, across a range of judicial and administrative contexts, it becomes essential to explore how they answer legal questions, and in particular the factors that lead them to decide difficult questions in one way or another. A specific feature of legal decisions is the need to respond to arguments advanced by contending parties. A legal decision-maker must be able to engage with, and respond to, including through being potentially persuaded by, arguments advanced by the parties. Conversely, they should not be unduly persuadable, influenced by a particularly compelling advocate to decide cases based on the skills of the advocates, rather than the merits of the case. We explore how frontier open- and closed-weights LLMs respond to legal arguments, reporting original experimental results examining how the quality of the advocate making those arguments affects the likelihood that a model will agree with a particular legal point of view, and exploring the factors driving these results. Our results have implications for the feasibility of adopting LLMs across legal and administrative settings.

</details>


### [120] [Apriori-based Analysis of Learned Helplessness in Mathematics Tutoring: Behavioral Patterns by Level, Intervention, and Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26237)
*John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用Apriori算法分析数学辅导系统日志，发现跳过问题且不使用提示是导致未解决结果的最频繁行为模式。低无助感学生具有更强的坚持行为与成功解决之间的关联，而高度无助学生表现出更多回避行为。研究结果表明系统干预对改变学生行为模式具有重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索数学辅导系统中习得性无助（LH）学生的行为交互模式，识别与无助感相关的特定行为特征，为教学干预提供数据支持。

Method: 采用Apriori关联规则算法分析数学辅导系统日志中的行为交互模式，从无助感水平、系统干预、问题解决结果三个维度进行交互数据挖掘。

Result: 低LH学生表现出问题解决与不跳过行为之间的强关联，以及提示使用与成功解决的正相关；高LH学生表现出更多回避模式，跳过行为与未解决结果紧密相关。无干预组在坚持-成功关联上提升最高，有干预组在跳过-未解决模式上更强。不跳过行为始终与成功解决相关，而跳过无提示预测未解决结果。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了不同无助感水平学生在数学辅导系统中的行为模式差异，提出针对性干预建议。跳过问题且不使用提示的行为模式是预测未解决问题的关键指标，值得在教学系统中重点关注。

Abstract: This study applied the Apriori algorithm to analyze behavioral interaction patterns associated with learned helplessness (LH) in mathematics tutoring system logs. Interaction data were examined across three dimensions: LH level (low vs. high), system-based intervention (with vs. without), and problem-solving outcomes (solved vs. unsolved). The analysis of the complete dataset showed that skipping problems without using hints was the most frequent pattern linked to unsolved outcomes, while persistence behaviors such as not skipping were less dominant overall. Comparisons by LH level showed that low-LH students had stronger links between problem solving and not skipping, as well as positive associations between hint use and solved outcomes. High-LH students showed more avoidance patterns, with skipping strongly tied to unsolved outcomes. In the comparison of system-based intervention conditions, students without intervention had the highest lift for persistence-success links, while the with-intervention group had stronger patterns involving skipping behaviors leading to unsolved outcomes. Outcome-specific analysis showed that not skipping was consistently associated with solved problems across all groups, while skipping without hints predicted unsolved outcomes. Practical implications and recommendations are discussed.

</details>


### [121] [DreamProver: Evolving Transferable Lemma Libraries via a Wake-Sleep Theorem-Proving Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26311)
*Youyuan Zhang,Jialiang Sun,Hangrui Bi,Chuqin Geng,Wenjie Ma,Zhaoyu Li,Xujie Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DreamProver通过"清醒-睡眠"程序归纳范式，利用迭代两阶段过程（wake阶段尝试证明并提出候选引理，sleep阶段抽象提炼并压缩引理库），逐步演化出一组可迁移的高层引理，在多个数学基准上显著提升证明成功率，同时产生更简洁的证明并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing lemma-based approaches suffer from a trade-off between adaptability (fixed libraries) and generality (specific lemmas lack transferability to new theorems).

Method: An iterative two-stage "wake-sleep" program induction paradigm: the wake stage proves theorems and proposes candidate lemmas; the sleep stage abstracts, refines, and consolidates candidates to compress and optimize the lemma library.

Result: Substantially improved proof success rates across diverse mathematical benchmarks, with more concise proofs and reduced computational cost.

Conclusion: DreamProver produces a compact set of reusable, high-level lemmas that generalize across related mathematical domains, substantially improving proof success rates on diverse benchmarks while generating more concise proofs at lower computational cost.

Abstract: We introduce DreamProver, an agentic framework that leverages a "wake-sleep" program induction paradigm to discover reusable lemmas for formal theorem proving. Existing approaches either rely on fixed lemma libraries, which limit adaptability, or synthesize highly specific intermediate lemmas tailored to individual theorems, thereby lacking generality. DreamProver addresses this gap through an iterative two-stage process. In the wake stage, DreamProver attempts to prove theorems from a training set using the current lemma library while proposing new candidate lemmas. In the "sleep" stage, it abstracts, refines, and consolidates these candidates to compress and optimize the library. Through this alternating cycle, DreamProver progressively evolves a compact set of high-level, transferable lemmas that can be effectively used to prove unseen theorems in related domains. Experimental results demonstrate that DreamProver substantially improves proof success rates across a diverse set of mathematical benchmarks, while also producing more concise proofs and reducing computational cost.

</details>


### [122] [Benchmarking the Safety of Large Language Models for Robotic Health Attendant Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26577)
*Mahiro Nakao,Kazuhiro Takemoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了72个大语言模型在机器人健康护理场景中的安全性，创建了包含270条有害指令的数据集。结果显示平均违规率达54.4%，大多数模型超过50%；表面合理的指令比明显破坏性指令更难拒绝；闭源模型显著优于开源模型；医学领域微调未带来安全收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人健康护理场景中LLM部署的安全性尚未得到充分评估，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 基于美国医学协会医学伦理原则构建270条有害指令数据集，覆盖9类禁止行为类别，在 Robotic Health Attendant 框架模拟环境中测试72个LLM。

Result: 平均违规率54.4%；表面合理的指令（如设备操纵、急救延迟）比明显破坏性指令更难拒绝；模型规模和发布时间是开源模型安全性的主要决定因素；闭源模型（中位数23.7%）显著优于开源模型（中位数72.8%）；医学微调未带来显著安全收益；提示防御策略仅产生有限改善。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在机器人健康护理场景中存在严重安全隐患，安全性评估应被视为该领域开发与部署的首要标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for deployment as the control component of robotic health attendants, yet their safety in this context remains poorly characterized. We introduce a dataset of 270 harmful instructions spanning nine prohibited behavior categories grounded in the American Medical Association Principles of Medical Ethics, and use it to evaluate 72 LLMs in a simulation environment based on the Robotic Health Attendant framework. The mean violation rate across all models was 54.4\%, with more than half exceeding 50\%, and violation rates varied substantially across behavior categories, with superficially plausible instructions such as device manipulation and emergency delay proving harder to refuse than overtly destructive ones. Model size and release date were the primary determinants of safety performance among open-weight models, and proprietary models were substantially safer than open-weight counterparts (median 23.7\% versus 72.8\%). Medical domain fine-tuning conferred no significant overall safety benefit, and a prompt-based defense strategy produced only a modest reduction in violation rates among the least safe models, leaving absolute violation rates at levels that would preclude safe clinical deployment. These findings demonstrate that safety evaluation must be treated as a first-class criterion in the development and deployment of LLMs for robotic health attendants.

</details>


### [123] [Human-in-the-Loop Benchmarking of Heterogeneous LLMs for Automated Competency Assessment in Secondary Level Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26607)
*Jatin Bhusal,Nancy Mahatha,Aayush Acharya,Raunak Regmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了多个LLM在自动化数学能力评估中的表现，发现Gemini-based MoE模型表现优于dense 70B模型，揭示架构设计对约束任务的适配性比模型规模更重要，建议LLM作为辅助工具而非自主评估者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CBE从分数制向能力映射转变带来的手动评估瓶颈问题

Method: 多提供商模型集成测试，Gemini-based MoE模型对比dense 70B模型，基于kappa_w一致性评估

Result: 发现"架构兼容性差距"：MoE模型（kappa_w≈0.38）优于dense 70B模型（kappa_w=-0.0261），架构合规性比参数规模更重要

Conclusion: LLM尚不适合自主认证评估，但在"Human-in-the-Loop"框架内可提供高价值辅助支持

Abstract: As Competency-Based Education (CBE) is gaining traction around the world, the shift from marks-based assessment to qualitative competency mapping is a manual challenge for educators. This paper tackles the bottleneck issue by suggesting a "Human-in-the-Loop" benchmarking framework to assess the effectiveness of multiple LLMs in automating secondary-level mathematics assessment. Based on the Grade 10 Optional Mathematics curriculum in Nepal, we created a multi-dimensional rubric for four topics and four cross-cutting competencies: Comprehension, Knowledge, Operational Fluency, and Behavior and Correlation.
  The multi-provider ensemble, consisted of open-weight models -- Eagle (Llama 3.1-8B) and Orion (Llama 3.3-70B) -- and proprietary frontier models Nova (Gemini 2.5 Flash) and Lyra (Gemini 3 Pro), was benchmarked against a ground truth defined by two senior mathematics faculty members (kappa_w = 0.8652). The findings show a marked "Architecture-compatibility gap". Although the Gemini-based Mixture-of-Experts (Sparse MoE) models achieved "Fair Agreement" (kappa_w ~ 0.38), the larger Orion (70B) model exhibited "No Agreement" (kappa_w = -0.0261), suggesting that architectural compliance with instruction constraints outweighs the scale of raw parameters in rubric-constrained tasks. We conclude that while LLMs are not yet suitable for autonomous certification, they provide high-value assistive support for preliminary evidence extraction within a "Human-in-the-Loop" framework.

</details>


### [124] [When to Vote, When to Rewrite: Disagreement-Guided Strategy Routing for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26644)
*Zhimin Lin,Yixin Ji,Jinpeng Li,Yu Luo,Dong Li,Junhua Fang,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于输出分歧度进行测试时扩展策略选择的训练无关框架，针对数学推理任务动态选择重采样、多数投票或重写等不同策略，在保持甚至降低计算成本的同时提升准确率3%-7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法（如重复采样、自纠正、树搜索）在困难问题上存在边际收益递减问题，且缺乏对实例难度的自适应能力。

Method: 核心洞察：输出分歧度与实例难度及预测正确性高度相关。提出基于分歧度的实例级路由框架：轻度不一致使用轻量解析，中度分歧使用多数投票，高度模糊使用重写式重构。方法为训练无关，无需额外训练成本。

Result: 在7个数学基准数据集和3个模型上验证，方法提升准确率3%-7%，同时减少采样成本。

Conclusion: 将测试时扩展重新表述为实例级路由问题而非单一策略内的计算分配问题，是一种有效且经济的提升LRM推理能力的方法。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on mathematical reasoning tasks but remain unreliable on challenging instances. Existing test-time scaling methods, such as repeated sampling, self-correction, and tree search, improve performance at the cost of increased computation, yet often exhibit diminishing returns on hard problems. We observe that output disagreement is strongly correlated with instance difficulty and prediction correctness, providing a useful signal for guiding instance-level strategy selection at test time. Based on this insight, we propose a training-free framework that formulates test-time scaling as an instance-level routing problem, rather than allocating more computation within a single strategy, dynamically selecting among different scaling strategies based on output disagreement. The framework applies lightweight resolution for consistent cases, majority voting for moderate disagreement, and rewriting-based reformulation for highly ambiguous instances. Experiments on seven mathematical benchmarks and three models show that our method improves accuracy by 3% - 7% while reducing sampling cost compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [125] [SciHorizon-DataEVA: An Agentic System for AI-Readiness Evaluation of Heterogeneous Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26645)
*Dianyu Liu,Chuan Qin,Xi Chen,Xiaohan Li,Wenxi Xu,Yuyang Wang,Xin Chen,Yuanchun Zhou,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciHorizon-DataEVA系统，用于可扩展评估异构科学数据的AI就绪度。核心贡献包括：Sci-TQA2原则（四维度：治理可信度、数据质量、AI兼容性、科学适应性）以及Sci-TQA2-Eval层级多智能体评估方法。该系统通过动态构建评估规范和自适应评估机制，实现了对异构科学数据的可靠评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI4Science中模型的效能受制于科学数据的AI就绪度，但目前缺乏可扩展且系统化的评估机制。

Method: 提出Sci-TQA2-Eval层级多智能体评估方法，采用有向循环工作流编排；通过轻量级数据集分析、适用性感知指标激活和知识增强规划动态构建数据集感知评估规范；通过自适应工具中心评估机制（内置验证与自校正）执行评估。

Result: 在跨多个领域的科学数据集上进行实验，验证了SciHorizon-DataEVA的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SciHorizon-DataEVA能够对异构科学数据进行可扩展且可靠的AI就绪度评估，实验验证了其在跨领域科学数据集上的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: AI-for-Science (AI4Science) is increasingly transforming scientific discovery by embedding machine learning models into prediction, simulation, and hypothesis generation workflows across domains. However, the effectiveness of these models is fundamentally constrained by the AI-readiness of scientific data, for which no scalable and systematic evaluation mechanism currently exists. In this work, we propose SciHorizon-DataEVA, a novel agentic system to scalable AI-readiness evaluation of heterogeneous scientific data. At the evaluation-criteria level, we introduce the Sci-TQA2 principles, which organize AI-readiness into four complementary dimensions: Governance Trustworthiness, Data Quality, AI Compatibility, and Scientific Adaptability. Each dimension is decomposed into measurable atomic elements that enable fine-grained and executable assessment. To operationalize these principles at scale, we develop Sci-TQA2-Eval, a hierarchical multi-agent evaluation approach orchestrated through a directed, cyclic workflow. Our Sci-TQA2-Eval dynamically constructs dataset-aware evaluation specifications by combining lightweight dataset profiling, applicability-aware metric activation, and knowledge-augmented planning grounded in domain constraints and dataset-paper signals. These specifications are executed through an adaptive, tool-centric evaluation mechanism with built-in verification and self-correction, enabling scalable and reliable assessment across heterogeneous scientific data. Extensive experiments on scientific datasets spanning multiple domains demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SciHorizon-DataEVA for principled AI-readiness evaluation.

</details>


### [126] [FutureWorld: A Live Environment for Training Predictive Agents with Real-World Outcome Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26733)
*Zhixin Han,Yanzhi Zhang,Chuyang Wei,Maohang Gao,Xiawei Yue,Kefei Chen,Yu Zhuang,Haoxiang Guan,Jiyan He,Jian Li,Yitong Duan,Yu Shi,Mengting Hu,Shuxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FutureWorld，一个将实时未来预测任务重新定义为学习型环境的框架。该环境通过大语言模型智能体系统，在真实世界事件发生前进行预测，并利用预测-结果-参数更新的闭环训练机制实现持续学习。实验表明该训练方法有效，并建立了评估前沿智能体系统的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已从多个角度探索未来预测，但尚未将其统一框架为学习型环境。实时未来预测具有作为学习环境的独特优势：可提供大量基于多样真实事件的预测问题，同时自然地防止答案泄露。但现有工作未能充分利用这一特性来构建完整的训练闭环。

Method: 本文提出FutureWorld——一个实时智能体强化学习环境。核心创新在于：1) 构建预测-结果实现-参数更新的完整训练闭环；2) 对三个开源基座模型进行连续多日训练；3) 建立每日基准测试用于评估前沿智能体系统的性能基线。

Result: 实验结果表明，在FutureWorld环境中对多个开源模型进行连续天数训练能够产生有效学习效果，且基于该环境构建的每日基准测试成功建立了当前主流智能体系统的性能基线。

Conclusion: 实时未来预测可被有效框架化为学习型强化学习环境，FutureWorld证明了"预测-结果-学习"的闭环训练范式的可行性，为构建能够从真实世界持续学习的智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Live future prediction refers to the task of making predictions about real-world events before they unfold. This task is increasingly studied using large language model-based agent systems, and it is important for building agents that can continually learn from real-world. Just as interactive environments have often driven progress in agents, advancing live future prediction naturally motivates viewing it as a learning environment. Prior works have explored future prediction from several different parts, but have generally not framed it as a unified learning environment. This task is appealing for learning because it can provide a large number of prediction questions grounded in diverse real-world events, while preventing answer leakage. To leverage the advantages of live future prediction, we present FutureWorld, a live agentic reinforcement learning environment that closes the training loop between prediction, outcome realization, and parameters update. In our environment, we take three open-source base models and train them for consecutive days. The results show that training is effective. Furthermore, we build a daily benchmark based on the environment and evaluate several frontier agents on it to establish performance baselines for current agent systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [ProMax: Exploring the Potential of LLM-derived Profiles with Distribution Shaping for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26231)
*Yi Zhang,Yiwen Zhang,Kai Zheng,Tong Chen,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ProMax框架，通过分布塑形(distributional shaping)方法，利用LLM提取的用户画像增强推荐系统性能。核心创新在于从检索视角出发，通过双重分布重塑过程引导推荐模型学习用户偏好，并揭示了用户与物品画像在特征空间中的协作关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究存在两个主要局限：(1) LLM提取的画像如何增强推荐性能的机制尚不清晰；(2) 现有方法多依赖非线性对齐和融合策略，导致语义信息损失，未能充分发挥画像潜力。

Method: 1) 采用密集检索(dense retrieval)揭示用户与物品画像在特征空间中的协作关系；2) 引入双重分布重塑过程，以画像分布作为引导信号，使推荐模型学习对观测交互之外物品的用户偏好；3) 将ProMax应用于4种经典推荐方法。

Result: ProMax在3个公开数据集上显著提升了基线模型性能，并优于现有基于LLM的推荐方法。

Conclusion: 从检索视角重新审视画像设计是有效的；分布塑形方法能够有效利用LLM提取的语义信息增强推荐性能；该框架具有良好的通用性，可与多种推荐方法结合。

Abstract: The remarkable text understanding and generation capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have revitalized the field of general recommendation based on implicit user feedback. Rather than deploying LLMs directly as recommendation models, a more flexible paradigm leverages their ability to interpret users' historical interactions and semantic contexts to extract structured profiles that characterize user preferences. These profiles can be further transformed into actionable high-dimensional representations, serving as powerful signals to augment and strengthen recommendation models. However, the mechanism by which such profiles enhance recommendation performance within the feature space remains insufficiently understood. Moreover, existing studies predominantly rely on nonlinear alignment and fusion strategies to incorporate these profiles, which often lead to semantic loss and fail to fully exploit their potential. To address these limitations, we revisit profiles from a retrieval perspective and propose a simple yet effective recommendation framework built upon distribution shaping (ProMax) in this paper. We begin by employing dense retrieval to uncover the collaborative relationships between user and item profiles within the feature space. Based on this insight, we introduce a dual distribution-reshaping process, in which the profile distribution acts as a guiding signal to steer the recommendation model toward learning user preferences for unseen items beyond the scope of observed interactions. We apply ProMax to four classic recommendation methods on three public datasets. The results indicate that ProMax substantially improves base model performance and outperforms existing LLM-based recommendation approaches.

</details>


### [128] [TimeMM: Time-as-Operator Spectral Filtering for Dynamic Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26247)
*Wei Yang,Rui Zhong,Zihan Lin,Xiaodan Wang,Cheng Chen,Huan Ren,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TimeMM是一个用于动态多模态推荐的时间条件谱滤波框架，通过将交互时效性映射为参数化时序核函数来重加权用户-项目交互图，并引入自适应谱滤波和谱感知模态路由机制捕捉非平稳用户兴趣演化，最终实现线性时间复杂度下对多模态时序偏好的精细建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法局限：现有大多模态推荐系统依赖静态交互图或粗糙时序启发式方法，无法捕捉用户兴趣的非平稳动态特性——不同偏好因素以不同速率变化，且视觉与文本线索在不同时间 regime 下主导决策。该问题在多模态场景下被进一步放大，亟需细粒度时序适应机制。

Method: TimeMM框架包含三个核心技术组件：(1) 时间即算子（Time-as-Operator）：将交互时效性映射为参数化时序核函数，重加权用户-项目图的边，生成成分特异性表征，规避显式特征分解；(2) 自适应谱滤波：根据时序上下文混合算子库，产生预测相关的有效谱响应；(3) 谱感知模态路由：对视觉和文本模态贡献进行时序条件校准；(4) 排名空间谱多样性正则化：促进互补专家行为，防止滤波器库崩溃。

Result: TimeMM在真实世界基准数据集上持续超越现有最优多模态推荐系统，验证了时空调制谱滤波范式的有效性。

Conclusion: TimeMM通过时空调制谱滤波框架，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时，有效解决了多模态推荐中用户偏好非平稳演化的核心挑战。实验证明该方法在真实基准数据集上持续超越现有最优多模态推荐系统，验证了时序感知谱滤波与模态路由机制的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation improves user modeling by integrating collaborative signals with heterogeneous item content. In real applications, user interests evolve over time and exhibit nonstationary dynamics, where different preference factors change at different rates. This challenge is amplified in multimodal settings because visual and textual cues can dominate decisions under different temporal regimes. Despite strong progress, most multimodal recommenders still rely on static interaction graphs or coarse temporal heuristics, which limits their ability to model continuous preference evolution with fine-grained temporal adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose TimeMM, a time-conditioned spectral filtering framework for dynamic multimodal recommendation. TimeMM instantiates Time-as-Operator by mapping interaction recency to a family of parametric temporal kernels that reweight edges on the user--item graph, producing component-specific representations without explicit eigendecomposition. To capture non-stationary interests, we introduce Adaptive Spectral Filtering that mixes the operator bank according to temporal context, yielding prediction-specific effective spectral responses. To account for modality-specific temporal sensitivity, we further propose Spectral-Aware Modality Routing that calibrates visual and textual contributions conditioned on the same temporal context. Finally, a ranking-space Spectral Diversity Regularization encourages complementary expert behaviors and prevents filter-bank collapse. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that TimeMM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multimodal recommenders while maintaining linear-time scalability.

</details>


### [129] [Explaining the "Why": A Unified Framework for the Additive Attribution of Changes in Arbitrary Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26266)
*Changsheng Zhou,Dajun Chen,Zhitao Shen,wei jiang,Yong Li,Peng Di*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper presents a game-theoretic framework for attributing changes in aggregated measures, offering a principled trade-off between generality and performance through measure classification and a spectrum of algorithms.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current attribution methods struggle to explain aggregated measure changes and lack a unified solution that is simultaneously general, holistic, and rigorous in interpretability.

Method: Introduce a principled attribution framework using cooperative game theory, with a classification of measures based on mathematical structure, enabling a spectrum of algorithms from general approximations to exact closed-form solutions.

Result: The framework provides numerical accuracy, generality for non-additive measures, unique interpretability for paradoxes, and significant practical utility outperforming existing systems.

Conclusion: The framework significantly outperforms existing root cause analysis systems in practical applications, validated through simulations, Simpson's Paradox case study, and comparative experiments.

Abstract: Explaining why aggregated measures change is a critical challenge in data analytics that existing systems struggle to address. While current attribution methods exist, they lack a unified solution that is simultaneously general for arbitrary measures, holistic across both data dimensions and measure composition, and rigorous in its interpretability. To bridge this gap, we introduce a principled framework that reframes attribution through the powerful lens of cooperative game theory. Our key contribution is a classification of measures based on their mathematical structure, which enables a spectrum of algorithms-from general approximations to exact, closed-form solutions-that offer a principled trade-off between generality and performance. We demonstrate our framework's superiority through a multi-faceted evaluation: simulations first confirm its numerical accuracy and then its generality for non-additive measures; a case study on Simpson's Paradox showcases its unique interpretability; and a final experiment proves its practical utility by significantly outperforming existing root cause analysis systems.

</details>


### [130] [Meta-Learning and Targeted Differential Privacy to Improve the Accuracy-Privacy Trade-off in Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26390)
*Peter Müllner,Dominik Kowald,Markus Schedl,Elisabeth Lex*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出在数据层面仅对敏感属性相关的高度典型用户数据应用差分隐私（ targeted DP），在模型层面采用元学习提升模型对DP噪声的鲁棒性，从而在隐私保护与推荐精度之间实现更优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）机制中的噪声会显著降低推荐系统的精度，如何在保护用户隐私的同时维持较高的推荐准确率是隐私保护推荐系统的核心挑战。

Method: 方法分为两个层次：①数据层面——仅对可能泄露敏感属性（如性别、年龄）的高度典型用户数据施加DP，而非均匀地对所有数据应用DP，以减少不必要的扰动；②模型层面——采用元学习（meta-learning）策略增强模型对残余DP噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 相比标准DP方法和完全DP基线，所提方案在精度和隐私风险两方面均表现更优：元学习提升了精度，目标性DP降低了经验隐私风险。

Conclusion: 选择性对数据应用DP（数据层面）结合元学习（模型层面）的策略可有效平衡推荐精度与用户隐私，为隐私保护推荐系统的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Balancing differential privacy (DP) with recommendation accuracy is a key challenge in privacy-preserving recommender systems, since DP-noise degrades accuracy. We address this trade-off at both the data and model levels. At the data level, we apply DP only to the most stereotypical user data likely to reveal sensitive attributes, such as gender or age, to reduce unnecessary perturbation; we refer to this as targeted DP. At the model level, we use meta-learning to improve robustness to remaining DP-noise. This achieves a better trade-off between accuracy and privacy than standard approaches: Meta-learning improves accuracy and targeted DP leads to lower empirical privacy risk compared to uniformly applied DP and full DP baselines. Overall, our findings show that selectively applying DP at the data level together with meta-learning at the model level can effectively balance recommendation accuracy and user privacy.

</details>


### [131] [CARD: Non-Uniform Quantization of Visual Semantic Unit for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26427)
*Yibiao Wei,Jie Zou,Pengfei Zhang,Xiao Ao,Weikang Guo,Zeyu Ma,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CARD框架，通过视觉语义单元统一多模态信号并结合非均匀量化框架NU-RQ-VAE，解决生成式推荐中SID质量不足和代码本失衡两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐框架在构建语义ID（SID）时存在两大问题：两阶段范式缺乏异构融合的监督信号，以及非均匀嵌入导致代码本利用不均和生成偏差。

Method: (1) 引入视觉语义单元，将文本、视觉和协同信号统一到结构化视觉表示中，在编码前进行整体语义建模，缩小语义鸿沟；(2) 开发NU-RQ-VAE非均匀量化框架，在量化过程中加入可学习的可逆非均匀变换，将偏斜分布映射到更均衡的潜在空间。

Result: CARD在多个数据集上 consistently 优于基线方法；非均匀变换模块具有即插即用特性，在不同量化方案下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了生成式推荐的SID质量和代码本利用率，为多模态生成推荐提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative recommendation frameworks typically represent items as discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs). While existing studies have sought to enhance SID construction by incorporating multimodal content, collaborative signals, or more advanced quantization techniques, learning high-quality SIDs still faces two key challenges: (1) The two-stage generative recommendation paradigm (SID construction and autoregressive generation) provides insufficient supervision for heterogeneous fusion, which hinders learning high-quality SIDs, and (2) non-uniform embeddings lead to codeword imbalance and generation bias. To address these challenges, we propose a novel generative recommendation framework, called CARD. CARD introduces a visual semantic unit that unifies textual, visual, and collaborative signals into a structured visual representation prior to encoding, enabling holistic semantic modeling and effectively alleviating the semantic gap, thereby reducing the reliance on supervision signals during SID learning. Furthermore, to deal with the highly non-uniform distribution of item semantic embeddings in recommendation scenarios, we develop a non-uniform quantization framework (NU-RQ-VAE), which incorporates a learnable and invertible non-uniform transformation into the quantization process to map skewed semantic distributions into a more balanced latent space, thereby significantly improving codebook utilization and quantization accuracy. Experiments on multiple datasets show that CARD consistently outperforms baseline methods under various settings; meanwhile, the proposed non-uniform transformation module is plug-and-play and remains robust across different quantization schemes. Code is available at https://github.com/HAI-UESTC/CARD.

</details>


### [132] [Efficient Listwise Reranking with Compressed Document Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26483)
*Hervé Déjean,Stéphane Clinchant*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RRK是一种高效的列表式重排序器，通过将文档压缩为多token固定大小嵌入表示来实现高效重排序。8B参数模型比小型重排序器(0.6-4B参数)快3-18倍，且效果相当或更优，尤其在长文档任务上优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重排序过程通常计算成本高昂，尤其是使用大型语言模型时。现有方法如使用更小的LLM或控制输入长度存在局限性。受RAG中文档压缩最新进展的启发，研究者寻求新的效率优化方案。

Method: 提出RRK方法，一种结合文档压缩与列表式重排序的高效系统。核心创新在于将文档压缩为多token固定大小嵌入表示，采用蒸馏方法进行简单训练，实现丰富压缩表示与列表式重排序的结合。

Result: 8B参数模型运行速度比0.6-4B参数的小型重排序器快3-18倍，同时效果相当或更优。在长文档基准测试上，效率优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 丰富的压缩表示与列表式重排序的结合能够构建高效且有效的重排序系统，该方法在性能和效率之间实现了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Reranking, the process of refining the output from a first-stage retriever, is often considered computationally expensive, especially when using Large Language Models (LLMs). A common approach to mitigate this cost involves utilizing smaller LLMs or controlling input length. Inspired by recent advances in document compression for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), we introduce RRK, an efficient and effective listwise reranker compressing documents into multi-token fixed-size embedding representations. Our simple training via distillation shows that this combination of rich compressed representations and listwise reranking yields a highly efficient and effective system. In particular, our 8B-parameter model runs 3x-18x faster than smaller rerankers (0.6-4B parameters) while matching or outperforming them in effectiveness. The efficiency gains are even more striking on long-document benchmarks, where RRK widens its advantage further.

</details>


### [133] [When to Retrieve During Reasoning: Adaptive Retrieval for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26649)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ReaLM-Retrieve框架，通过步骤级不确定性检测、时机感知的检索干预策略和高效集成机制，解决大语言模型推理链与RAG系统的对齐问题，在多跳推理基准上实现SOTA性能，检索效率提升3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1和OpenAI o1）生成数千token的思维链，但其与检索增强生成（RAG）的整合存在根本性错位：当前RAG系统在推理开始前提供上下文进行优化，而推理模型需要在多步推理链中进行证据注入。

Method: 提出ReaLM-Retrieve框架，包含三个核心创新：(1)步骤级不确定性检测器，在推理步骤粒度而非token或句子级别识别知识缺口；(2)检索干预策略，学习何时外部证据能最大程度有益于正在进行的推理过程；(3)效率优化集成机制，将每次检索开销降低3.2倍。

Result: 在MuSiQue、HotpotQA和2WikiMultiHopQA三个基准上，答案F1平均提升10.1个百分点（范围9.0%-11.8%），检索调用减少47%（p<0.01）；在MuSiQue上达到71.2% F1，检索调用仅1.8次；支持证据 Recall@5达81.3%，精确率和MRR均优于固定间隔基线。

Conclusion: ReaLM-Retrieve在推理密集型检索任务上实现了最优的效率-精度权衡，在MuSiQue上仅用1.8次检索调用即可达到71.2% F1，同时保持了更高的召回率、精确率和排序性能。

Abstract: Large reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1 and OpenAI o1 generate extended chains of thought spanning thousands of tokens, yet their integration with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) remains fundamentally misaligned. Current RAG systems optimize for providing context before reasoning begins, while reasoning models require evidence injection during multi-step inference chains. We introduce ReaLM-Retrieve, a reasoning-aware retrieval framework that addresses this mismatch through three key innovations: (1) a step-level uncertainty detector that identifies knowledge gaps at reasoning-step granularity rather than token or sentence level; (2) a retrieval intervention policy that learns when external evidence maximally benefits ongoing reasoning; and (3) an efficiency-optimized integration mechanism that reduces per-retrieval overhead by 3.2x compared to naive integration. Experiments on MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ReaLM-Retrieve achieves on average 10.1% absolute improvement in answer F1 over standard RAG (range: 9.0-11.8% across the three benchmarks) while reducing retrieval calls by 47% compared to fixed-interval approaches like IRCoT (all improvements significant at p<0.01, paired bootstrap). On the challenging MuSiQue benchmark requiring 2-4 hop reasoning, our method achieves 71.2% F1 with an average of only 1.8 retrieval calls per question. Analysis shows that ReaLM-Retrieve also improves retrieval quality itself, achieving 81.3% Recall@5 with consistently higher precision and MRR than fixed-interval baselines on supporting evidence, establishing new state-of-the-art efficiency-accuracy trade-offs for reasoning-intensive retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [134] [The Bandit's Blind Spot: The Critical Role of User State Representation in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26651)
*Pedro R. Pires,Gregorio F. Azevedo,Rafael T. Sereicikas,Pietro L. Campos,Tiago A. Almeida*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper reveals that in contextual bandit-based recommender systems, the choice of embedding-based user state representation significantly impacts performance—often more than algorithm selection. Large-scale experiments demonstrate that no single representation strategy dominates universally, highlighting the need for domain-specific evaluation and holistic system design that prioritizes embedding quality alongside algorithmic innovation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: State representation (user embedding) as a critical yet underexplored component in contextual bandit recommender systems; user state encapsulates interaction history and directly impacts model decisions and learning outcomes.

Method: large-scale experiments comparing various embedding-based state representations derived from matrix factorization models across multiple datasets; ablation study isolating the impact of state representation from algorithm choice.

Result: State representation variations can yield performance improvements exceeding those from changing the bandit algorithm itself; no single embedding or aggregation strategy consistently dominates across different datasets.

Conclusion: state representation quality is equally or more important than algorithm selection; future bandit-based recommender systems require holistic approaches prioritizing embedding quality alongside algorithmic innovation.

Abstract: With the increasing availability of online information, recommender systems have become an important tool for many web-based systems. Due to the continuous aspect of recommendation environments, these systems increasingly rely on contextual multi-armed bandits (CMAB) to deliver personalized and real-time suggestions. A critical yet underexplored component in these systems is the representation of user state, which typically encapsulates the user's interaction history and is deeply correlated with the model's decisions and learning. In this paper, we investigate the impact of different embedding-based state representations derived from matrix factorization models on the performance of traditional CMAB algorithms. Our large-scale experiments reveal that variations in state representation can lead to improvements greater than those achieved by changing the bandit algorithm itself. Furthermore, no single embedding or aggregation strategy consistently dominates across datasets, underscoring the need for domain-specific evaluation. These results expose a substantial gap in the literature and emphasize that advancing bandit-based recommender systems requires a holistic approach that prioritizes embedding quality and state construction alongside algorithmic innovation. The source code for our experiments is publicly available on https://github.com/UFSCar-LaSID/bandits_blind_spot.

</details>


### [135] [Factorized Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26760)
*Tianqi Gao,Chengkai Huang,Zihan Wang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Factorized Latent Reasoning (FLR)框架，通过将LLM推荐中的潜在推理分解为多个解耦的偏好因子，解决现有方法单一潜在向量无法捕捉用户多维偏好的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐方法通常使用单一潜在向量表示用户意图，但用户偏好本质上是多维度的，这种单向量表示难以有效捕捉用户偏好的复杂性。

Method: FLR框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量级多因子注意力模块，迭代细化潜在思维表示，各因子关注用户交互历史的不同方面；(2) 正交性、注意力多样性和稀疏性正则化目标，促进因子多样化与专业化；(3) 基于群体相对策略优化的强化学习方法，在潜在推理空间直接实现稳定对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，FLR一致性地超越强基线方法，同时提升了模型的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过将潜在推理分解为多个解耦因子并结合有效的强化学习对齐策略，FLR为LLM推荐系统提供了一种更精确、更可解释的用户偏好建模方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adopted for recommendation by framing user preference modeling as a language generation problem. However, existing latent reasoning approaches typically represent user intent with a single latent vector, which struggles to capture the inherently multi-faceted nature of user preferences. We propose Factorized Latent Reasoning (FLR), a novel framework for LLM-based sequential recommendation that decomposes latent reasoning into multiple disentangled preference factors. FLR introduces a lightweight multi-factor attention module that iteratively refines a latent thought representation, where each factor attends to distinct aspects of the user's interaction history. To encourage diversity and specialization, we design orthogonality, attention diversity, and sparsity regularization objectives, and dynamically aggregate factor contributions for the final prediction. We further integrate FLR with an efficient reinforcement learning strategy based on group-relative policy optimization, enabling stable alignment directly in the latent reasoning space. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that FLR consistently outperforms strong baselines while improving robustness and interpretability.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Large Language Models for Multilingual Code Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25960)
*Chao Jiang,Dugang Liu,Cheng Wen,Zhiwu Xu,Hua Zheng,Muhammad Sadiq,Jawwad Ahmed Shamsi,Shengchao Qin,Zhong Ming*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这篇论文是一篇关于多语言代码智能的综述，聚焦于代码生成和代码翻译两个任务，涵盖代表性方法、基准测试和评估指标，并讨论了可信跨语言泛化的挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在AI辅助软件工程领域已有重要应用，但研究存在明显的语言偏向性——集中于Python等高资源语言，而对Rust、OCaml等语言的代码生成能力相对薄弱。同时，现实世界的软件系统本质上是多语言的，因此亟需稳健的多语言代码智能解决方案。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统回顾了代表性方法、基准测试和评估指标，提供了该领域研究现状的全面视角。

Result: 该综述全面梳理了多语言代码生成和代码翻译两个核心任务的研究现状，涵盖了方法、基准测试和评估指标等方面的代表性成果。

Conclusion: 强调了可信跨语言泛化面临的挑战，并指出了未来的研究机遇，为该领域的后续发展提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models have transformed AI-assisted software engineering, but current research remains biased toward high-resource languages such as Python, with weaker performance in languages like Rust and OCaml. Since real-world systems are inherently polyglot, robust multilingual code intelligence is crucial. This survey focuses on two key tasks: multilingual code generation from shared natural-language requirements, and multilingual code translation that preserves semantics across languages. It reviews representative methods, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics, and highlights challenges and opportunities for trustworthy cross-language generalization.

</details>


### [137] [LLM-Guided Issue Generation from Uncovered Code Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26118)
*Diany Pressato,Honghao Tan,Mariam Elmoazen,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IssueSpecter是一款自动化bug发现工具，通过结合覆盖率分析与LLM缺陷识别生成优先级排序的结构化问题报告。实验表明84.6%的报告有效，LLM排序显著优于规则排序，且在相同条件下优于现有SOTA工具CoverUp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者被大量AI生成的问题报告所淹没，这些报告缺乏可操作性和可复现性，导致对自动化bug检测工具的信任度下降。现有工具难以生成可直接操作的问题报告，开发者需要额外精力解读测试意图。

Method: IssueSpecter采用覆盖率分析结合LLM缺陷识别的技术路线。具体包括：1) 对未覆盖代码段进行覆盖率分析定位潜在缺陷；2) 利用LLM进行缺陷识别并生成结构化报告；3) 使用LLM-based ranking进行问题优先级排序。

Result: 实验评估表明：在13个活跃维护的Python项目中生成10,467份问题报告；手动标注top-130报告显示84.6%有效（仅15.4%误报）；LLM排序比规则排序在P@3上提升50%，MRR提升41%；相比CoverUp，IssueSpecter的bug有效率更高（81.0% vs 76.2%）。

Conclusion: IssueSpecter通过结合覆盖率分析与LLM缺陷识别，有效解决了AI生成问题报告缺乏可操作性的痛点。在13个Python项目中验证了其实用价值，84.6%的问题报告有效，且在相同条件下优于现有SOTA工具CoverUp。该工具能够生成结构化报告（包含严重程度评级、重现步骤和修复建议），帮助开发者优先处理高影响力bug。

Abstract: Developers are increasingly overwhelmed by AI-generated issue reports that lack actionability and reproducibility, eroding trust in automated bug detection tools. In this paper, we present IssueSpecter, an automated tool that finds bugs in uncovered code segments and automatically generates prioritized, actionable issue reports. IssueSpecter combines coverage analysis with LLM-based defect identification, producing structured reports complete with severity ratings, reproduction steps, and suggested fixes. We evaluate IssueSpecter on 13 actively maintained Python projects, generating 10,467 issue reports. Manual annotation of the top-130 ranked issues by IssueSpecter confirms that 84.6% of the LLM-generated issues are valid or warrant further investigation, with only 15.4% false positives. LLM-based ranking outperforms rule-based ranking by 50% at P@3 and 41% in MRR. The identified bugs cover a wide variety of types, from logic and boundary errors to security vulnerabilities and state consistency bugs. By ranking issues by priority, IssueSpecter aims to help developers focus their attention on the most impactful bugs first. Finally, we validate IssueSpecter through case studies reproducing real bugs surfaced from its generated issue reports, demonstrating its practical value for automatic bug discovery in open-source Python projects. Compared against CoverUp, a state-of-the-art coverage-driven test generation tool, IssueSpecter achieves a higher bug validity rate (81.0% vs. 76.2%) under identical evaluation conditions, using the same model and the same number of evaluated artifacts per project, while additionally providing structured issue reports with reproduction steps and candidate fixes that are immediately actionable without requiring developers to interpret generated test intent.

</details>


### [138] [ImproBR: Bug Report Improver Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26142)
*Emre Furkan Akyol,Mehmet Dedeler,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ImproBR是一个基于LLM的自动化bug报告检测与改进系统，通过混合检测器（微调DistilBERT+启发式分析+LLM）和RAG管道（基于Minecraft Wiki知识）来识别并修复缺失的S2R、OB、EB信息，在Mojira数据集上实现了报告质量的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bug跟踪系统中用户提交的报告质量普遍较低，缺少关键的Steps to Reproduce (S2R)、Observed Behavior (OB)和Expected Behavior (EB)信息，导致开发人员难以有效处理和复现问题。

Method: 采用混合检测器架构，融合微调DistilBERT、启发式分析和LLM分析器；使用GPT-4o mini配合section-specific few-shot prompts；引入基于Minecraft Wiki领域知识的RAG管道进行增强。

Result: 在139个真实挑战性报告上，结构完整性从7.9%提升至96.4%；可执行S2R比例从28.8%提升至67.6%；完全可复现报告从1个增加至13个。

Conclusion: ImproBR通过混合检测器与大语言模型的结合，显著提升了bug报告的质量和可执行性，特别是在Minecraft这类复杂软件生态中具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Bug tracking systems play a crucial role in software maintenance, yet developers frequently struggle with low-quality user-submitted reports that omit essential details such as Steps to Reproduce (S2R), Observed Behavior (OB), and Expected Behavior (EB). We propose ImproBR, an LLM-based pipeline that automatically detects and improves bug reports by addressing missing, incomplete, and ambiguous S2R, OB, and EB sections. ImproBR employs a hybrid detector combining fine-tuned DistilBERT, heuristic analysis, and an LLM analyzer, guided by GPT-4o mini with section-specific few-shot prompts and a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in Minecraft Wiki domain knowledge. Evaluated on Mojira, ImproBR improved structural completeness from 7.9% to 96.4%, more than doubled the proportion of executable S2R from 28.8% to 67.6%, and raised fully reproducible bug reports from 1 to 13 across 139 challenging real-world reports.

</details>


### [139] [AI Observability for Large Language Model Systems: A Multi-Layer Analysis of Monitoring Approaches from Confidence Calibration to Infrastructure Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26152)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文系统性分析了2025-2026年五项AI可观测性研究（置信校准、内部状态监控、思维链可监测性、云端自动化运维基准、非侵入式推理追踪），提出五层可观测性分类体系。核心发现：各监控层已快速成熟，但模型级置信信号与基础设施级异常的整合——即统一运营智能——仍是领域核心开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生产部署需要覆盖全栈的可观测性系统，但现有方案仅针对独立层次，缺乏系统性分析。

Method: 将5项研究归入五层可观测性分类体系，进行统一比较，识别未解决问题。

Result: 构建了五层可观测性分类体系，整合关键发现，识别出四个关键未解决问题。

Conclusion: 整合挑战是领域核心问题——将模型级置信信号与基础设施级异常连接为统一运营智能仍有待突破。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) in production environments has created an urgent need for observability systems that span the full stack -- from model internals to GPU kernels. Yet existing monitoring approaches address isolated layers of this stack, and no comprehensive analysis has examined how these techniques relate, overlap, or complement each other. This paper presents a structured analysis of five recent research contributions (2025-2026) that collectively define the emerging landscape of AI observability: confidence calibration via reinforcement learning (MIT), internal state monitoring through propositional probes (UC Berkeley), chain-of-thought monitorability evaluation (OpenAI), autonomous cloud operations benchmarking (Microsoft Research, UC Berkeley, UIUC), and non-intrusive inference-level tracing (TRUFFLD). We organize these contributions into a five-layer observability taxonomy, synthesize their key findings into a unified comparison, and identify four critical gaps that remain unaddressed. We further contextualize these research directions against practical operational observability systems that translate infrastructure telemetry into actionable insights for site reliability teams. Our analysis reveals that while individual monitoring layers have matured rapidly, the integration challenge -- connecting model-level confidence signals with infrastructure-level anomalies into coherent operational intelligence -- remains the defining open problem for the field.

</details>


### [140] [LLM-Assisted Empirical Software Engineering: Systematic Literature Review and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26192)
*Victoria Gomes,Delaney Selb,Fabio Palomba,Rodrigo Spinola,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统性文献综述（2020-2025年，12个软件工程顶会，50篇主要论文），分析了LLM在实证软件工程中的应用。识别出69个LLM辅助任务，主要集中于数据处理和分析阶段，以自动化为导向，但存在幻觉、不一致性、提示敏感性等问题，报告实践也不完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证软件工程面临数据规模扩大、方法论复杂性和可重复性挑战，LLM作为支持工具的潜力日益显现，但应用分散，缺乏系统性综合指导。

Method: 系统性文献综述（Systematic Literature Review），时间范围2020-2025年，涵盖12个软件工程顶会，采用定性与定量综合分析，最终纳入50篇主要研究。

Result: 识别出69种LLM辅助任务，主要应用于软件仓库挖掘和对照实验，聚焦分类、过滤和评估；LLM在各阶段均有使用但集中于数据处理和分析；集成方式以自动化为主，决策支持功能有限；优势包括效率和可扩展性；局限包括幻觉、不一致性、提示敏感性和可重复性问题；报告实践常不完整。

Conclusion: LLM在ESE中的应用增长迅速，但以自动化驱动为主，在以人为中心的集成和透明度方面存在明显差距；研究提出了负责任使用的启示和研究议程。

Abstract: Context: Empirical Software Engineering (ESE) faces increasing challenges due to data scale, methodological complexity, and reproducibility concerns. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as promising tools to support empirical workflows, yet their use remains fragmented, with no comprehensive synthesis to guide responsible adoption.
  Aims: This study analyzes how LLMs are used in ESE, examining supported tasks, phases of the empirical lifecycle, integration into workflows, reported benefits and limitations, and the extent of reproducibility-related reporting. It also identifies gaps and future research directions.
  Method: We conducted a systematic literature review of peer-reviewed papers (2020-2025) across 12 leading software engineering venues, resulting in 50 primary studies analyzed through qualitative and quantitative synthesis.
  Results: We identified 69 LLM-assisted tasks, mainly in mining software repositories and controlled experiments, focusing on classification, filtering, and evaluation. LLMs are used across multiple phases but are concentrated in data processing and analysis. Their integration is largely automation-oriented, with limited decision-support use. Benefits emphasize efficiency and scalability, while limitations include hallucinations, inconsistency, prompt sensitivity, and reproducibility issues. Reporting practices are often incomplete.
  Conclusion: LLM use in ESE is growing but remains automation-driven, with gaps in human-centered integration and transparency. We outline implications and research agenda for responsible use.

</details>


### [141] [Agentic AI in the Software Development Lifecycle: Architecture, Empirical Evidence, and the Reshaping of Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26275)
*Happy Bhati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综合分析了AI Agent在软件工程领域的范式转变，提出了六层参考架构，对比传统SDLC与Agentic SDLC，并基于SWE-bench等基准测试数据论证了AI Agent的性能提升与经济影响，同时指出了五大开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在多步推理、工具使用和长时规划能力上的突破，软件工程领域正在经历从代码补全工具（如GitHub Copilot）到智能体系统（如Claude Code、Devin等）的根本性转变，现有研究缺乏对这一转变的系统性梳理与理论框架。

Method: 综合分析来自Anthropic、OpenAI、Google DeepMind、Microsoft Research、Princeton、Stanford等机构的研究成果；提出六层参考架构描述Agentic软件工程系统；对比传统SDLC与A-SDLC；汇总SWE-bench Verified等基准测试与实证研究数据。

Result: 实证数据显示SWE-bench Verified准确率从2023年10月的1.96%提升至2026年4月的78.4%；对照研究显示可节省13.6%-55.8%的时间；Anthropic 2026年调研显示49%的岗位中AI完成了至少四分之一的任务。

Conclusion: 核心研究对象已从代码生成转向人类监督下的委托执行；开放问题包括评估体系、治理机制、技术债务、技能再分配和注意力经济学；Agentic转型是否对软件工程学科产生净正向效益取决于这五个问题的解决。

Abstract: The arrival of large language models (LLMs) capable of multi-step reasoning, tool use, and long-horizon planning has produced a qualitative shift in software engineering. Where earlier code-completion tools such as GitHub Copilot operated at the granularity of a line or function, modern agentic systems -- Claude Code, OpenAI Codex CLI, Google Jules, Devin, OpenHands, SWE-agent, MetaGPT, ChatDev, and DeepMind's AlphaEvolve -- operate at the granularity of a repository, a feature, or an algorithm. We synthesize work from Anthropic, OpenAI, Google DeepMind, Microsoft Research, Princeton, Stanford, and the broader academic community to characterize this transition. We propose a six-layer reference architecture for agentic software engineering systems, contrast a traditional Software Development Lifecycle (SDLC) with an emerging Agentic SDLC (A-SDLC), and consolidate empirical evidence on performance (a rise from 1.96% to 78.4% on SWE-bench Verified between October 2023 and April 2026), productivity (13.6%-55.8% time savings across controlled studies), and labor-market impact (49% of jobs sampled by Anthropic in 2026 saw AI used for at least a quarter of their tasks). We argue that the central object of inquiry has shifted from code generation to delegated execution under human supervision, and we identify five open problems -- evaluation, governance, technical debt, skill redistribution, and the economics of attention -- that will determine whether the agentic transition is net-positive for the discipline.

</details>


### [142] [Asset Administration Shell-Based OCL Validation Framework for Model-Based System Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26384)
*Om Parkash,Jannik Bauer,Vincent Schmitt,Thomas Greiner,Rainer Drath*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过资产管理外壳（Asset Administration Shells）管理OCL约束及其验证结果的方法，解决MBSE模型与OCL约束在不同工具中分离管理导致的效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业系统复杂性增加，对自动化和互操作性的需求要求MBSE中模型具有一致性和语义验证。OCL支持形式化约束定义，但MBSE模型和OCL约束通常在独立工具中管理，导致模型约束应用和结果解释需要大量人工工作。

Method: 提出一种通过资产管理外壳（Asset Administration Shells）管理OCL约束及其验证结果的方法，并通过虚构的工业场景进行演示。

Result: 成功展示了所提出的方法可行，所有 artifacts 在 GitHub 仓库中公开以支持复现。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了 MBSE 建模工具与 OCL 约束管理之间的鸿沟，为企业系统的互操作性和自动化验证提供了新途径。

Abstract: Increasing complexity of modern enterprise systems and the demand for automation and interoperability require consistent and semantically validated models in Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE). The Object Constraint Language (OCL) supports formal definition of such constraint validations. However, MBSE models and OCL constraints are typically managed in separate tools, causing manual effort during model constraint application and result interpretation. To address this gap, this paper proposes an approach to managing OCL constraints and their validation results through Asset Administration Shells (a well-established technology for interoperability in enterprise systems). The methodology is demonstrated through a fictional industrial scenario, and to support reproducibility, all artifacts are publicly available in a GitHub repository.

</details>


### [143] [Towards Intelligent Computation Offloading in Dynamic Vehicular Networks: A Scalable Multilayer Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26416)
*Falk Dettinger,Matthias Weiß,Baran Can Gül,Sruthi Mangala Suresh,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于软件定义车辆(SDV)的四层计算卸载框架，通过增强型粒子群优化算法(PSO)实现云/边资源的动态分配，在满足严格时延约束的同时降低平均响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义车辆面临日益严重的计算能力缺口问题：先进算法和频繁软件更新需要更多算力支持，而车载硬件在车辆10年以上的使用周期内保持静态。这一矛盾威胁到高级驾驶辅助系统(ADAS)和实时感知等安全关键功能的性能。

Method: 提出四层计算卸载流水线，动态分配车载功能至云端和边缘资源；设计增强型粒子群优化算法，融合距离惩罚、方向惩罚和功能需求约束，优化移动车辆的边缘服务器选择；基于Kubernetes云基础设施和真实车辆移动轨迹进行评估。

Result: 相比传统暴力搜索方法，该方案降低平均响应时间，同时保持时延敏感任务的成功率。改进的PSO算法在CPU上对10个服务器和任务平均执行时间为26ms；在GPU上对15个服务器和1000个任务平均执行时间为550ms。

Conclusion: 该计算卸载流水线有效弥合了下一代软件定义车辆的计算能力缺口，验证了边缘计算与智能优化算法结合在车载场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Software Defined Vehicles face an increasing computational gap as advanced algorithms and frequent software updates demand more processing power while onboard hardware remains static throughout a vehicle's 10+ year lifespan. This mismatch threatens the performance of safety-critical functions including advanced driver-assistance systems and real-time perception tasks. We propose a novel four-layer computation offloading pipeline that dynamically distributes vehicular functions to cloud and edge resources while meeting strict Round Trip Time constraints. Our key contribution is an enhanced Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm that integrates distance- and direction-based penalties with functional requirements to optimize edge server selection for mobile vehicles.
