Evaluation using a Kubernetes-based cloud infrastructure with realistic vehicular mobility patterns demonstrates that our approach reduces average response time compared to conventional Brute-Force methods while maintaining the success rate for latency-critical tasks. The modified Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm achieves an average execution time of 26 ms across ten servers and tasks on Central Processing Unit, and 550ms across 15 servers with 1000 tasks on Graphics Processing Unit. These results confirm the pipeline's effectiveness in bridging the computational gap for next-generation Software Defined Vehicles (SDV).

</details>


### [144] [An Empirical Study of Speculative Decoding on Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26469)
*Yijia Li,Junkai Chen,Xing Hu,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个系统性评估推测解码在软件工程任务中有效性的研究，通过全面基准测试发现小模型加速更显著，不同SD方法适用于不同任务场景，SE任务的特性使其更适合激进优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程任务中应用广泛，但自回归推理的高延迟成为交互式部署的主要瓶颈，而推测解码在长上下文仓库级任务和复杂agent交互中的应用研究不足。

Method: 对多种SD策略（基于模型和无模型方法）进行系统基准测试，覆盖代码生成、编辑和修复等代表性场景。

Result: SD对小模型的加速效果优于大模型；基于模型的方法适合代码生成，无模型方法更适合仓库级修复和编辑；SE任务的重复性提升了无模型方法性能；SE任务可预测性高，支持更激进的超参数设置。

Conclusion: 该研究为软件工程场景下LLM推理加速提供了系统性指南，证明SD技术可有效降低延迟，且不同方法适用于不同任务类型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become widely used for Software Engineering (SE) tasks, spanning from function-level code generation to complex repository-level workflows. However, the high latency of autoregressive inference remains a significant bottleneck, hindering their deployment in interactive environments. While Speculative Decoding (SD) offers a promising technique for lossless acceleration, prior research on long-context repository-level tasks and complex agentic interactions remains limited. To bridge this gap, we present the first systematic empirical study to evaluate the effectiveness of SD in SE tasks. We systematically benchmark a comprehensive spectrum of strategies, encompassing both model-based and model-free methods, across representative generation, editing, and repair scenarios. Our empirical results indicate that SD demonstrates clear potential for accelerating inference, particularly for smaller models that achieve higher speedups than those of their larger counterparts. We find that the effectiveness of SD methods varies across different task scenarios. Model-based approaches are well-suited for code generation, whereas model-free methods are better adapted to repository-level repair and editing scenarios. Furthermore, we observe that the repetitiveness of SE tasks improves the performance of model-free methods. In contrast to natural language tasks, the higher predictability of SE tasks allows for more aggressive hyperparameters. Our findings are summarized as guidelines to help increase inference efficiency for SE scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [Cognitive Atrophy and Systemic Collapse in AI-Dependent Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26855)
*Frank Ginac*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM在软件开发中引发的"认知系统性崩溃"风险，提出"认识论债务"概念——工程师以被动AI验证替代主动逻辑推导所付出的隐性代价，指出递归训练于合成代码将导致全球软件资产同质化，并以2026年亚马逊宕机事件为案例，呼吁建立人机协作的教育框架以维护工程韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The uncritical integration of LLMs into SDLC creates hidden socio-technical risks: engineers lose foundational logical derivation skills (Epistemological Debt), mental models for root-cause analysis erode, and recursive training on synthetic code threatens software diversity and resilience.

Method: Case study analysis of 2026 Amazon outages as an illustrative example of "mechanized convergence"; framework proposal for human-in-the-loop pedagogical standards.

Result: The paper illustrates how mechanized convergence leads to systemic fragility, widening the gap between system complexity and human comprehension.

Conclusion: Engineering leaders must adopt rigorous human-in-the-loop pedagogical standards that balance AI-driven productivity with epistemic sovereignty, moving beyond prompt-based development to preserve long-term resilience in increasingly opaque software ecosystems.

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into the software development lifecycle (SDLC) masks a critical socio-technical failure: Cognitive-Systemic Collapse. This paper introduces "Epistemological Debt," the hidden carrying cost incurred when engineers substitute logical derivation with passive AI verification. This debt erodes the mental models essential for root-cause analysis, widening the gap between system complexity and human comprehension. Furthermore, recursive training on synthetic code threatens to homogenize the global software reservoir, diminishing the variance required for robust engineering. Using the 2026 Amazon outages as a case study, this research illustrates how "mechanized convergence" leads to systemic fragility. To preserve long-term resilience, engineering leaders must move beyond prompt-based development to implement rigorous human-in-the-loop pedagogical standards. This framework balances AI-driven productivity with the epistemic sovereignty necessary to manage increasingly opaque software ecosystems.

</details>


### [146] [Identifying and Characterizing Semantic Clones of Solidity Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26526)
*Ermanno Francesco Sannini,Francesco Salzano,Simone Scalabrino,Rocco Oliveto,Remo Pareschi,Corrado Aaron Visaggio,Andrea Di Sorbo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Smart Contracts are essential blockchain components, mainly written in Solidity. The high availability of public Solidity code leads to frequent reuse and high clone ratios. Since cloning can propagate vulnerabilities and flaws, effective detection is crucial. Although existing techniques work well in detecting syntactic clones, the identification of semantic clones is an open problem. To address this challenge, in this paper, we present and empirically assess a scalable methodology, based on analyzing code and comments, to spot semantically equivalent Solidity functions. We first collected an up-to-date dataset of about 300,000 Ethereum smart contracts, 82.07% of which are compliant with modern Solidity version 0.8. Manual validation of a statistically significant sample comprising 1,155 function pairs confirms the effectiveness of our solution, achieving an overall precision of 59% (rising to 84% for homonymous functions) and a recall of 97%. Besides, we explore the structural differences occurring on semantically equivalent Solidity functions, demonstrating that they often represent design alternatives focused on security choices, modularization, and gas optimization. Finally, we investigate the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as documentation engines in scenarios where code comments are poor or absent. Our results show that LLM-generated summaries, combined with sentence transformers like BERT, can bridge the documentation gap, enabling the identification of semantic clones in uncommented code with 75% precision. This work establishes a modern benchmark for Solidity clone detection and provides a foundation for the automated discovery of secure and efficient code alternatives.

</details>


### [147] [Graph Construction and Matching for Imperative Programs using Neural and Structural Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26578)
*Arshad Beg,Diarmuid O'Donoghue,Rosemary Monahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种将命令式程序及其规范注释转换为类型化属性图的流水线，通过整合AST解析与语义嵌入模型(SentenceTransformer、CodeBERT)，实现跨编程语言和注释风格的一致图表示构建，为验证制品复用奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证制品的重用需要识别程序及其规范之间的结构和语义相似性，而图构建是实现这一目标的基础步骤。

Method: 1. 使用AST解析提取程序结构；2. 集成SentenceTransformer和CodeBERT生成语义嵌入；3. 统一构建跨语言（ACSL、JML、Dafny）的类型化属性图。

Result: 验证了可跨不同编程语言和注释风格构建一致的图表示，涵盖C/ACSL、Java/JML和Dafny for C#数据集。

Conclusion: 该工作为后续语义 enrichment 和近似图匹配提供了实用基础，可支持大规模验证制品复用。

Abstract: Reusing verification artefacts requires identifying structural and semantic similarities across programs and their specifications. In this paper, we focus on graph construction as a foundational step toward this goal. We present a pipeline that converts imperative programs and their annotations into typed, attributed graphs. Our experiments cover datasets including C with ACSL, Java with JML, and Dafny for C\#. The pipeline integrates abstract syntax tree parsing with semantic embeddings derived from models such as SentenceTransformer and CodeBERT. This enables the generation of graph representations that capture both structural relationships and semantic context. Our results show that consistent graph representations can be constructed across different languages and annotation styles. This work provides a practical basis for future steps in semantic enrichment and approximate graph matching for scalable verification artefact reuse.

</details>


### [148] [Recommendations for Efficient and Responsible LLM Adoption within Industrial Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26590)
*Krishna Ronanki,Beatriz Cabrero-Daniel,Tomas Herda,Stefan Sitkovich,Jennifer Horkoff,Christian Berger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过多案例研究和在线调查，开发了七项可操作的建议，用于在工业软件环境中高效且负责任地采用大型语言模型（LLM）。调查结果表明参与者对这些建议的感知相关性有高度共识，并提出将建议与欧盟AI法案原则进行映射的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）在软件工程（SE）活动中展现出显著的正向影响，其在行业中的采用率迅速增长。然而，目前缺乏针对工业软件环境中高效且负责任地采用LLM的可操作最佳实践指南。

Method: 采用多案例研究方法，对三家在软件工程活动中使用LLM的组织进行研究，通过定性主题分析综合出七项建议。同时进行了一项补充性在线调查，邀请来自不同行业的软件从业人员评估这些建议的感知相关性。

Result: 研究结果和建议聚焦于六个核心领域：（i）用户偏好将LLM作为AI助手使用；（ii）相关利益相关者满意度在LLM输出评估中的重要性；（iii）界定LLM在软件工程任务中的适用性范围；（iv）LLM对软件工程工作流程的影响；（v）建立人工监督机制的必要性和方向；（vi）从业人员利用LLM所需的技能。在线调查表明参与者对这些建议的感知相关性有高度共识。

Conclusion: 本文概述了未来研究方向，包括将七项建议与欧盟AI法案（AIA）原则进行映射，以审视它们与当前监管合规框架的关系，为LLM的合规应用提供理论支撑。

Abstract: Context: Large language models (LLMs) are observed to have a significant positive impact on various software engineering (SE) activities. With improved accessibility, the adoption of powerful LLMs in industry has surged recently. However, there is a lack of actionable best practices for the efficient and responsible adoption of LLMs within industrial software settings. Objectives: We developed seven actionable recommendations to address this research gap. Methods: We conducted a multi-case study with three organisations that use LLMs within their SE activities and synthesised seven recommendations through qualitative thematic analysis. We conducted a complementary online survey with software practitioners from various industries to evaluate the perceived relevance of our recommendations. Results: Our results and recommendations focus on (i) users' preference to use LLMs as AI assistants, (ii) the importance of relevant stakeholders' satisfaction in the LLM-output evaluation, (iii) scoping the applicability of LLMs within SE tasks, (iv) the effect of LLMs on SE workflows, (v) the necessity and directions for developing human oversight mechanisms, and (vi) the necessary skills for practitioners for leveraging LLMs within SE. The online survey indicates a high level of agreement from the participants regarding the perceived relevance of the recommendations. Conclusion: We outline future research directions, including mapping the seven recommendations to the principles of the EU AI Act (AIA) in order to examine how they relate to the current regulatory compliance frameworks.

</details>


### [149] [TDD Governance for Multi-Agent Code Generation via Prompt Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26615)
*Tarlan Hasanli,Shahbaz Siddeeq,Bishwash Khanal,Pyry Kotilainen,Tommi Mikkonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个AI原生的测试驱动开发(TDD)框架，将经典TDD原则形式化为机器可读的宣言，通过分层架构在规划、生成、修复和验证阶段实施结构化治理机制，包括相位排序、有界修复循环、验证门控和原子变异控制，以解决LLM在软件开发中的不稳定性、非确定性和缺乏开发纪律的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models (LLMs) accelerate software development but exhibit instability, non-determinism, and weak adherence to development discipline in unconstrained workflows. While test-driven development (TDD) provides structured Red-Green-Refactor process, existing LLM-based approaches only use tests as auxiliary inputs rather than enforceable process constraints.

Method: The authors propose an AI-native TDD framework that formalizes classical TDD principles in a machine-readable manifesto, distributed across planning, generation, repair, and validation stages in a layered architecture. The system separates model proposal from deterministic engine authority and enforces governance mechanisms including phase ordering, bounded repair loops, validation gates, and atomic mutation control.

Result: By operationalizing TDD principles as structured prompt-level and workflow-level governance mechanisms, the system aims to improve stability and reproducibility in LLM-assisted development.

Conclusion: The proposed framework offers a promising direction for reliable LLM-assisted development by encoding software engineering discipline directly into prompt orchestration.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) accelerate software development but often exhibit instability, non-determinism, and weak adherence to development discipline in unconstrained workflows. While test-driven development (TDD) provides a structured Red-Green-Refactor process, existing LLM-based approaches typically use tests as auxiliary inputs rather than enforceable process constraints. We present an AI-native TDD framework that operationalizes classical TDD principles as structured prompt-level and workflow-level governance mechanisms. Extracted principles are formalized in a machine-readable manifesto and distributed across planning, generation, repair, and validation stages within a layered architecture that separates model proposal from deterministic engine authority. The system enforces phase ordering, bounded repair loops, validation gates, and atomic mutation control to improve stability and reproducibility. We describe architecture and discuss encoding software engineering discipline directly into prompt orchestration, which we think offers a promising direction for reliable LLM-assisted development.

</details>


### [150] [Understanding the Skills Gap between Higher Education Institutions and the Software Engineering Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26655)
*Huy Phan,Ievgeniia Kuzminykh,Bogdan Ghita*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过网页抓取和模糊匹配文本分析工具，分析了英国30所大学的课程与300份软件工程职位描述，揭示了高校课程与行业需求之间的技能差距。课程偏重编程语言和数据库管理，而行业最需求的是软件设计与规划能力。课程中系统结构和软件领域内容不足，数据库管理和编译器设计可能过度强调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域快速发展，毕业生所需技能不断变化，多项研究表明高校课程与行业需求之间存在技能差距，需要明确具体的差距所在以便高校调整培养方案。

Method: 开发自定义网页抓取和文本分析工具，采用模糊匹配算法，从300份职位描述和30所英国大学本科课程中提取并分类技能，将两者进行映射对比分析。

Result: 课程强调编程语言（18%）和数据库管理（12.83%）；行业最需软件设计与规划（88.68%），通用编程语言（78.30%）和系统结构（66.04%）需求也高。系统结构和软件领域在课程中严重不足，数据库管理和编译器设计可能过度强调。

Conclusion: 高校应调整课程设置以更好匹配行业需求，重点加强软件设计与规划、系统结构和软件领域相关内容的教学，为毕业生进入动态变化的软件工程职业做好准备。

Abstract: In the rapidly evolving field of software engineering, the skills required of graduates entering the job market are constantly changing. Several studies have identified a gap between the skills taught in university curricula and those demanded by the software engineering industry. This chapter investigates the technical skill and expertise gap between higher education institutions (HEIs) and the UK software engineering industry by mapping job descriptions to the skills included in computer science degree programmes. A custom web scraping and text analysis tool, utilising fuzzy matching, was developed to extract and categorise skills from 300 job postings and undergraduate curricula from 30 UK universities. The analysis showed that the curricula place a strong emphasis on Programming Languages (18%) and Database Management (12.83%). In contrast, the industry s most frequently requested skill category is Software Design and Planning, which appears in approximately 88.68% of job descriptions, highlighting its critical importance. General Programming Language and System Structures also show strong demand, present in over 78.30% and 66.04% of postings, respectively. The mapping indicates that areas such as System Structures and Software Domains are significantly underrepresented in curricula, while Database Management and Compiler Design may be overemphasised. These insights can support HEIs in aligning their programmes with industry needs, supporting the preparation of graduates for dynamic careers in software engineering.

</details>


### [151] [Will It Break in Production? Metric-Driven Prediction of Residual Defects in Python Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26667)
*Giuseppe De Rosa,Pietro Liguori*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索了Python后发布故障预测，对比了基于LLM、无监督学习和监督度量学习的多种方法。结果表明，监督机器学习模型(尤其是集成方法)在预测残余故障方面表现最优，过程度量指标是最关键的预测因素，而代码嵌入与度量指标在特征空间中互补而非冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的动态特性增加了测试难度，可能导致部分缺陷逃逸检测，因此有效的故障预测对保障软件质量至关重要。

Method: 构建了超过4000个标记故障的平衡数据集，提取83个产品、过程、统计及Python特有度量指标，结合归一化代码表示，采用跨项目实验设计，评估LLM、无监督模型、监督度量模型(RandomForest、XGBoost、CatBoost)及PCA分析。

Result: 监督度量模型(RandomForest、XGBoost、CatBoost)表现显著优于LLM和无监督模型，召回率达0.85-0.9，假阴性降低一个数量级；过程度量(年龄、变更频率、开发者活动)及类和文件大小指标最具预测力；PCA显示度量指标与代码嵌入在表示空间中占据不同区域，二者捕获互补信息。

Conclusion: 监督学习方法在Python后发布故障预测中显著优于无监督和LLM方法，可将假阴性降低一个数量级；过程度量指标和代码嵌入在表示空间中占据互补区域，共同构成有效的预测能力。

Abstract: Python's dynamic nature complicates testing and increases the possibility that some defects evade detection, so an effective fault prediction becomes essential. We examine whether post-release faults can be predicted using modern ML and DL. Using a balanced dataset of over 4,000 labeled faults with 83 product, process, statistical, and Python-specific metrics plus normalized code representations, we conduct cross-project experiments. LLMs and unsupervised models fail to distinguish residual from non-residual faults, while supervised metric-based models (RandomForest, XGBoost, CatBoost) perform far better, yielding a 0.85-0.9 recall and cutting false negatives by an order of magnitude. Process metrics, especially age, churn, and developer-activity, alongside class and file size, consistently prove most predictive. Notably, the Principal Component Analysis shows that metrics and code embeddings occupy distinct regions of the representation space, suggesting that they capture complementary rather than redundant information.

</details>


### [152] [Which Types of Heterogeneity Matter for Root Cause Localization in Microservice Systems ?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26670)
*Runzhou Wang,Shenglin Zhang,Wenwei Gu,Yongxin Zhao,Chenyu Zhao,Dan Pei,Yuxuan Chen,Yangyuxin Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对微服务根因定位中的异质性挑战，提出NexusRCL框架。该框架通过异构图建模服务和主机的不同节点类型，结合事件抽象机制和主动学习，有效捕获数据级和实体级的异质性。实验结果表明其在工业数据集上大幅领先现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生系统中存在固有的异质性（ diverse observability data和multiple system entities），但现有方法仅关注异质性的单一方面，无法充分利用完整的诊断价值。需要深入理解实体级别的差异和非对称依赖如何影响故障行为。

Method: 1) 对两个微服务基准进行实证分析，发现实体级异质性导致非对称的故障传播，跨层交互（服务与主机之间）占主导；2) 提出NexusRCL半监督框架，将服务和主机建模为异构图中的不同节点类型，捕获跨层依赖关系；3) 设计基于事件的抽象机制以捕捉数据级异质性；4) 采用主动学习最小化标注成本。

Result: 在两个工业基准数据集上，NexusRCL相比现有最优方法实现了显著性能提升：Top-1准确率(A@1)提升最高达49.85%，平均Top-5准确率(A@5)提升最高达32.70%。

Conclusion: NexusRCL通过将服务和主机建模为异构图中的不同节点类型，并结合基于事件的抽象机制，有效捕获了数据级和实体级的异质性。主动学习策略显著降低了标注成本。在两个工业基准数据集上的评估表明，该方法在根因定位任务上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Microservice root cause localization is fundamentally challenged by the inherent heterogeneity of cloud-native systems, which encompasses diverse observability data and multiple system entities. Existing approaches typically focus on only one aspect of heterogeneity and thus fail to capture its full diagnostic value. In this work, we systematically examine the multifaceted role of heterogeneity within both microservice systems and the RCL process. This analysis motivates a deeper investigation into how entity-level distinctions and their asymmetric dependencies influence fault behavior. Our empirical analysis of two microservice benchmarks reveals that entity-level heterogeneity naturally gives rise to heterogeneous fault propagation, which is highly asymmetric and dominated by cross-layer interactions between services and hosts. In light of this, we propose NexusRCL, a semi-supervised framework that internalizes these propagation patterns by formalizing services and hosts as distinct node types within a heterogeneous graph. This design, coupled with an event-based abstraction mechanism, allows NexusRCL to effectively capture both data level and entity-level heterogeneity while minimizing labeling costs through active learning. Comprehensive evaluations on two industrial benchmark datasets demonstrate NexusRCL's superior performance, achieving improvements of up to 49.85\% in Top-1 accuracy (A@1) and 32.70\% in Average Top-5 accuracy (A@5) compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [153] [What Makes Software Bugs Escape Testing? Evidence from a Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26672)
*Domenico Cotroneo,Giuseppe De Rosa,Cristina Improta,Benedetta Gaia Varriale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对超过14000个缺陷进行大规模实证分析，发现后发布缺陷与代码演化历史密切相关，集中在成熟代码区域，建议可靠性工程应针对此类区域加强测试资源配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究主要关注发布前的缺陷，而对于残留在测试阶段逃逸并在发布后暴露的残余缺陷的本质，目前认识不足。

Method: 基于超过14000个从开源项目中挖掘的缺陷，对C/C++和Java系统进行大规模特征分析；采用多维度软件度量指标（复杂度、规模、结构、开发历史等）来刻画代码属性。

Result: 后发布缺陷集中于老旧、频繁修改和高变更率的组件中，通常需要比前发布缺陷更长、更复杂的修复。

Conclusion: 残余缺陷更多地源于代码演化与过程动态，而非单纯的代码结构问题；可靠性工程应优先针对成熟和复杂代码区域进行针对性测试。

Abstract: Understanding how software defects manifest and evolve in production environments is critical for improving reliability. While previous research has largely focused on pre-release defects, the nature of residual faults, i.e., those escaping testing and surfacing post-release, remains poorly understood. This paper presents a large-scale characterization of pre- and post-release defects across C/C++ and Java systems, encompassing over 14k defects mined from open-source projects. We employ a broad suite of software metrics to capture diverse code attributes such as complexity, size, structure, and development history.
  Results show that post-release defects are concentrated in older, frequently modified, and high-churn components, typically requiring longer and more complex fixes than pre-release ones. These findings highlight that residual defects arise more from evolutionary and process dynamics than code structure alone, suggesting that reliability engineering should prioritize targeted testing in mature and complex code regions.

</details>


### [154] [Reproducible Automated Program Repair Is Hard -- Experiences With the Defects4J Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26674)
*Adam Krafczyk,Klaus Schmid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了广泛使用的APR基准数据集Defects4J（包含835个来自17个Java项目的缺陷）存在的问题。研究发现21.6%的缺陷在严格的可复现性要求下不适合评估实验，另有7.1%的缺陷测试套件存在明显不足（删除单行代码即可通过所有测试）。论文提出了超越传统缺陷复现的APR缺陷数据集系统性要求，并开发了更严格检查测试套件 adequacy的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前APR研究中广泛使用的缺陷基准数据集（如Defects4J，被引用超过1800次）在实际使用中存在超越传统测试用例复现的重大挑战，需要系统性地识别和量化这些问题。

Method: 对Defects4J中的835个缺陷进行系统性调查，执行测试套件并采用严格的可复现性要求；设计实验检测测试套件是否under-specified（通过删除单行代码使所有测试通过）。

Result: 180个缺陷（21.6%）不适合评估实验；59个缺陷（7.1%）的测试套件存在明显不足，删除单行代码即可通过所有测试而无需应用人工补丁。

Conclusion: 需要建立超越传统缺陷复现的APR缺陷数据集系统性要求；现有的Defects4J数据集存在不可忽视的质量问题；需要更严格的评估框架来检测测试套件缺陷（如flaky tests）。

Abstract: In the research of automated program repair (APR), benchmark datasets consisting of known defects in combination with test suites that indicate the defects are of high importance. They allow for an evidence-based comparison of different APR approaches. In our own work on APR we found significant challenges when working with widely used defect datasets, which go beyond mere repeatability of defects via test cases. We summarize these identified challenges and related lessons learned to bring them to the attention of the APR community and quantify the potential impact of them.
  In particular, we investigate the widely used benchmark Defects4J, which has according to Google Scholar over 1,800 citations. It consists of 835 defects from 17 open-source Java projects; a hand-curated collection of defects, test suites that clearly indicate the defect, and human patches where any unrelated changes are removed. We find that, when executing the test suites with strict requirements for reproducibility in APR settings (beyond merely reproducing the defect via test cases), 180 (21.6 %) of the defects are not suitable for evaluation experiments. Further, we find that an additional 59 (7.1 %) defects have test suites that are obviously under-specified, as deleting a single statement from the code base makes all test cases pass, although the human-written patch does not only delete code.
  Our contributions are: a systematic collection of requirements for defect datasets for APR beyond traditional reproducibility of defects, a description of practical experiences and quantitative analysis of problems with the Defects4J dataset, as well as an implementation of an evaluation framework for APR tools for Java programs. This evaluation framework does stricter checking for indications of inadequate test suites, to avoid otherwise unnoticed problems in the test suite, such as flaky tests.

</details>


### [155] [When Model Editing Meets Service Evolution: A Knowledge-Update Perspective for Service Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26686)
*Guodong Fan,Cuiyun Gao,Chun Yong Chong,Lu Zhang,Jing Li,Jinglin Zhang,Shizhan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对动态服务生态系统中的推荐挑战，提出EVOREC框架，通过模型编辑和有限自动机约束解码技术解决服务过时与冗余问题，实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务推荐依赖静态表示和历史数据，难以适应动态演化的服务生态系统；大语言模型虽具潜力，但面临服务事实过时及无效/冗余服务两大挑战。

Method: 提出EVOREC框架，采用"定位-编辑"范式的模型编辑技术高效融入更新后的服务事实；同时引入基于有限自动机(FA)的约束解码机制与去重策略，确保服务的结构语义有效性并消除重复推荐。

Result: 在真实服务数据集上，EVOREC在Recall@5上平均提升25.9%；在演化场景下优于微调方法22.3%。

Conclusion: EVOREC框架展现出对服务演化的强适应性，为动态生态系统中的服务推荐提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of software services poses substantial challenges to the design and implementation of effective recommendation systems. Traditional service recommendation approaches often rely on static representations and historical usage data, which are insufficient for adapting to the dynamic and evolving nature of service ecosystems. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential to overcome these limitations by leveraging rich contextual understanding. However, their practical use faces two major challenges: outdated service facts and invalid or redundant services. To address these issues, we propose EVOREC, an evolution-aware framework for service recommendation that leverages model editing in a locate-then-edit paradigm to incorporate updated service facts without costly retraining efficiently. This allows the model to remain aligned with evolving service ecosystems. To address invalid service issues, we introduce a Finite Automata (FA)-based constrained decoding mechanism with deduplication, which enforces structural and semantic validity while eliminating repeated services. Experiments on real-world service datasets demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing baselines, e.g., achieving an average relative improvement of 25.9% in Recall@5. Moreover, under evolving service scenarios, our approach outperforms model fine-tuning approaches by 22.3%, demonstrating strong adaptability to service evolution and providing a practical solution for service recommendation in dynamic ecosystems

</details>


### [156] [Comparing Smart Contract Paradigms: A Preliminary Study of Security and Developer Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26727)
*Matteo Vaccargiu,Andrea Pinna,Maria Ilaria Lunesu,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项混合方法研究比较了同一开发团队用 Solidity（命令式）和 Move（资源导向）实现的 12 对功能等效智能合约。结果表明 Move 显式安全检查密度降低 60%（6.7% vs 16.8%），但代码量增加 47%；开发者调查显示 Move 学习难度适中但安全信心更高，55% 倾向在安全关键应用中使用 Move。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约漏洞已造成数十亿美元损失，研究旨在探讨编程语言范式是否能降低安全负担。命令式语言（如 Solidity）需开发者手动实现安全检查，而资源导向语言（如 Move）将安全保证编码在类型系统中。

Method: 混合方法研究：(1) 定量分析：12 对功能等效合约对，由同一开发团队分别用 Solidity 和 Move 实现；(2) 定性调查：11 位同时掌握两种语言的开发者的问卷调查。

Result: ① Move 显式安全开销降低 60%（安全检查密度：6.7% vs 16.8%，p=0.002，Cohen's d=-1.75）；② 代码量增加 47%（p=0.002，d=1.90）；③ 圈复杂度相同；④ 开发者学习难度适中，但对 Move 安全性信心更高（中位数 6/7，11 人中 10 人高于中性）；⑤ 55% 倾向在安全关键应用中选择 Move，尽管生态成熟度存在差距。

Conclusion: 资源导向范式将安全从运行时验证转移至编译时保证；采用需投入学习和工具建设成本。该受控比较为语言选择决策提供初步证据，并指明开发者资源改进方向。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities have caused billions in financial losses, raising questions about whether programming language paradigms can reduce security overhead. While imperative languages like Solidity require developers to manually implement security checks, resource-oriented languages like Move encode safety guarantees in type systems. We present a preliminary mixed-methods study analyzing 12 functionally-equivalent contract pairs implemented in both Solidity and Move by the same development team, complemented by a survey of 11 developers experienced in both languages. Quantitative analysis reveals that Move reduces explicit security overhead by 60\% (security check density: 6.7% vs. 16.8%, p=0.002, Cohen's d=-1.75) at the cost of 47% larger code size (p=0.002, d=1.90), while maintaining identical cyclomatic complexity. Developer surveys show moderate learning difficulty but higher safety confidence in Move (Median=6/7, 10 of 11 above neutral), with 55% preferring Move for security-critical applications despite ecosystem maturity gaps. These preliminary findings suggest resource-oriented paradigms shift security from runtime validation to compile-time guarantees, though adoption requires investment in learning and tooling. The controlled comparison provides initial evidence for paradigm effects on smart contract development, informing language selection decisions and identifying opportunities for improved developer resources.

</details>


### [157] [What Is the Cost of Energy Monitoring? An Empirical Study on the Overhead of RAPL-Based Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26815)
*Jeremy Diamond,Vincenzo Stoico*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文量化了基于RAPL接口的能量监测工具在高频（1kHz）测量中引入的开销，并提出改进方案。作者通过两个对照实验评估了7种工具，并开发了自己的用户空间应用和内核模块来降低开销。研究发现现有工具的时间开销范围为0.25%至46.75%，而优化后的工具可接近无工具基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAPL接口虽广泛用于软件能耗估算，但工具设计差异和高频轮询会产生测量开销（工具本身消耗的时间和能量），影响测量准确性。

Method: 实验一：评估7种工具（包括作者开发的用户空间应用和内核模块）与无工具基线的对比，使用6个NAS基准测试函数量化开销；实验二：隔离并计时MSR轮询的关键函数（rdmsr、sys/proc_read），估算执行延迟。

Result: 现有用户空间工具在1kHz频率下产生0.25%-46.75%的时间开销；作者的工具显著降低了系统调用和内联数学运算开销；系统调用慢于rdmsr，rdmsr慢于cpuid指令。

Conclusion: 简化工具设计并采用低级指令访问RAPL值可大幅改进基于RAPL的能量测量；研究为开发者提供了高频能量分析工具的设计指导，并证明特定技术可加速RAPL值访问。

Abstract: The Running Average Power Limit (RAPL) interface is widely used to estimate software energy consumption via CPU and DRAM counters, but tool design differences and high-frequency polling can introduce measurement overhead, namely, extra time and energy consumed by the tool itself.This paper quantifies the impact of RAPL-based tools on high-frequency (1 kHz) energy monitoring and investigates mitigation strategies. We conduct two controlled experiments: the first evaluates seven tools, including a user-space application and a kernel module developed by the authors, against a no-tool baseline, using six NAS Benchmark functions to quantify overhead. The second experiment isolates and times key functions for polling Model-Specific Registers (MSRs) (rdmsr and sys/proc_read) to estimate their execution latencies and identify potential slowdowns. The results show that existing user-space tools can introduce substantial time overhead at 1 kHz, whereas our tools significantly reduce system call overhead and inline math overhead. The time overhead of existing tools ranges from 0.25% to 46.75%. Our solutions maintain time overhead levels close to the baseline. We also find that system calls are slower than rdmsr, which in turn is slower than traditionally long-running instructions like cpuid. These findings indicate that RAPL-based energy measurement can be substantially improved by simplifying tool design and employing lower-level instructions to access RAPL values. Our findings provide guidance for practitioners on how to develop high-frequency energy profiling tools, show possible situations that can skew energy values, and demonstrate that access to RAPL values can be faster using specific techniques.

</details>


### [158] [Hot Fixing in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26892)
*Carol Hanna,Karine Even-Mendoza,W. B. Langdon,Mar Zamorano López,Justyna Petke,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模实证分析了GitHub上超过61,000个仓库中的热修复（hot fix）行为，发现热修复具有紧急性特征：单人贡献、小规模修改（2-3个提交/文件、<10行修改）、审查人数少（<2人）、测试文件修改少。同时，该研究对比了人类与AI智能体在热修复中的行为差异，识别出10余种不同的修复模式，为未来人机协作提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管热修复在软件维护中具有重要运营意义，但在自主编码智能体时代，热修复如何重塑常规维护工作流程的大规模实证证据仍然有限。现有研究缺乏对热修复紧急性特征的深入理解，以及人类与AI智能体在处理热修复时的行为差异分析。

Method: 研究团队分析了Hao-Li/AIDev数据集中超过61,000个GitHub仓库中的热修复事件，通过仓库级别的紧急性操作化定义，提取并对比了人类和AI智能体-authored热修复的代码变更特征。

Result: 热修复呈现一致的紧急性模式：协作减少（通常单人）、变更更小更精准（中位数2-3个提交和文件、修改<10行）、审查有限（通常<2人）、测试文件修改显著少于常规bug修复。人类与AI智能体在热修复中展现出超过10种不同的修复行为模式。

Conclusion: 该研究首次基于仓库级别紧急性操作化进行大规模实证分析，厘清了人类与AI智能体在热修复中的差异特征，为理解现实世界的热修复实践及未来人机自动化协作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite the operational importance of hot fixes, large-scale evidence on how they reshape routine maintenance workflows, particularly in the era of autonomous coding agents, remains limited. We analyse hot fixes present in over 61,000 GitHub repositories from the Hao-Li/AIDev dataset and find consistent patterns of urgency: reduced collaboration (typically a single contributor), smaller and more targeted changes (median 2-3 commits and files, with <10 line modifications), limited review (often fewer than two reviewers), and substantially fewer test file modifications than regular bug fixes, consistent with their urgency-driven character. Leveraging the same urgency contexts, we examine differences between human- and AI-agent-authored hot fixes, revealing over 10 distinct repair behaviours, thus offering insights into future human-automation collaboration for hot fixing. Our study is the first to empirically analyse hot fix code changes at scale using a repository-level operationalisation of urgency. The comparison of human and agentbehaviours delineates their distinct characteristics, providing a foundation for understanding hot fixing in real-world practice

</details>


### [159] [ClassEval-Pro: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Class-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26923)
*Yeheng Chen,Chaoxiang Xie,Yuling Shi,Wenhao Zeng,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ClassEval-Pro是一个包含300个类级任务的基准测试，用于评估LLM的组合式代码创建能力。该基准通过自动化三阶段流程构建，涵盖11个领域，经过严格验证（LLM Judge Ensemble + >90%行覆盖率的测试套件）。最佳模型Pass@1仅为45.6%，表明类级代码生成仍是重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码生成评估存在两个极端：要么局限于独立函数级合成，要么依赖昂贵且易受数据污染影响的手动策划类级任务。介于两者之间的组合式代码创建（从规格说明构建完整、结构化的类）能力尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 采用自动化三阶段流水线构建基准：(1)复杂度增强，(2)跨领域类组合，(3)整合2025年1月之后的真实GitHub代码。每个任务通过LLM Judge Ensemble验证，并需通过覆盖率超过90%的测试套件。评估了5个前沿LLM在5种生成策略下的表现。

Result: 最强模型类级Pass@1仅为45.6%；最强与最弱模型间存在17.7个百分点差距，证明基准具有区分力。策略选择与模型能力高度相关：结构化方法（如自底向上）可使弱模型提升最多9.4个百分点，而组合式生成性能低至1.3%。

Conclusion: 对500个手动标注失败案例的错误分析表明：逻辑错误（56.2%）和依赖错误（38.0%）是主要失败原因，揭示跨方法协调是类级代码生成的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: LLMs have achieved strong results on both function-level code synthesis and repository-level code modification, yet a capability that falls between these two extremes -- compositional code creation, i.e., building a complete, internally structured class from a specification -- remains underserved. Current evaluations are either confined to isolated functions or rely on manually curated class-level tasks that are expensive to scale and increasingly susceptible to data contamination. We introduce ClassEval-Pro, a benchmark of 300 class-level tasks spanning 11 domains, constructed through an automated three-stage pipeline that combines complexity enhancement, cross-domain class composition, and integration of real-world GitHub code contributed after January 2025. Every task is validated by an LLM Judge Ensemble and must pass test suites with over 90% line coverage. We evaluate five frontier LLMs under five generation strategies. The best model achieves only 45.6% class-level Pass@1, with a 17.7-point gap between the strongest and weakest models, confirming the benchmark's discriminative power. Strategy choice strongly interacts with model capability: structured approaches such as bottom-up improve weaker models by up to 9.4 percentage points, while compositional generation collapses to as low as 1.3%. Error analysis over 500 manually annotated failures reveals that logic errors (56.2%) and dependency errors (38.0%) dominate, identifying cross-method coordination as the core bottleneck.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Mining Negative Sequential Patterns to Improve Viral Genomic Feature Representation and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25968)
*Wenxi Zhu,Wensheng Gan,Zhenlian Qi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该研究提出GeneNSPCla框架，利用负序列模式(NSPs)提取病毒基因组中的缺失特征，结合GONPM+算法实现病毒序列分类，在8个分类器中平均准确率较原始负模式算法提升10.03%，较正模式算法提升24.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基因组的分类模型主要依赖组成或频率-based的子序列特征，在复杂或不平衡数据集上往往存在可解释性差和准确率降低的问题。

Method: 该研究提出GeneNSPCla框架，基于负序列模式(NSPs)从RNA病毒基因组的核苷酸序列中提取区分性缺失特征；将NSPs转化为数值特征向量并集成到多个监督分类器中。同时提出适应基因组数据处理的负模式挖掘算法GONPM+。

Result: GONPM+在8个分类器中的平均准确率比原始负模式挖掘算法提升10.03%，比正模式挖掘算法提升24.75%。

Conclusion: GeneNSPCla通过整合基于缺失的序列信息，为病毒基因组分析与分类提供了新的互补视角。GONPM+算法能够发现更长且更具生物学意义的负序列模式。

Abstract: Viruses represent the most abundant biological entities on Earth and play a pivotal role in microbial ecosystems, yet, as prominent human pathogens, they are closely linked to human morbidity and mortality. Accurate identification of viral sequences from viral genome sequences is therefore essential, but existing genome-based classification models that largely relying on composition- or frequency-based subsequence features often suffer from limited interpretability and reduced accuracy, particularly on complex or imbalanced datasets. To address these limitations, we propose GeneNSPCla (Genomic Negative Sequential Pattern-based Classification), a novel viral classification framework based on Negative Sequential Patterns (NSPs) that extracts discriminative absence-based features from nucleotide sequences of RNA viral genomes. By transforming these NSPs into numerical feature vectors and integrating them into multiple supervised classifiers, GeneNSPCla effectively captures both presence and absence signals in viral sequences. Furthermore, we propose a negative pattern mining algorithm adapted for processing genomic data: GONPM+, which can discover longer and more biologically meaningful negative sequential patterns. The experimental results demonstrate that the average accuracy of GONPM+ in 8 classifiers has improved by 10.03% compared to the original negative pattern mining algorithm and by 24.75% compared to the positive pattern mining algorithm. These findings highlight the effectiveness of incorporating absence-based sequential information, providing a new and complementary perspective for viral genome analysis and classification.

</details>


### [161] [CacheRAG: A Semantic Caching System for Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26176)
*Yushi Sun,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CacheRAG通过引入缓存增强架构，将传统的无状态规划器转变为持续学习者，解决了LLM驱动知识图谱问答系统中的模式幻觉和检索覆盖受限问题。该系统通过三层创新设计实现显著性能提升，在CRAG数据集上达到13.2%的准确率和17.5%真实性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的知识图谱问答系统作为无状态规划器，在隔离环境中生成检索计划，未能利用历史查询模式。类似于数据库系统每次从头优化查询而不使用计划缓存，导致模式幻觉和检索覆盖受限。这是当前RAG增强KGQA架构的根本性设计缺陷。

Method: CacheRAG提出三大创新设计原则：（1）模式无关用户界面：基于中间语义表示（ISR）的两阶段语义解析框架，支持非专家用户纯自然语言交互，后端适配器将LLM与本地模式上下文对接以编译可执行物理查询；（2）多样性优化的缓存检索：领域→方面双层层次索引结合最大边际相关性（MMR）最大化缓存示例的结构多样性，缓解推理同质化；（3）有界启发式扩展：确定性深度和广度子图操作符配合严格复杂度保证，在避免无限API执行风险的同时显著提升检索召回率。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，CacheRAG显著优于现有最先进基线方法：在CRAG数据集上实现13.2%的准确率提升和17.5%真实性提升。

Conclusion: CacheRAG通过缓存增强机制有效解决了LLM驱动KGQA系统的核心问题，其创新设计在保持系统安全性的同时大幅提升了问答质量和检索效果，为构建更加智能和可靠的知识图谱问答系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has significantly advanced Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA). However, existing LLM-driven KGQA systems act as stateless planners, generating retrieval plans in isolation without exploiting historical query patterns: analogous to a database system that optimizes every query from scratch without a plan cache. This fundamental design flaw leads to schema hallucinations and limited retrieval coverage. We propose CacheRAG, a systematic cache-augmented architecture for LLM-based KGQA that transforms stateless planners into continual learners. Unlike traditional database plan caching (which optimizes for frequency), CacheRAG introduces three novel design principles tailored for LLM contexts: (1) Schema-agnostic user interface: A two-stage semantic parsing framework via Intermediate Semantic Representation (ISR) enables non-expert users to interact purely in natural language, while a Backend Adapter grounds the LLM with local schema context to compile executable physical queries safely. (2) Diversity-optimized cache retrieval: A two-layer hierarchical index (Domain $\rightarrow$ Aspect) coupled with Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) maximizes structural variety in cached examples, effectively mitigating reasoning homogeneity. (3) Bounded heuristic expansion: Deterministic depth and breadth subgraph operators with strict complexity guarantees significantly enhance retrieval recall without risking unbounded API execution. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that CacheRAG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines (e.g., +13.2% accuracy and +17.5% truthfulness on the CRAG dataset).

</details>


### [162] [Evergreen: Efficient Claim Verification for Semantic Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26180)
*Alexander W. Lee,Benjamin Han,Shayak Sen,Sam Yeom,Ugur Cetintemel,Anupam Datta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Evergreen是一个声明验证系统，将自然语言聚合中的声明验证重新定义为语义查询处理任务。通过验证感知优化和通用语义查询优化，在保持F1=1.00验证质量的同时，相比未优化方法成本降低3.2倍、延迟降低4.0倍；相比强LLM-as-judge基线成本降低48倍；相比检索增强智能体使用弱LLM即可达到相同性能，成本降低63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义聚合操作产生的自然语言聚合可能包含未基于底层关系支撑的声明，验证这些声明具有挑战性：涉及量词、分组和比较，远超LLM上下文窗口，需昂贵组合语义和符号处理。

Method: 将声明验证编译为声明式语义验证查询并在同一引擎上执行，采用验证感知优化（早停、相关性排序、置信序列估计）和通用优化（算子融合、相似度过滤、提示缓存），使用半环溯源为每个判决生成最小元组集合的引用。

Result: 在真实餐厅评论数据集基准测试中，Evergreen达到优秀验证质量（F1=1.00）。相比未优化验证，使用强LLM时成本降低3.2倍、延迟降低4.0倍。使用弱LLM时，F1超越强LLM-as-judge基线，成本降低48倍。使用弱LLM相比使用强LLM的检索增强智能体，达到相同F1的同时成本降低63倍、延迟降低4.2倍。

Conclusion: Evergreen通过将声明验证重新定义为语义查询处理任务，并结合验证感知优化和通用语义查询优化，在保持验证质量（F1=1.00）的同时显著降低成本和延迟。

Abstract: With recent semantic query processing engines, semantic aggregation has become a primitive operator, enabling the reduction of a relation into a natural language aggregate using an LLM. However, the resulting semantic aggregate may contain claims that are not grounded in the underlying relation. Verifying such claims is challenging: they often involve quantifiers, groupings, and comparisons over relations that far exceed LLM context windows and require a costly combination of semantic and symbolic processing.
  We present Evergreen, a system that recasts claim verification as a semantic query processing task with tailored optimizations and provenance capture. Evergreen compiles each claim into a declarative semantic verification query and executes it on the same engine that produced the aggregate. To reduce cost and latency, Evergreen avoids unnecessary LLM calls through verification-aware optimizations (early stopping, relevance sorting, and estimation with confidence sequences) and general-purpose optimizations for semantic queries (operator fusion, similarity filtering, and prompt caching). Each verdict is accompanied by citations that identify a minimal set of tuples justifying the result, with semantics based on semiring provenance for first-order logic.
  On a benchmark of real-world restaurant review datasets reflecting production-inspired workloads, Evergreen achieves excellent verification quality (F1 = 1.00) with a strong LLM while reducing cost by 3.2x and latency by 4.0x compared to unoptimized verification. Even with a significantly weaker LLM, Evergreen outperforms a strong LLM-as-a-judge baseline in F1 at 48x lower cost and 2.3x lower latency. Relative to a retrieval-augmented agent, Evergreen compares favorably in F1 and latency with similar cost when both use a strong LLM; yet, with a much weaker LLM, it achieves the same F1 at 63x lower cost and 4.2x lower latency.

</details>


### [163] [PiLLar: Matching for Pivot Table Schema via LLM-guided Monte-Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26356)
*Yunjun Gao,Chuangyu Ouyang,Congcong Ge,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PiLLar是首个面向透视表(pivot table)模式的匹配框架，通过LLM驱动的搜索范式实现无需训练的跨领域自适应匹配，解决了匿名化数据环境下的模式匹配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透视表在现代数据湖生态中广泛存在，准确匹配透视表模式成为数据集成的关键前提。然而，实践中大量匿名化数据的存在给匹配精度和泛化能力带来重大挑战。传统方法难以应对这种数据敏感性任务。

Method: 1) 提出LLM驱动的搜索范式，在最少标注的隐私合规数据上操作，实现跨领域零样本适配；2) 建立该范式的误差动态理论分析框架，确保方法的渐进收敛性；3) 构建新基准PTbench，从四个代表性领域挖掘适合逆透视的表格，对语义一致属性执行逆透视，并进行采样和匿名化处理。

Result: PiLLar在正确预测的匹配上达到87.94%的平均准确率，验证了其相对于现有方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地解决了透视表模式匹配问题，通过LLM与理论分析的结合，在保护数据隐私的前提下实现了高质量的模式对齐，为数据集成领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Pivot tables are ubiquitous in data lakes of modern data ecosystems, making accurate schema matching over pivot tables a key prerequisite for data integration. In this paper, we focus on matching for pivot table schema, which is a novel joint schema-value matching task. It aims to align schemas between pivot tables and standard relational tables, where a correct match must be semantically consistent at the schema level and compatible at the value level. However, due to the inherent data sensitivity of this task, the prevalence of anonymized data in practice poses significant challenges to its matching accuracy and generalization capability. To tackle these challenges, we propose PiLLar, the first matching for pivot table schema framework. We first formulate PiLLar as an LLM-driven search paradigm that operates with minimal annotated privacy-compliant data, thereby achieving training-free adaptation across diverse domains. Next, we provide a theoretical analysis on the error dynamics of the paradigm to ensure the asymptotic convergence of the proposed method. Furthermore, we introduce a new benchmark PTbench, derived from four representative real-world domains and constructed by mining unpivot-suitable tables, performing unpivot on semantically coherent attributes, and applying sampling and anonymization. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of PiLLar, which achieves an average accuracy of 87.94% on the correctly predicted matches.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Sociodemographic Biases in Educational Counselling by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25932)
*Tomasz Adamczyk,Wiktoria Mieleszczenko-Kowszewicz,Beata Bajcar,Grzegorz Chodak,Aleksander Szczęsny,Maciej Markiewicz,Karolina Ostrowska,Aleksandra Sawczuk,Przemysław Kazienko*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究大型语言模型(LLM)在教育咨询中的社会人口统计学偏见。通过对6个LLM回答900个不同背景学生 vignettes 的测试(共243,000个响应),发现所有模型都表现出可测量的偏见,模式与人类偏见部分一致但有显著差异。学生描述的精确度是关键因素:模糊信息使差距扩大近3倍,而具体个性化信息可大幅减少偏见。研究强调了详细的学生特定信息对促进AI教育决策公平的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As LLMs are increasingly integrated into educational settings, understanding their potential sociodemographic biases is critical for ensuring fair and equitable educational counselling.

Method: Evaluated responses from six LLMs answering questions about 900 vignettes describing students in diverse circumstances. Each vignette was systematically tested across 14 sociodemographic identifiers (race, gender, socioeconomic status, immigrant background) plus a control condition, yielding 243,000 model responses.

Result: (1) All models exhibit measurable biases; (2) bias patterns partially align with documented human biases but diverge in notable ways; (3) vague or minimal student descriptions amplify disparities nearly threefold, while concrete individualized metrics substantially reduce them; (4) bias profiles vary substantially across models.

Conclusion: AI-driven educational decisions benefit from detailed, student-specific information to promote fairness and equity. The findings highlight the importance of context-rich and personalized educational representations when deploying LLMs in educational counselling contexts.

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into educational settings, understanding their potential biases is critical. This study examines sociodemographic biases in LLM-based educational counselling. We evaluate responses from six LLMs answering questions about 900 vignettes describing students in diverse circumstances. Each vignette is systematically tested across 14 sociodemographic identifiers - spanning race and gender, socioeconomic status, and immigrant background - along with a control condition, yielding 243,000 model responses. Our findings indicate that (1) all models exhibit measurable biases, (2) bias patterns partially align with documented human biases but diverge in notable ways, (3) the magnitude of these biases is strongly influenced by the precision of the student descriptions, where vague or minimal information amplifies disparities nearly threefold, while concrete, individualised metrics substantially reduce them, and (4) bias profiles vary substantially across models. These results demonstrate the importance of context-rich and personalised educational representations, suggesting that AI-driven educational decisions benefit from detailed student-specific information to promote fairness and equity.

</details>


### [165] [The Creation and Analysis of Government AI Transparency Statements in Australia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26075)
*Shidong Pan,Haochen Gong,Boming Xia,Xiaoyu Sun,Xiwei Xu,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper introduces AITS-101, the first government AI Transparency Statements dataset, and conducts the first systematic analysis of these documents. Through multi-method analysis, the study uncovers considerable variation in disclosure practices and gaps between policy requirements and actual implementation, providing insights for designing more effective public-sector AI transparency standards.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As governments increasingly deploy AI in public services, transparency becomes essential for accountability and public trust. Australia's AITS requires disclosure of AI usage practices, but there is a significant lack of empirical evidence on how these requirements are actually realized in practice.

Method: The authors employed stylometric analysis, quantitative document analysis, and qualitative document analysis to examine disclosure coverage, structure, and recurring patterns in the AITS documents.

Result: The findings reveal substantial variation in AI-related practice disclosure across government bodies, and highlight significant gaps between policy intent and implementation reality.

Conclusion: The paper provides actionable insights for improving public-sector AI transparency standards by identifying implementation gaps between policy intent and actual practice.

Abstract: Governments increasingly deploy AI in public services, making transparency essential for accountability and public trust. Australia's Standard for AI Transparency Statements (AITS) requires government bodies to disclose how AI is used in practice, yet little empirical evidence exists on how these requirements are realised in documents. This paper presents the first government AITS dataset, dubbed AITS-101, and provides the first systematic analysis of their content. Using stylometric, quantitative, and qualitative document analyses, we examine disclosure coverage, structure, and recurring patterns. Our findings reveal substantial variation in AI-related practice disclosure, highlight gaps between policy intent and implementation, and inform the design of more effective public-sector AI transparency standards.

</details>


### [166] [The Buy-or-Build Decision, Revisited: How Agentic AI Changes the Economics of Enterprise Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26482)
*David Klotz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文挑战了AI将使SaaS过时的"SaaSageddon"论点，认为该预测对大多数企业应用类别而言过度夸张。研究表明，AI时代Make模式对商品化工具和差异化定制应用最具吸引力，而Buy模式仍适用于受监管和任务关键型系统。AI从根本上改变了Make选项的治理属性，从传统的纯层级治理转变为融合代码所有权与外部AI基础设施依赖的混合治理形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "SaaSageddon"叙事预测AI将使SaaS市场大部分领域过时，企业可以极低成本内部构建软件，这挑战了企业应用"制造或购买"决策的传统经济学基础，亟需系统性重新评估。

Method: 概念性研究方法，融合交易成本经济学与资源基础观，并评估当前AI能力，对七个典型决策决定因素进行因子层面分析，同时开发企业应用对AI诱导的经济学转变敏感度的类型学分类。

Result: ①因子层面分析揭示AI如何重塑成本、战略差异化、资产专用性、供应商锁定、上市时间、质量与合规、组织能力等七大决定因素；②开发了企业应用对AI引发经济学转变敏感度的类型学分类；③证明AI从根本上改变了Make选项的治理属性，从Williamson的纯层级治理转变为混合治理形式，具有与AI前内部开发截然不同的经济学、能力要求和治理结构。

Conclusion: Make模式在AI时代对商品化工具和差异化定制应用最具吸引力，而Buy模式仍适用于受监管和任务关键型系统。AI将Make从纯粹的层级治理转变为混合治理形式，结合代码所有权与外部AI基础设施依赖。

Abstract: Advances in generative artificial intelligence, particularly agentic coding systems capable of autonomous software development, are disrupting the economics of the make-or-buy decision for enterprise applications. The "SaaSocalypse" narrative predicts that AI will render large segments of the Software-as-a-Service market obsolete by enabling firms to build software in-house at a fraction of historical cost. This paper adopts a conceptual research approach, combining transaction cost economics and the resource-based view with an assessment of current AI capabilities, to systematically re-evaluate the factors underlying the make-or-buy decision. It makes three contributions. First, it provides a factor-level analysis of how AI reshapes seven canonical decision determinants: cost, strategic differentiation, asset specificity, vendor lock-in, time-to-market, quality and compliance, and organizational capability. Second, it develops a typology of enterprise applications by their sensitivity to AI-induced shifts in make-or-buy economics. Third, it demonstrates that AI fundamentally transforms the governance properties of the Make option, shifting it from Williamson's pure hierarchy to a hybrid governance form that combines code ownership with external AI infrastructure dependency, with qualitatively different economics, capability requirements, and governance structures than pre-AI in-house development. The analysis finds that the SaaSocalypse thesis is overstated for most enterprise application categories; Make is most compelling for commodity utilities and differentiating custom applications in the AI era, while regulated and mission-critical systems remain predominantly in the buy domain.

</details>


### [167] [Counting own goals: High-level assessment of the economic relationship between the ICT and the Oil and Gas sectors and its environmental implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26539)
*Gauthier Roussilhe,Béatrice Dromard,Srinjoy Mitra*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文揭示了ICT行业与石油天然气行业之间被忽视的财务关联及其环境后果。通过投入产出分析发现，ICT行业约2%的年资金流向了石油天然气行业；2022年石油天然气行业获得的ICT投入是清洁能源行业的4倍以上。研究提供了石油天然气行业数字活动的分类框架和案例研究，以评估数字化带来的新增排放，并探讨了生成式AI与石油天然气行业的早期渊源，为全面评估ICT行业净环境影响提供了方法学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICT行业虽被视为环保领域的重要贡献者，但其对石油和天然气等碳密集型产业数字化的推动作用——即"自身失误"——在20多年来鲜有讨论。现有环境评估主要关注ICT的碳足迹和减排贡献，却忽视了数字化可能增加碳排放的一面。

Method: 使用投入产出分析方法和2000年至2022年的经济数据，追踪ICT部门与石油天然气部门之间的资金流动，并对石油天然气行业的数字活动进行分类，同时利用案例研究估算数字化可能带来的新增排放量。

Result: 研究表明，平均每年约有2%的ICT行业资金流向石油天然气行业；2022年，每向可再生能源和核能行业投入1美元，就有超过4美元流向石油天然气行业。文章还提供了石油天然气行业数字活动的分类体系，以及两个估算数字化新增排放量的案例研究，并探讨了GPU技术发展与石油天然气行业之间的早期关联。

Conclusion: 本文为定义ICT与石油天然气行业之间的关系奠定了基础，并提供了估算石油天然气行业数字化新增排放量所需的分析要素，包括数字活动的分类框架和案例研究。研究揭示了ICT与石油天然气行业之间长期被忽视的联系，对AI时代的碳排放评估具有重要前瞻意义。

Abstract: The ICT sector has been one of the most successful and fastest-growing industry in history. While the environmental issue in this sector has mainly been addressed by assessing its footprint and, to a lesser extent, its avoided emissions or net impacts, the additional emissions from the digitalization of carbon-intensive activities, such as the Oil and Gas (O&G) sector, have rarely been discussed. By doing so, we have forgotten to count the own goals conceded over more than 20 years in the troubled relationship between the ICT and the O&G sector. Using input-output analysis and economic data ranging from 2000 to 2022, we observe that on average 2% of the annual financial flows from the ICT sector are directed towards the Oil and Gas sector. Considering the significant growth of the ICT sector during this time, O&G companies now spends a massive amount on ICT products in absolute terms. It also appears that in 2022, for each dollar going from the ICT sector to the renewable and nuclear energy industry, more than $4 go to the O&G industry. In addition, we also provide a classification of digital activities in the O&G sector to facilitate environmental assessments and present two case studies estimating potential added emissions from the digitalization of oil activities. Finally, looking at the immense growth in generative AI, we provide an exploration of causal links between the current success of GPU technology and its intricate early relationship with the O&G sector. This article lays the groundwork for defining the nature of the relationship between ICT and O&G, which predates the current hype surrounding generative AI. We provide the analytical elements needed to begin estimating the added emissions from the digitalisation of O&G.

</details>


### [168] [Risk Reporting for Developers' Internal AI Model Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24966)
*Oscar Delaney,Sambhav Maheshwari,Joe O'Brien,Theo Bearman,Oliver Guest*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper provides a harmonized reporting standard for frontier AI developers to document internal use risks, addressing gaps in existing legal frameworks (CA SB 53, NY RAISE Act, EU GPAI Code of Practice) through a structured approach focusing on two threat vectors (autonomous AI misbehavior and insider threats) and three risk factors (means, motive, opportunity).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frontier AI companies deploy their most advanced models internally for extended periods (weeks to months) before public release, creating risks that external deployment frameworks fail to address. With limited external visibility into internal AI use and rapid AI R&D automation, regular detailed risk reporting becomes critical for identifying and managing internal use risks before they materialize.

Method: The guide establishes a harmonized reporting standard suitable for three regulatory frameworks (CA SB 53, NY RAISE Act, EU GPAI Code of Practice). The reporting framework is structured around: (1) Two threat vectors - autonomous AI misbehavior and insider threats; (2) Three risk factors for each vector - means, motive, and opportunity. The primary audience is evaluation and safety teams at frontier AI developers, with regulators and auditors as secondary audience.

Result: A standardized framework for producing internal use risk reports that demonstrates model safety before internal deployment, provides documentation of safeguards and residual risks, and creates accountability mechanisms for when substantially more capable or riskier models are deployed internally.

Conclusion: This guide addresses the gap between internal AI deployment and external regulatory oversight by providing a harmonized reporting standard that bridges California's SB 53, New York's RAISE Act, and the EU's GPAI Code of Practice. The framework focuses on two threat vectors and three risk factors to structure comprehensive internal use risk reports.

Abstract: Frontier AI companies first deploy their most advanced models internally, for weeks or months of safety testing, evaluation, and iteration, before a possible public release. For example, Anthropic recently developed a new class of model with advanced cyberoffense-relevant capabilities, Mythos Preview, which was available internally for at least six weeks before it was publicly announced. This internal use creates risks that external deployment frameworks may fail to address.
  Legal frameworks, notably California's Transparency in Frontier Artificial Intelligence Act (SB 53), New York's Responsible AI Safety And Education (RAISE) Act, and the EU's General-Purpose AI Code of Practice, all discuss risks from internal AI use. They require frontier developers to make and implement plans for how to manage risks from internal use, and to produce internal use risk reports describing their safeguards and any residual risks. This guide provides a harmonized standard for companies to produce internal use risk reports suitable for all three regulatory frameworks. It is addressed primarily to evaluation and safety teams at frontier AI developers, and secondarily to regulators and auditors seeking to understand what good reporting looks like.
  Given the pace of AI R&D automation and the limited external visibility into how companies use their most capable models internally, regular and detailed risk reporting may be one of the few mechanisms available to ensure that the risks from internal AI use are identified and managed before they materialize. Whenever a substantially more capable or riskier model is deployed internally, the developer should create a risk report and argue why the model is safe to deploy. We structure the reporting framework around two threat vectors -- autonomous AI misbehavior and insider threats -- and three risk factors for each: means, motive, and opportunity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Efficient, VRAM-Constrained xLM Inference on Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26334)
*Aditya Ukarande,Deep Shekhar,Marc Blackstein,Ram Rangan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出pipelined sharding技术，通过CPU-GPU混合调度实现客户端设备上的高效无损LLM/VLM推理。对于LLM，TTFT提升6.7倍，TPS提升30倍；对于VLM，VRAM需求降低10倍；批处理模式下吞吐量提升8.2倍。该工作被MLSys 2026工业 track接收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型(LLM)和视觉语言模型(VLM)在客户端设备上部署需求的增长，如何在显存受限的客户端系统上实现高效、无损的高精度模型推理成为关键挑战。当前方法难以同时优化首Token响应时间(TTFT)和Token生成速率(TPS)这两个关键指标。

Method: 提出pipelined sharding技术，结合以下关键技术：1) 子层级别的模型分片；2) CPU卸载；3) 流水线化的复制-计算；4) VRAM中优先级的tensor放置。针对VLM，提出VLMOpt方案，整合vision tensor CPU卸载、flash attention和VLM VRAM重叠避免策略。

Result: 在多种模型和客户端系统上的严格评估表明：交互模式下LLM的TTFT提升最高6.7倍，TPS提升最高30倍；CR1 VLM推理的VRAM需求降低10倍；批处理模式下吞吐量提升最高8.2倍。

Conclusion: 该技术将应用于NVIDIA的两个重要产品：In-Game Inferencing SDK (IGI SDK)和Cosmos-Reason1物理AI推理VLM。通过benchmark引导的CPU-GPU混合调度，成功在VRAM受限条件下实现了密集模型和MoE模型的高效推理。

Abstract: To usher in the next round of client AI innovation, there is an urgent need to enable efficient, lossless inference of high-accuracy large language models (LLMs) and vision language models (VLMs), jointly referred to as xLMs, on client systems. To address this, we present pipelined sharding, a novel, benchmark-profile-guided CPU-GPU hybrid scheduling technique to achieve efficient, VRAM-constrained inference for both dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) LLMs. Using a combination of model sharding at the sub-layer level, CPU offloading, pipelined copy-compute, and prioritized tensor placement in VRAM, it optimizes both time-to-first-token (TTFT) and tokens per second (TPS) metrics, while flexibly adapting to system and inference conditions. For efficient, high-accuracy VLM inference, we combine pipelined sharding with a llama.cpp implementation of three well-understood prior ideas (jointly called VLMOpt), namely, vision tensor CPU offloading, flash attention, and vision and language model VRAM overlap avoidance.
  These enhancements are targeted at improving client xLM inference in future releases of two important NVIDIA products - the In-Game Inferencing software development kit (IGI SDK) and the Cosmos-Reason1 (CR1) physical AI reasoning VLM. Highlights from our rigorous evaluation spanning multiple models and client systems include: for interactive use, TTFT improves by up to 6.7x and TPS by up to 30x for LLMs, and CR1 inference's VRAM demand is down by 10x, while in batched mode, throughput improves by up to 8.2x, all compared to their respective aggressive baselines. This paper is accepted at the 9th MLSys Conference (Industry Track), 2026. Code and artifact available at: https://github.com/deepshnv/pipeshard-mlsys26-ae

</details>


### [170] [SplitFT: An Adaptive Federated Split Learning System For LLMs Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26388)
*Yimeng Shan,Zhaorui Zhang,Sheng Di,Yu Liu,Xiaoyi Lu,Benben Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SplitTF，一个针对大语言模型(LLM)微调的自适应联邦分割学习系统。该系统允许不同客户端根据其计算资源和模型性能需求设置不同的分割层，并通过在分割层降低LoRA秩来减少通信开销。同时提出基于长度的Dirichlet方法来模拟真实世界的异构数据分布。实验表明该方法在微调时间效率和模型性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦分割学习在应对分布式模型训练中的计算资源约束和数据隐私问题时表现出色，但将其应用于LLM微调时面临关键挑战：1) 如何自适应地为不同客户端设置分割层以应对数据和设备异构性；2) 如何有效降低微调过程中的通信开销。目前尚无工作同时解决这些问题。

Method: 提出SplitTF系统，包含三个核心设计：1) 允许不同客户端根据计算资源和训练性能设置不同分割层；2) 在分割层降低LoRA秩以减少通信开销；3) 提出基于长度的Dirichlet方法划分训练数据以模拟真实异构场景。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多个流行基准数据集上的微调时间效率和模型性能均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SplitTF能够有效解决联邦分割学习应用于LLM微调时的自适应分割层设置和通信开销问题，为异构环境下的分布式LLM微调提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Split Learning has been identified as an efficient approach to address the computational resource constraints of clients in classical federated learning, while guaranteeing data privacy for distributed model training across data owners. However, it faces some critical challenges when such a training strategy meets large language models (LLMs) for fine-tuning. Such challenges include setting the cutlayer adaptively across different clients to address the data and device heterogeneity issues, which affect the system performance significantly. In addition, efficiently reducing the communication overhead during the fine-tuning procedure is also another challenge. No work tries to address these challenges.
  To bridge this gap, we propose SplitTF, an adaptive federated split learning system for LLMs fine-tuning. SplitFT enables different clients to set different cut layers according to their computation resources and trained model performance. SplitFT also proposes to reduce the LoRA rank in cutlayer to reduce the communication overhead. In addition to simulating the heterogeneous data in real-world applications for our proposed split federated learning system, we propose a length-based Dirichlet approach to divide the training data into different clients. Extensive experimental results show that our proposed approach outperforms the state-of-the-art approach for fine-tuning time efficiency and model performance based on various popular benchmarks.

</details>


### [171] [DUAL-BLADE: Dual-Path NVMe-Direct KV-Cache Offloading for Edge LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26557)
*Bodon Jeong,Hongsu Byun,Youngjae Kim,Weikuan Yu,Kyungkeun Lee,Jihoon Yang,Sungyong Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DUAL-BLADE解决边缘AI系统中LLM推理的KV缓存内存瓶颈问题，通过双路径KV驻留框架动态分配张量至页面缓存或NVMe直通路，结合流水线并行优化，实现延迟降低和SSD利用率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The increasing deployment of LLM inference on edge AI systems requires efficient execution under tight memory budgets. KV caches often exceed available device memory, and existing NVMe-based offloading solutions suffer from cache thrashing, unpredictable latency, and high software overhead under memory pressure due to reliance on kernel page cache.

Method: DUAL-BLADE is a dual-path KV residency framework that dynamically assigns KV tensors to either a page-cache path or an NVMe-direct path based on runtime memory availability. The NVMe-direct path bypasses the filesystem by mapping KV tensors to contiguous LBA regions, enabling low-overhead direct storage access. It also incorporates adaptive pipeline parallelism to overlap storage I/O with GPU DMA.

Result: Substantial reduction in prefill latency (33.1%) and decode latency (42.4%), along with 2.2x improvement in SSD utilization across diverse memory budgets.

Conclusion: DUAL-BLADE reduces prefill latency by 33.1% and decode latency by 42.4%, while improving SSD utilization by 2.2x across diverse memory budgets, effectively mitigating I/O bottlenecks in LLM inference.

Abstract: The increasing deployment of Large Language Model (LLM) inference on edge AI systems demands efficient execution under tight memory budgets. A key challenge arises from Key-Value (KV) caches, which often exceed available device memory. Although NVMe-based offloading offers scalable capacity, existing file-based designs rely heavily on the kernel page cache, leading to cache thrashing, unpredictable latency, and high software overhead under memory pressure. We present DUAL-BLADE, a dual-path KV residency framework that dynamically assigns KV tensors to either a page-cache path or an NVMe-direct path based on runtime memory availability. The NVMe-direct path bypasses the filesystem by mapping KV tensors to contiguous logical block address (LBA) regions, enabling low-overhead direct storage access. DUAL-BLADE further incorporates adaptive pipeline parallelism to overlap storage I/O with GPU DMA, improving inference throughput. Our evaluation shows that DUAL-BLADE substantially mitigates I/O bottlenecks, reducing prefill and decode latency by up to 33.1% and 42.4%, respectively, while improving SSD utilization by 2.2x across diverse memory budgets.

</details>


### [172] [MPI Malleability Validation under Replayed Real-World HPC Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26576)
*S. Iserte,M. Madon,G. Da,J. Pierson,A. J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在真实HPC环境中验证动态资源管理（DRM）技术（如弹性可变性）的新方法，通过在MareNostrum 5超算的125节点上回放工作负载日志进行验证，结果表明该方法有效，MPI弹性可变性可减少27%的 workloads 时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Skepticism among administrators and users regarding DRM techniques (e.g., malleability) feasibility under real-world conditions despite extensive analytical and simulation studies.

Method: Replay workload logs on HPC infrastructure with adaptation to target cluster; validated on a 125-node partition of MareNostrum 5 supercomputer.

Result: Novel parallel efficiency-aware malleability reduced malleable workload time by 27%; maintained resource utilization rate; introduced queueing delays for individual jobs but did not delay baseline workload.

Conclusion: The proposed validation methodology is effective; MPI malleability achieved 27% workload time reduction without delaying baseline workload while maintaining resource utilization.

Abstract: Dynamic Resource Management (DRM) techniques can be leveraged to maximize throughput and resource utilization in computational clusters. Although DRM has been extensively studied through analytical workloads and simulations, skepticism persists among end administrators and users regarding their feasibility under real-world conditions. To address this problem, we propose a novel methodology for validating DRM techniques, such as malleability, in realistic scenarios that reproduce actual cluster conditions of jobs and users by replaying workload logs on a High-performance Computing (HPC) infrastructure. Our methodology is capable of adapting the workload to the target cluster. We evaluate our methodology in a malleability-enabled 125-node partition of the Marenostrum 5 supercomputer. Our results validate the proposed method and assess the benefits of MPI malleability on a novel use case of a pioneer user of malleability (our "PhD Student"): parallel efficiency-aware malleability reduced a malleable workload time by 27% without delaying the baseline workload, although introducing queueing delays for individual jobs, but maintaining the resource utilization rate.

</details>


### [173] [DMRlib: Easy-coding and Efficient Resource Management for Job Malleability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26624)
*Sergio Iserte,Rafael Mayo,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DMRlib是一个旨在降低进程弹性应用开发难度的库，通过提供简化的MPI类接口和预定义通信模式，使科学编程社区能够更容易地采用进程弹性技术。实验证明该方案可显著提升数据中心吞吐量达3倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进程弹性已在数据中心中证明能提升资源利用率和全局生产力，但其带来的额外开发成本限制了科学编程社区的采用。

Method: 提出DMRlib库，提供极简的MPI类语法和预定义的通信模式以简化弹性应用开发；部署了刚性(rigid)和可塑(moldable)两种作业提交模式的多种场景进行实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比传统非弹性作业工作负载，弹性方法可实现超过3倍的全局吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DMRlib的弹性方法相比传统非弹性工作负载可将全局吞吐量提升超过3倍。

Abstract: Process malleability has proved to have a highly positive impact on the resource utilization and global productivity in data centers compared with the conventional static resource allocation policy. However, the non-negligible additional development effort this solution imposes has constrained its adoption by the scientific programming community. In this work, we present DMRlib, a library designed to offer the global advantages of process malleability while providing a minimalist MPI-like syntax. The library includes a series of predefined communication patterns that greatly ease the development of malleable applications. In addition, we deploy several scenarios to demonstrate the positive impact of process malleability featuring different scalability patterns. Concretely, we study two job submission modes (rigid and moldable) in order to identify the best-case scenarios for malleability using metrics such as resource allocation rate, completed jobs per second, and energy consumption. The experiments prove that our elastic approach may improve global throughput by a factor higher than 3x compared to the traditional workloads of non-malleable jobs.

</details>


### [174] [FACT: Compositional Kernel Synthesis with a Three-Stage Agentic Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26666)
*Sina Heidari,Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Deep learning compilers and vendor libraries deliver strong baseline performance but are bounded by finite, engineer-curated catalogs. When these omit needed optimizations, practitioners substitute hand-written CUDA or CUTLASS, demanding expertise in GPU microarchitecture and C++ template metaprogramming. Recent LLM-based agents target kernel generation in raw CUDA, forcing rediscovery of optimizations already encoded in mature libraries.
  We present FACT (Framework for Agentic CUTLASS Transpilation), a framework that employs a three-stage, agent-driven workflow optimizing PyTorch modules through multi-pattern composition while grounding synthesis in CUTLASS C++. (1) Pattern discovery: an LLM agent inspects the traced graph, matches subgraphs to optimization rules, retrieves vetted examples from an architecture-specific index, and outputs prioritized patterns. (2) Pattern realization: each pattern is implemented as a CUTLASS kernel wrapped in a PyTorch extension, verified, and auto-tuned by sweeping parameters inferred from the CUTLASS hierarchy. (3) Pattern composition: extensions are loaded together into a single composed module for end-to-end benchmarking.
  We evaluate the workflow using KernelBench's evaluation framework and provided modules on an NVIDIA A100. On Level 1, we apply the workflow to three GEMM workloads (square matrix multiply, batched matrix multiply, and large-$K$ matrix multiply). Auto-tuned CUTLASS kernels improve over PyTorch cuBLAS baseline by $1.06\times$--$1.18\times$. On Level 3 MiniGPT block, composing fused multi-head attention with fused MLP GEMM+GELU yields $2.79\times$ end-to-end speedup. Our work couples agentic graph-level pattern discovery with auto-tuning and a dynamic pattern table, offering a practical path from traced PyTorch to deployable kernels by automating CUTLASS kernel synthesis and auto-tuning.

</details>


### [175] [COPUS: Co-adaptive Parallelism and Batch Size Selection in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26687)
*Akhmed Sakip,Erland Hilman Fuadi,Omar Sayedelahl,Zonghang Li,Jianshu She,Alham Fikri Aji,Steve Liu,Eric Xing,Qirong Ho*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出COPUS系统，通过Goodput指标联合自适应调优大模型训练中的全局批量大小、3D并行策略和微批量大小，解决了传统方法中批量大小与并行策略独立优化导致的次优配置问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法独立优化批量大小或并行策略，但忽略了二者的强耦合性：最优并行策略会随批量大小变化而改变，单独优化任一维度将导致训练过程部分时段处于次优配置。

Method: COPUS采用Goodput（吞吐量×统计效率）作为联合优化指标，结合3D并行下的在线梯度噪声尺度估计与候选配置的吞吐量评估，实现训练过程中批量大小、并行策略和微批量大小的自适应协同调整。

Result: 在1-4节点8×H100和8×MI210 GPU上，针对3B-32B参数模型的预训练任务，COPUS相比最快基线实现平均3.9-8.0%的收敛时间缩短，峰值提升达11.1%。

Conclusion: COPUS通过联合优化批量大小和并行策略，相比独立优化方法可获得3.9-8.0%的平均收敛加速，峰值可达11.1%，验证了系统与统计效率协同调优的有效性。

Abstract: Training large language models requires jointly configuring two interdependent aspects of the system: the global batch size, which governs statistical efficiency, and the 3D parallelism strategy, which governs hardware throughput. Existing approaches make these decisions independently: optimization work adapts the batch size to track the evolving critical batch size while keeping parallelism fixed, and systems work selects the fastest parallelism for a given fixed batch size without anticipating that the optimal batch size could change. We show that these decisions are tightly coupled: the throughput-optimal parallelism strategy may shift as the global batch size changes, so any method that fixes one while adapting the other operates with a suboptimal configuration for part of the training run.
  We present COPUS, a system that adaptively tunes the global batch size, parallelism strategy, and micro-batch size as training evolves. COPUS is guided by Goodput, the product of throughput and statistical efficiency, which models both hardware and statistical effects jointly and directly measures useful convergence per unit of wall-clock time. The system combines online gradient noise scale estimation under 3D parallelism with throughput-aware evaluation of candidate configurations, and supports efficient reconfiguration of both batch size and parallelism during training. We evaluate COPUS on LLM pre-training workloads across 1-4 nodes of 8xH100 and 8xMI210 GPUs and model sizes from 3B to 32B parameters, demonstrating average time-to-convergence speedups of 3.9-8.0% over the fastest baseline across four configurations, with peak gains up to 11.1%, including system overheads.

</details>


### [176] [A Semantic Quantum Circuit Cache for Scalable and Distributed Quantum-Classical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26788)
*Mar Tejedor,Javier Conejero,Rosa M. Badia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed
